  - **Playground:** `PLAYGROUND_MULTI_DIFF` takes an empty side for created or deleted files and shows files that aren't UTF-8 as binary; `PLAYGROUND_MULTI_DIFF_VARIANT=cards|noCards|noCardsNonCompact`.
  - **Tests:** GPUI tests for the variants, binary placeholders, smooth wheel scrolling, the anchored layout, whole-list view state, keyboard cursor moves and the reveal highlight, and unit tests for the variant configurations and the anchoring.
  - **Breaking:**
    - `MultiDiffDocument` has a new public field, `binary`; add `binary: false` to struct literals.
    - `EditorColors` has new public fields.
    - The list now keeps its viewport anchored when files change height, so `scroll_top()` can include room above the first file and the list can scroll past the last one until that room is used up.
- **Suggest widget scrolling and details (#292):** the suggest list scrolls by pixels, its scrollbars fade, and the details panel is sized to its content without lagging a frame, as with Monaco's `ListView`, `ScrollableElement` and `SuggestDetailsWidget`.
  - **Pixel scrolling:** the list scrolls by the wheel's pixels (smoothly with a trackpad) instead of by whole rows, and the rows at its top and bottom show in part. Only the rows in view are rendered. Moving the focus scrolls the focused row fully into view, as little as it takes (Monaco's `List.reveal`), and a new list shows its focused row near the top, with a third of a row above it (`showSuggestions`). Resolving an item no longer scrolls the list back to it.
  - **List height:** like Monaco's, the list's height is no longer cut to whole rows when the room around the cursor caps it.
  - **Scrollbar fade:** the list's and the details panel's scrollbars fade in over 100ms while the mouse is over them, and out over 800ms after the mouse leaves or 500ms after scrolling, from wherever they were (`.monaco-scrollable-element`'s `.visible` and `.invisible.fade`). The widget repaints every frame only while one is fading.
  - **Details height:** the details panel's type and docs are measured, at the width the panel gets, before it's placed, so the panel fits new content in the same frame instead of the next.
  - **Click-through corners:** the empty corners of the box around the list and the details panel let clicks, the wheel and hovers through to the editor, as Monaco's separate list and details widgets do.
  - **Playground:** `PLAYGROUND_KEYS` takes `wheel:x,y,dy`, which turns the wheel at a point of the window, for screenshots of scrolled widgets.
  - **Tests:** GPUI tests for pixel scrolling and revealing the focused row, the scrollbar fading in and out, the details panel's height in the frame its content changes, and clicks through the empty corners; the details tests no longer draw twice.
- **Keybinding labels follow the host's keybinding rules (#294):** labels look up keys as Monaco's `lookupKeybinding` does. A rule from `add_keybinding_rule(s)` naming a command's Monaco id now shows instead of the default binding, as Monaco's dynamic keybindings come after the defaults. Before, rules were bound as `RunCommand` and labels only found the default.
  - This applies to the command palette, the context menu, the lightbulb, the code action menu's Organize Imports and Fix All rows, the find widget's titles, and the hover's status bar.
  - **Hover status bar:** "View Problem" and "Quick Fix..." keys are looked up through the shared formatter instead of being hard-coded.
- **Drags end when the window goes inactive (#301):** a widget drag that captured the pointer (hover, find and diff sashes, the color picker, the quick input, diff overview and multi diff sliders, the diff editor's hidden-lines bar edges) now ends, and gives the pointer back, when the window stops being the active one, as Monaco's pointer capture is lost. Only the change ends it: a drag in a window that isn't active (a click-through) goes on.
  - **Tests:** the window going inactive mid-drag (diff editor sash), and the quick input slider, diff overview slider and hidden-lines bar edge drags keeping their pointer window-wide.
- **Quick input Page Up/Down (#293):** Page Down focuses the last fully visible row, and only when it's focused already scrolls a page and focuses the new last row; Page Up does the same upwards (Monaco `List.focusNextPage` / `focusPreviousPage`). Before, they moved the focus by a page of rows. Ported from VS Code's `listWidget.ts`, which isn't vendored.
- **Mouse pointer during drags:** the I-beam shows only over the text, and every other area keeps its own pointer, during drags too, as with Monaco's CSS cursors and its `GlobalPointerMoveMonitor`'s pointer capture.
  - **Text selection drags:** the gutter, minimap, scrollbars, folding controls and widgets keep their arrow or hand while drag-selecting text, instead of all turning into the I-beam; the I-beam stays over the text.
  - **Pointer capture:** a drag started off the text keeps that area's pointer over the whole window, the text and outside the editor included, until the release: the arrow for the editor's scrollbar and minimap sliders, a held scrollbar arrow and line selections started on the line numbers; the resize cursor for the hover's sashes and corner, the find widget's sash, the suggest widget's and peek view's sashes and the diff editor's sash; `grabbing` for the color picker's strips (now only on the strip being dragged) and the arrow for its saturation box; the arrow for the suggest, peek, quick input and multi diff scrollbars and the diff overview's slider; the diff editor's hidden-lines bar edges keep their `n-resize`/`s-resize`/`ns-resize` over the whole window rather than only over the diff editor.
  - **Drag and drop:** dragging the selection keeps Monaco's `mouseStyle` (the arrow, or copy) over the editor.
  - **Back to normal:** the pointer goes back to each area's own on the release, on a move without the button (a release the window didn't see), when the editor loses focus, and when a widget closes mid-drag (Escape, the hover hiding).
  - **Tests:** GPUI tests for a text selection drag over the gutter, minimap and scrollbar, scrollbar, minimap slider and line-number drags, dragging the selection, the hover's, find widget's, suggest widget's and peek view's sashes, the color picker's strips and saturation box, the diff editor's sash and the multi diff scrollbar, each with the release and cancelled drags, through new test hooks (`captured_cursor_for_tests` and `mouse_cursor_at_for_tests`) since GPUI's test platform doesn't keep the cursor.
- **Playground demo inlay hints:** the type hint after `let mut name` goes after the name, not after `mut`, and patterns like `let Some(x)` get none.
- **Decoration options and editor API (#272):** the last of Monaco's `IModelDecorationOptions` and `InjectedTextOptions`, injected text cursor stops, token change ranges and the remaining `ICodeEditor` and `monaco.editor` API.
  - **`showIfCollapsed`:** `DecorationOptions::show_if_collapsed`. As in Monaco (`getInjectedTextInInterval`), `before`/`after` text on an empty range shows only with it, and with it an empty or narrow background is widened to a typical character's width, centered, and a range ending at a line's start stops at the end of the line before. Inlay hints, inline suggestions and edits, and the diff editor's inline deleted text set it.
  - **`firstLineDecorationClassName`:** `DecorationOptions::first_line_decoration` (`LinesDecoration`: a bar or text in the line decorations area), drawn only on the first view line of a wrapped line.
  - **Injected text cursor stops:** `InjectedText::cursor_stops` (`InjectedTextCursorStops::Both`, `Right`, `Left`, `None`), with Monaco's `ModelLineProjectionData` ported. From a model position (typing, `set_selections`) the cursor shows before injected text, as Monaco's does (it used to show after `before` text). Left, Right, Down, End and clicks put it on the side of the text the stops allow (Monaco's `MoveOperations` with `PositionAffinity` and `_validateViewState`); Up puts it before. Inlay hints stop only after a hint, and ghost text only before.
  - **`attachedData` and tokens:** `InjectedText::attached_data` (`AttachedData`) comes back in the new `MouseTarget::injected_text` (`InjectedTextTarget`, with the decoration), and `InjectedText::tokens` styles parts of the text (Monaco's internal `tokens`). A click on `after` text in the middle of a line now goes to its column, not the next one.
  - **Theme colors:** overview ruler and minimap colors are `DecorationColor`s, a resolved color or a theme color id (`DecorationColor::theme`, Monaco `ThemeColor`) looked up in the editor's theme when drawn, so marks follow theme changes; both take Monaco's `darkColor`. `Theme::color(id)` resolves any of Monaco's color ids (Monaco `IColorTheme.getColor`).
  - **Decoration fonts:** `DecorationOptions::font_family`, `font_weight` and `font_style` (Monaco `fontFamily`, `fontWeight`, `fontStyle`).
  - **Token change ranges:** `TextModelEvent::SyntaxUpdated` and `CodeEditorEvent::ModelTokensChanged` carry `ranges`, the rows whose highlighting may have changed (Monaco `IModelTokensChangedEvent.ranges`): every row after a first parse, then the edited rows and what tree-sitter's changed ranges or a Monarch grammar's retokenized lines add (the new `Highlighter::changed_rows` and `SyntaxSnapshot::changed_ranges`).
  - **Model API:** `TextModel::on_will_dispose` (Monaco `onWillDispose`). `create_model(cx, value, language, uri)` (Monaco `createModel`) fails when a live model has the uri.
  - **Editor API:** `set_model(None)` (Monaco `setModel(null)`): the editor draws only its background and ignores input, `execute_edits` and `trigger` until it's given a model; `get_model` and `has_model`. As with any model change, view zones go and the code lenses reset; content, glyph margin and gutter widgets keep their positions for the next model rather than being resolved against none. `add_contribution`/`get_contribution::<T>()` (Monaco `getContribution`, by type). `get_font_size_at_position`, `write_screen_reader_content` and `set_aria_options` (`AriaOptions`: role and active descendant). `set_selections` puts the cursors' view positions back where the model positions show, as Monaco's `fromModelSelections`, even for the same selections.
  - **Commands:** `register_command` returns a `Disposable` and, as Monaco's `registerCommand`, shadows an earlier handler for the id until disposed. The LSP client disposes its own commands when a server stops, leaving another server's with the same id.
  - **Already there:** marker code links (`Marker::code_target`, Monaco `code: { value, target }`, #247).
  - **Tests:** ports of Monaco's `lineBreakData.test.ts` and `viewModelImpl.test.ts` "normalizePosition with multiple touching injected text", unit tests for the cursor stops, `showIfCollapsed` filtering and widening, injected tokens and decoration fonts, and GPUI tests for each item: empty-range injected text, moves and clicks around injected text with each kind of stop, attached data in mouse targets, first-line decorations on wrapped lines, theme-colored overview ruler and minimap marks across theme changes, token change ranges, `on_will_dispose`, contributions, the font size and aria options, an editor without a model, disposable commands and `create_model` with a taken uri.
  - **Breaking:**
    - `DecorationOptions::overview_ruler` is an `OverviewRulerDecoration` (`OverviewRulerDecoration::new(color, lane)`) instead of a `(Hsla, OverviewRulerLane)`, and `MinimapDecoration::color` an `Option<DecorationColor>` (`Some(color.into())`).
    - `DecorationOptions` has new public fields (`show_if_collapsed`, `first_line_decoration`, `font_family`, `font_weight`, `font_style`), as do `InjectedText` (`tokens`, `cursor_stops`, `attached_data`), `MinimapDecoration` (`dark_color`) and `MouseTarget` (`injected_text`); use `..Default::default()` in struct literals.
    - Injected text on an empty range needs `show_if_collapsed: true` to show.
    - The cursor shows before `before` text at its position until moved onto it.
    - `TextModelEvent::SyntaxUpdated` and `CodeEditorEvent::ModelTokensChanged` are struct variants (`SyntaxUpdated { ranges }`; match with `{ .. }`).
    - `register_command` returns a `Disposable` (`#[must_use]`; dropping it keeps the command) and no longer replaces an earlier handler for the id.
    - `CodeEditor::set_model` takes `impl Into<Option<Entity<TextModel>>>`; passing a model still works.
- **Inline completions (#249):** the inline completions provider API, options and menus follow Monaco's `languages.ts`, `InlineCompletionsModel`, `InlineEditsGutterIndicator` and `GutterIndicatorMenuContent` more closely.
  - **Gutter indicator without a glyph margin:** the inline edit's pill spans the whole gutter (Monaco's overlay over the glyph margin, line numbers and line decorations), so editors with `glyph_margin: false` show it in the line numbers' space, with click-to-accept and the menu. With a glyph margin it no longer takes a glyph lane.
  - **Gutter menu:** the menu is a popup beside the pill kept inside the window (Monaco's `showInstantHover`), so it's no longer clipped for edits near the editor's edges, and it closes when the editor scrolls.
  - **Keyboard in the menus:** the inline toolbar's "⋯" menu takes the keyboard when it opens, and the gutter menu once clicked, as Monaco's menus: Up, Down, Home and End move, Enter or Space runs, Escape closes and returns to the editor. The mouse selects the row it's over, and the gutter menu stays open while it has the keyboard.
  - **Inline edits and the suggest widget:** while the suggest widget has a selection, requests pass it as `InlineCompletionContext::selected_suggestion_info`, and their results are kept apart from the other completions, which empties those (Monaco's `suggestWidgetInlineCompletions`). So, as in Monaco, the inline edit, its gutter pill and the long-distance "Tab to jump" box no longer show behind and around the suggest list; they come back when it closes (`seedInlineCompletionsWithSuggestWidget`). A completion that extends the selected suggestion extends its preview (`_computeAugmentation`, also without `suggest.preview`), and accepting the suggestion keeps its rest as ghost text and reports a partial accept (`handleSuggestAccepted`).
  - **Provider fields:** `InlineCompletion::complete_bracket_pairs` closes the brackets the text opens and drops those it closes without opening, by the language's brackets and ignoring strings and comments (Monaco `closeBrackets`, `fixBracketsInLine`), adjusting the range's end as Monaco does. `warning` (`InlineCompletionWarning`: a plain or markdown message and an icon) gives the ghost text the warning squiggle and shows the message above the toolbar. `shown_command` runs when the item first shows.
  - **Refresh:** `refresh_inline_completions(cx, registration, change_hint)` is Monaco's `onDidChangeInlineCompletions`: the focused editor asks that provider again, passing the `InlineCompletionChangeHint`, unless another provider's suggestion shows; with `inline_suggest.trigger_command_on_provider_change` every provider is asked for inline edits instead.
  - **Groups:** `InlineCompletionsProvider::excludes_group_ids` keeps the providers of those groups from being asked (Monaco's `getAvailableProviders`).
  - **Context:** `InlineCompletionContext` gains `request_uuid`, `request_issued_date_time`, `earliest_shown_date_time` and `change_hint`, and implements `Default`.
  - **Shown and partial accepts:** `handle_item_did_show` gets the text after bracket completion, and fires once per item rather than again as typing moves its range. `handle_partial_accept` gets `PartialAcceptInfo { kind: Word | Line | Suggest, accepted_length }`; after accepting a line, the length counts from the item's start across lines, as Monaco does.
  - **Options:** `inline_suggest.trigger_command_on_provider_change` (off), `inline_suggest.experimental.suppress_inline_suggestions` (provider groups not asked) and `show_on_suggest_conflict` (`Never`, `Always` or `WhenSuggestListIsIncomplete`: show a conflicting inline completion instead of the suggest widget's preview), and `inline_completions_accessibility_verbose` (off; accepted for parity, it adds nothing, as standalone Monaco has no accessible view keybinding). `emptyResponseInformation` only controls telemetry and isn't ported.
  - **Playground:** `PLAYGROUND_NO_GLYPH_MARGIN=1` turns the glyph margin off.
  - **Tests:** GPUI tests for the indicator without a glyph margin, the gutter menu's keyboard and closing on scroll, inline edits hiding while the suggest widget has a selection, bracket completion, `shown_command`, warnings, partial accept kinds, the request context, refreshes (and `trigger_command_on_provider_change`), excluded and suppressed groups, the suggest selection and augmentation, `show_on_suggest_conflict` and the toolbar menu's keyboard; ports of Monaco's "Change hint is passed from onDidChange to provideInlineCompletions" and "Change hint is undefined when onDidChange fires without hint"; a unit test of the bracket fixing.
  - **Breaking:**
    - `InlineCompletionsProvider::handle_item_did_show` takes the updated insert text, and `handle_partial_accept` takes a `PartialAcceptInfo` instead of a length.
    - `InlineCompletion` has new public fields `complete_bracket_pairs`, `warning` and `shown_command`; `InlineCompletionContext` has `request_uuid`, `request_issued_date_time`, `earliest_shown_date_time` and `change_hint`; `InlineSuggestOptions` has `trigger_command_on_provider_change` and `experimental`; `EditorOptions` has `inline_completions_accessibility_verbose`; `InjectedText` has `underline` (use `..Default::default()` in struct literals).
    - While the suggest widget has a selection, inline completions are asked with it and the inline edit doesn't show.
- **Menus, quick input and rename (#251):** the quick input, code action menu and lightbulb, context menu, rename box, inline messages and standalone color picker follow Monaco's `QuickInputList`, `ActionList`, `LightBulbWidget`, `ContextMenuController` and menu, `RenameWidget`, `MessageController` and `colorPicker.css`.
  - **Keybinding labels:** one formatter, the new `keybinding_label` module, ports Monaco's `keybindingLabels.ts` and `USLayoutResolvedKeybinding` (UI, ARIA, Electron accelerator and user settings labels). Keys are named as in `keyCodes.ts` ("F12", "⌘F2", "PageDown", "UpArrow", "Del") and macOS modifiers come in Monaco's ⌃⇧⌥⌘ order ("⇧⌥F" instead of "⌥⇧f"). The command palette, the context menu, the code action menu, the lightbulb, the rename label and the find widget's titles all use it, and the code action menu looks up the Organize Imports and Fix All keys instead of a hard-coded "⇧⌥O".
  - **Quick input list:** a virtualized list that scrolls with the wheel (never the editor behind it) by pixels, with Monaco's 10px scrollbar while the mouse is over it (a `max(20, visible² / content)` slider that drags, and track clicks that jump), so every row is reachable. The keyboard reveals the active row. Its height follows the editor's size as it's resized (40% of it).
  - **Quick input rows:** separators are the label (`pickerGroup.foreground`) and top border (`pickerGroup.border`) of the first item of their group, not rows of their own, and there's no border above the first row. Rows have the `list.hoverBackground` hover; the match highlight is `list.highlightForeground` (`quickInputList.focusHighlightForeground` on the focused row). Keybindings are Monaco's `KeybindingLabel` keycaps: one cap per modifier and key, `keybindingLabel.*` colors with the darker bottom border, and "+" between caps outside macOS.
  - **Code action menu:** Up and Down keep the focused row in view (Monaco's list `reveal`), and the action bar ("Show Disabled", documentation) sits under the list instead of scrolling with it. The list is as wide as its widest row, measured (at least 100px and the action bar, at most 80% of the window), with 24px rows, 26px semibold 12px group headers in `descriptionForeground`, single-line titles cut with an ellipsis, 13px text and the `0 2px 8px widget.shadow` shadow; it's at most 70% of the window tall and the room on its side. Disabled rows are `disabledForeground`. Row titles name the real accept and preview keys.
  - **Lightbulb:** its title is Monaco's, with the keybinding: "Show Code Actions (⌘.)", "Show Code Actions. Preferred Quick Fix Available (⌥⌘.)" or "Run: {title}" for a lone AI fix, which a click now runs directly (Monaco `autoRun`). The mouse shows a pointer over it. `sparkle-filled` is `editorLightBulbAi.foreground` and the autofix icons `editorLightBulbAutoFix.foreground`. As in Monaco, the gutter lightbulb has no title.
  - **Code action kinds:** the context menu's Refactor... and Source Action... items and the Organize Imports, Fix All and Auto Fix commands need a provider declaring those kinds (from #261); tests cover the menu.
  - **Context menu:** the menu is as wide as its widest row (label, keybinding and submenu arrow, measured; at least Monaco's 130px, at most the window), with labels cut with an ellipsis, and scrolls when taller than the window. Submenus open 250ms after hovering their row (Monaco's show delay; the keyboard opens them at once), beside it on the side with room in the window, flipping left at the right edge and kept in the window vertically.
  - **Context menu triggers:** Shift+F10 reveals the cursor first and anchors below it once it's laid out (after any injected text before it). Right-clicking a sticky scroll line opens Monaco's sticky scroll menu ("Sticky Scroll", checked, which toggles it). Right-clicking injected text (inlay hints) no longer opens the editor's menu. A right-click no longer lets GPUI move the focus back to the editor after the menu took it, so the keyboard works in a menu opened with the mouse.
  - **Messages:** Monaco's `.anchor` arrow (an 8px `inputValidation.infoBorder` triangle) points at the position from the 8px gap, above the line, or below it with the arrow on top when there's no room above; the widget starts 6px left of the position. The box is `editorHoverWidget.background` and `.foreground` with an `inputValidation.infoBorder` border (2px in high contrast). It fades in over 150ms and out over 100ms, reveals its line (`revealLinesInCenterIfOutsideViewport`), and stays open when the editor loses focus while the mouse is over it.
  - **Rename box:** the input is `max(1.1 × name length, 20)` characters of the editor's font wide (Monaco's `input.size`) with a 200px minimum, a line tall, in a `3px`-padded, rounded wrapper with `input.border` and a `focusBorder` outline while focused. Candidates are a line plus 4px tall with `quickInputList.focus*` and `list.hoverBackground`, a line-height-wide icon column, and a list as wide as the longest candidate, at most seven rows and the room on its side; focusing past the seventh scrolls it into view. The box has Monaco's `0 0 8px 2px widget.shadow`, `widget.border`, `input.foreground` text and padding only with the preview label, goes above the line when there isn't room below for about six candidates, and is cancelled when its line scrolls out of view (`afterRender(null)`). The suggestions button has the `toolbar.hoverBackground` hover.
  - **Standalone color picker:** `editorHoverWidget.background`, `.foreground` and `.border`; the header's `color-mode` codicon and "Click to toggle color options (rgb/hsl/hex)" title (also in the hover picker); the close button's `toolbar.hoverBackground` hover with 3px padding and the insert button's `button.hoverBackground`; `grab` cursors on the hue and opacity strips, `grabbing` while dragging. Light text is picked against the hover background.
  - **Tests:** ports of `keybindingLabels.test.ts` (Windows, Linux and Mac US labels, ARIA, Electron accelerator and user settings labels, issue #91235) and `usLayoutResolvedKeybinding.test.ts` (chords, single modifiers); unit tests for quick input separators, the scrollbar slider, submenu flipping and the lightbulb's titles and colors; GPUI tests for quick input wheel scrolling, reveal and keycap labels, the code action menu's scrolling, measured size and keybindings, lightbulb titles, the context menu's width, Shift+F10 reveal, the sticky scroll menu, injected text and declared kinds, rename suggestion scrolling, width and scrolling out of view, and the message's arrow side, fade, reveal and blur.
  - **Breaking:** right-clicking injected text no longer opens the editor's context menu (an inlay hint part with a location shows its own, #253). Messages use the hover colors (`message_background` is no longer used for them) and may show below their line. The code action menu uses 13px text rather than the editor's font size. Keybinding labels in menus and titles changed format. The context menu's width is measured instead of 260px.
- **Peek view and widget API (#252):** the peek view, view zones, content widgets and overlay widgets follow Monaco's `ReferenceWidget`, `PeekViewWidget`, `ZoneWidget`, `ViewZones`, `ContentWidgets` and `OverlayWidgets`.
  - **Code lenses after `set_model`:** the old model's lenses are cleared and the new model's are fetched (Monaco `CodeLensContribution._onModelChange`). Before, a new model with the same text version as the old one kept the old lenses.
  - **Model changes:** `set_model` drops every view zone (Monaco's go with the old view) and emits `ViewZonesChanged`. Content and glyph margin widgets stay, re-anchored at the same positions in the new model (clipped to it), instead of resolving anchors of the old buffer against the new one. The find widget's top zone is re-added as in Monaco.
  - **View zones:** they scroll horizontally with the text, laid out at the scroll width, and can have a margin part in the gutter (`margin_view`, Monaco `marginDomNode`). New `ViewZone` fields: `after_column` and `after_column_affinity` (after a wrapped line's view line), `ordinal` (order after the same line, Monaco's default of the column or 10000), `show_in_hidden_areas`, `suppress_mouse_down` (a click places the cursor before or after the zone) and `min_width`. A zone is hidden when the position after it is folded away, as in Monaco. `layout_view_zone` moves a zone. Mouse targets on zones have Monaco's `position`, `position_before` and `position_after`, and the margin part is a `GutterViewZone` target.
  - **Content widgets:** `secondary_position`, `position_affinity` and `suppress_mouse_down`; widgets are capped to the text area's width (the window's when overflowing), like Monaco's `maxWidth`. `layout_content_widget` changes the position, secondary position, preference and affinity, and a `None` position hides a widget.
  - **Overlay widgets:** the right corners keep Monaco's `2 * verticalScrollbarWidth + minimapWidth` from the right edge and `BottomRight` twice the horizontal scrollbar's height from the bottom. `stack_ordinal` stacks widgets in a corner; `OverlayPosition::Manual` is Monaco's `null` preference; `min_content_width` widens the scroll range; `layout_overlay_widget` moves one.
  - **Peek results list:** virtualized, with 23px rows, Monaco's list scrollbar (slider shown on hover, draggable, track clicks) and its top shadow; the wheel scrolls the list only. With results in one file there's no file row. File rows have a count badge in `badge.*` colors and the folder as a description; matches use `peekViewResult.fileForeground`; the focused row uses the peek selection colors and `list.focusOutline` while the list has focus, `list.inactiveSelection*` otherwise; high contrast themes get bold file rows and dashed match borders.
  - **Peek keyboard:** focus moves over file rows too (Home, End, Up, Down), PageUp and PageDown move by Monaco's `focusPreviousPage`/`focusNextPage`, typing jumps to a matching row (`TypeNavigationController`), Left on a result goes to its file and Left/Right collapse and expand files, Enter and Space toggle a file, Ctrl+Enter (and Ctrl/Cmd/Alt+click on the title or double click in the preview) opens to the side, and Cmd+Down opens on macOS.
  - **Peek frame:** 1px borders in `peekView.border`, a 1px header/body separator, the zone widget's arrow gap below the frame, Monaco's title spacing (13px, 20px in, the folder at `.9em`, the meta after a `-`), the close action as a codicon button, a clickable title that opens the selected result, and `--vscode-shadow-lg`. The arrow moves to the second column from the first, the zone goes after the anchor's view line (`afterColumn`), and the height sash changes the height by whole lines from where the drag started, showing `sash.hoverBorder` when hovered.
  - **Peek preview:** Monaco's options (`scrollBeyondLastLine: false`, 14px vertical scrollbar, two overview ruler lanes, minimap off, wheel always consumed); sticky scroll and the glyph margin follow the outer editor; moving between results in one file scrolls smoothly.
  - **Keybindings:** F4 and Shift+F4 (and F12 and Shift+F12 when Go to Definition and Go to References don't apply) go to the next and previous reference only while a peek view is open or in its preview (Monaco `goToNextReference`'s `when`). After a navigation with several results in `goto` mode, F12 steps through them (`editor.gotoNextSymbolFromResult`); F4 no longer does.
  - **Theme:** `badge.background`, `badge.foreground`, `sash.hoverBorder` and `list.inactiveSelectionForeground`.
  - **Tests:** ports of `referencesModel.test.ts` (`nearestReference`) and of `ViewZones._getZoneOrdinal`; unit tests for the results rows, type-to-navigate, paging and overlay placement; GPUI tests for zones and widgets across `set_model`, horizontal scrolling and the margin part, ordinals, `afterColumn` and affinity, hidden areas, `suppressMouseDown` on zones and content widgets, secondary positions, hiding and preference changes, `maxWidth`, overlay corners, stacking, manual positions and `getMinContentWidthInPx`, and for the peek list (one file, file rows and tree keys, paging, typing, the wheel, virtualization), the frame layout, the title and close actions, the height sash and the preview's options.
  - **Breaking:**
    - `ViewZone`, `ContentWidget` and `OverlayWidget` have new fields; build them with `ViewZone::new(after_line, height, view)`, `ContentWidget::new(position, preference, view)` and `OverlayWidget::new(position, view)`.
    - `OverlayPosition` has a `Manual` variant, `EmbeddedKind` a `ViewZoneMargin` variant, and `MouseTargetDetail::ViewZone` has `position_before` and `position_after`; view zone mouse targets now have a position.
    - `TopRight` and `BottomRight` overlay widgets sit where Monaco puts them (a scrollbar's width further left, and above the horizontal scrollbar).
    - View zones (other than zone widgets like the peek view) scroll horizontally and are laid out at the scroll width; zones whose next line is folded away are hidden.
    - `set_model` removes all view zones.
    - F4 does nothing without a peek view, and F12 steps through `goto` navigation results.
- **Suggest widget and parameter hints (#248):** the suggest list, its details panel and status bar follow Monaco's `SuggestWidget`, `SuggestDetailsOverlay` and `SuggestWidgetStatus`, and parameter hints its `ParameterHintsModel` and `ParameterHintsWidget`, more closely.
  - **List scrollbar:** the list's scrollbar is Monaco's (`ScrollbarVisibility.Auto`): a 10px slider, at least 20px long, that shows while the mouse is over the list and for 500ms after scrolling, in `scrollbarSlider.background` (`.hoverBackground` under the mouse, `.activeBackground` while dragged). The slider can be dragged, and clicking the track centers the slider there. The details panel has the same scrollbar.
  - **Left and Right:** cursor keys no longer close the list. As in Monaco's `SuggestModel._onCursorChange`, any explicit keyboard move refilters at the new position, asks again left of where the list was requested, and closes once the cursor leaves the word or the line. Shift+arrows (a selection) close it.
  - **Up and Down with one suggestion:** with a single focused suggestion, Up, Down, Page Up/Down and their secondary keys (Cmd/Ctrl+Up/Down, Ctrl+N/P on macOS) move the cursor instead, which closes the list (Monaco's `suggestWidgetMultipleSuggestions || !suggestWidgetHasFocusedSuggestion` precondition). The `selectNextSuggestion` family of commands has that precondition too, and the key context gains `suggest_widget_multiple_suggestions` and `suggest_widget_has_focused_suggestion`.
  - **Sizing:** the list is sized as in Monaco's `_layout`: capped to the room above or below the cursor in the window and to the window's width, going above when it doesn't fit below and there's more room above. The side chosen when the list first shows is kept while the session lasts, so it no longer flips above and below as typing narrows and widens the list, and the wanted height comes back when more rows match (`_cappedHeight`). A list above the cursor resizes from its north edge (east and north; east and south below), and resizing one edge keeps the other dimension's stored size.
  - **Details panel:** the details are placed as by Monaco's `_placeAtAnchor`: east of the list, else west, else below it (above when the list is above the cursor), whichever fits first, aligned with the list's top or bottom and capped to the room in the window. They're as tall as their content (no longer the list's height), and resize from the edges their placement allows; the size is kept while the session lasts (Monaco's `_userSize`).
  - **Details type line:** the detail is in the editor's font. It wraps, as Monaco's `auto-wrap`, unless a line after the first is indented; then each line stays on one line, cut off with an ellipsis (Monaco's `white-space: pre`). The close button sits at Monaco's 6px from the top.
  - **Status bar:** the status bar's actions are clickable, as Monaco's action bar: Insert and Replace accept the suggestion, Show More / Show Less toggle the details, and "Select (↓)" shows while no suggestion is focused and focuses one. Status bar and messages use the UI font; the rows keep the editor's.
  - **Ellipsis:** labels, label details and the right-hand details are cut off with an ellipsis instead of mid-character.
  - **Options:** `suggest.fit_width_to_details` (Monaco `fitWidthToDetails`, off) widens the list to the widest label with its inline details until the list is resized, up to the editor's width, and lets the label keep its width. `suggest.share_suggest_selections` (Monaco `shareSuggestSelections`, off) shares the suggestions remembered for `suggest_selection` between windows; otherwise each window remembers its own (Monaco keeps them per workspace).
  - **Screen readers:** the focused suggestion is announced with Monaco's list aria label ("label detail, description, Kind", with ", docs: …" once resolved while the details show), and so are "Loading..." and "No suggestions.". Parameter hints announce the active parameter with its and the signature's docs ("{label}, {docs}, hint"), once per parameter.
  - **Parameter hints:** the active parameter of a text label is found on word boundaries (Monaco `getParameterLabelOffsets`), so parameter `a` in `add(a)` or `cat` in `concat(cat)` no longer highlights the name. The signature is ruled off from the docs (`.signature.has-docs::after`), with Monaco's padding (`4px 5px` for the signature, `0 10px 0 5px` and 8px paragraph margins for the docs); with several signatures the body has a rule on its left, the arrows sit at the bottom of the controls, and the widget is at least 3.3em tall. The maximum height follows the editor's height when the hints update.
  - **Parameter hints delay:** typing, cursor moves and edits ask the providers 120ms after the last of them (Monaco's `DEFAULT_DELAY`), with the triggers in between merged (`mergeTriggerContexts`); Cmd/Ctrl+Shift+Space asks at once.
  - **Tests:** GPUI tests for the list scrollbar (wheel, track and slider), keeping the side while typing, the north sash, the details west of the list, details resizing for the session, `fit_width_to_details`, the clickable status bar, parameter hints' maximum height, Left/Right refiltering, Up/Down with one suggestion, the status bar actions, per-window and shared memories, the announcements, the 120ms delay and merged triggers, providers without trigger characters and whole-word parameter labels; unit tests port `_layout`, `_placeAtAnchor`, `ScrollbarState`, `getAriaLabel`, `getParameterLabelOffsets` and `mergeTriggerContexts`.
  - **Breaking:**
    - `SignatureHelpProvider::trigger_characters` defaults to none instead of `(` and `,`, as Monaco's `signatureHelpTriggerCharacters` is optional: providers that relied on the default must return `vec!['(', ',']`.
    - Parameter hints open 120ms after typing a trigger character, not at once.
    - `SuggestOptions` has two new public fields, `share_suggest_selections` and `fit_width_to_details` (use `..Default::default()` in struct literals). Suggestion memories are now per window by default.
- **Code lens and inlay hints (#253):** code lenses follow Monaco's `CodeLensWidget` and `codelensController`, and inlay hints its `inlayHintsController`, `inlayHintsHover` and `inlayHintsLocations`.
  - **Lenses waiting to resolve:** a line whose lenses have no command yet keeps an empty zone (Monaco adds the content widget only once a lens has one) instead of "no commands", which now shows only when resolving gave nothing. Resolved lenses fade in over Monaco's 0.1s, and a later resolve request no longer drops one under way, which left lines empty.
  - **Font, height and colors:** the lenses are drawn from the editor's current options, theme and zoom (`codeLensFontSize`, `codeLensFontFamily`, the editor font's features, `editorCodeLens.foreground` and `editorLink.activeForeground`), and their zones take Monaco's new `codeLensHeight` when the font size, line height or zoom changes (`updateHeight`), so they no longer keep their old size after Increase Editor Font Size or a theme change.
  - **Position:** a line's lenses start at the measured x of its first non-whitespace character (Monaco places the content widget at `getLineFirstNonWhitespaceColumn`), so tab-indented code lines up, and they scroll horizontally with the text.
  - **Titles:** `$(icon)` in a title is drawn as a codicon (Monaco `renderLabelWithIcons`); `Command.tooltip` shows on hover.
  - **Inlay hint spacing and boxes:** `paddingLeft` and `paddingRight` are Monaco's `(fontSize / 3) | 0` px spacers instead of thin spaces; with `inlayHints.padding` a label gets `1px max(1, fontSize / 4)px` of padding and `fontSize / 4` px rounded corners, split over its first and last parts. A hint's background is its text's height, as Monaco's inline span (it was the line's height with 3px corners on every part). The new `InjectedText::text_box` (`InjectedTextBox`: margins, padding and corner radii) does this for any injected text.
  - **Label part context menu:** right-clicking a part with a location shows Monaco's `showGoToContextMenu` 8px below it, with the first item active: Go to Definition, Go to Declaration, Go to Type Definition, Go to Implementations and Go to References, run from the location (in its model, when one is open), then the part's command.
  - **Hovers from a part's location:** a part's hover adds the hover providers' answers at its location after the part's tooltip (`_resolveInlayHintLabelPartHover`), and says "Go to Definition (…), right click for more" for a part with both a location and a command.
  - **Steady widths while typing:** for 800ms after an edit, new hints before the cursor on its line keep the old hints' lengths, cut with `…` or padded with hair spaces as Monaco does, then are asked for again in full; hints are cached per model and version (Monaco `InlayHintsCache`, `_cacheHintsForFastRestore`) and shown at once when an editor switches back to a model or its options change.
  - **Context menu:** a menu replacing another keeps the focus (the old one's focus-out no longer hides it).
  - **Playground:** demo inlay hints (types after `let`, linked to their `struct`, and parameter names), and `PLAYGROUND_INLAY_PADDING=1`.
  - **Tests:** GPUI tests for empty zones, fading in and "no commands", lens font, height and color after option, zoom and theme changes, lens alignment with tabs and horizontal scrolling, lens tooltips, inlay hint spacers and padding, the location menu and its navigation, location hovers, fixed lengths while typing and the cache, and unit tests for the column, fixed length and `withCommands` logic.
  - **Breaking:** `InjectedText` has a new public field, `text_box` (use `..Default::default()` in struct literals).
- **Sticky scroll, gutter and line highlight (#254):** sticky scroll, the gutter, the folding controls and the current line highlight follow Monaco.
  - **Sticky scroll lines:** the sticky lines are Monaco's `findScrollWidgetState` over `getCandidateStickyLinesIntersecting`. A header shows only while its scope reaches where it would stick, so a scope that ended above no longer draws its header over its parent's (`mod tests {` / `fn transform_offsets() {` after Cmd+Down). The last line slides up under the one above as its scope scrolls out, and the widget shrinks with it. There are at most a quarter of the editor's height of them (`round(height / lineHeight * 0.25)`, then `maxLineCount`), and lines in folded regions are skipped.
  - **Sticky scroll model:** sticky scroll works with `folding: false`, computing the folding provider and indentation models itself. The indentation model uses the language's `offSide` and folding markers.
  - **Sticky scroll clicks:** a click acts on the mouse up and puts the cursor at the clicked column. A chevron click folds or unfolds without moving the cursor, keeping the header where it is. Cmd/Ctrl+click goes to the definition of the word under the mouse, and Cmd/Ctrl+hover links it. Hovering a sticky line no longer shows hovers for the text under it.
  - **Sticky scroll rendering:** sticky lines show their inline decorations, bracket pair colors and injected text (inlay hints), so they line up with the real lines. The folding controls are Monaco's 16px codicons after the line numbers. They show only with folding on and `showFoldingControls` other than `never`, and with `mouseover` only while the mouse is over the sticky gutter. The widget has `editorStickyScroll.shadow`'s `0 4px 2px -2px` box shadow, the `editorStickyScroll.border` bottom border and `editorStickyScrollGutter.background`. After a scroll, the keyboard-focused line stays focused while it shows, else the last line is.
  - **Current line highlight:** `renderLineHighlight: 'gutter'` paints only the gutter, and the gutter box stays while there's a selection; only the text box needs empty selections. Every cursor's line is highlighted, and with viewport wrapping every view line of it, the border only on the cursor's own view line. In `all` mode, the gutter box has no right border and no border while the focused editor's `editor.lineHighlightBackground` fills it. The text box runs under the vertical scrollbar. Unfocused editors use `editor.inactiveLineHighlightBackground`, and high contrast themes a 1px border.
  - **Glyph margin lanes:** decorations pick a lane with `DecorationOptions::glyph_margin` (`GlyphMarginOptions { position: GlyphMarginLane::Left | Center | Right, persist_lane }`), and glyph margin widgets with `lane` and `z_index`. The margin is a line height wide per lane in use (Monaco `GlyphMarginLanesModel`). Glyph decorations show on every line of their range, wrapped lines included. Where glyphs and widgets share a line and lane, the highest `z_index` shows, widgets first on a tie.
  - **Folding controls:** the chevrons fill the line decorations column with Monaco's 2px margin, at 140% of the font size in `editorGutter.foldingControlForeground`. With `mouseover` they fade in and out over 0.5s, and they have Monaco's "Click to expand/collapse the range." tooltips. `showFoldingControls: never` reserves no column and draws no chevrons. The folded placeholder is Monaco's plain grey "⋯" (`editor.foldPlaceholderForeground`) instead of a rounded box.
  - **Line numbers:** only the primary cursor's number is active. In relative mode the current line's number is left-aligned. With `renderFinalNewline: 'dimmed'`, the empty last line's number (line 1 of an empty model too) takes `editorLineNumber.dimmedForeground`, else the line number color at 40%, also when it's the active line. The column is `round(digits × maxDigitWidth)` with the widest digit's width.
  - **Gutter clicks:** clicks in the glyph margin and line decorations, folding controls included, and in the line numbers without `selectOnLineNumbers`, don't take focus (Monaco `_onMouseDown`).
  - **Tests:** ports of `stickyScroll.test.ts` (`getCandidateStickyLinesIntersecting`, issues #157180, #156268 and #159271) and `glyphLanesModel.test.ts`, and GPUI tests for the pushed last line, sticky scroll without folding, `offSide`, the height cap, chevron and column clicks, Cmd+click, injected text, keyboard focus, the line highlight modes, multiple cursors and wrapped lines, the inactive color, glyph lanes and multi-line glyphs, line numbers, the folding column and tooltip, and gutter focus.
  - **Breaking:** `GlyphMarginWidget` has `lane` and `z_index` fields (use `GlyphMarginWidget::new(row, view)` for the center lane), and `DecorationOptions` has `glyph_margin`. Glyph margin widgets are a lane wide (the line height) rather than the whole margin's width. Glyph decorations show on every line of their range instead of the first. A click on a sticky line moves the cursor on mouse up instead of mouse down.
- **Find widget fidelity (#250):** the find widget and the find-related commands follow Monaco's `FindWidget`, `FindController` and multicursor controller.
  - **Alt+Enter:** Select All Matches runs from the editor as well as the find inputs while the widget shows (its `findWidgetVisible` precondition), keeps the widget open and focuses the editor (Monaco `FindController.selectAllMatches`). Replace and Replace All get the same precondition.
  - **Multicursor from the find input:** Cmd/Ctrl+D, Cmd/Ctrl+K Cmd/Ctrl+D and Cmd/Ctrl+Shift+L in the find input act on the editor, searching for the widget's string (Monaco's `editorFocus` and `isFindWidgetSearch`), and keep the focus in the input. Cmd/Ctrl+Shift+L uses the find string only when it's a regex, within the find scope; otherwise it searches for the selection (or the widget's string, from the input) literally.
  - **Cmd/Ctrl+D seeds find:** a Cmd/Ctrl+D or Cmd/Ctrl+Shift+L session makes its text the search string (and the global find clipboard's), so the find widget and F3 see it. A session started from a word turns whole word and match case on and regex off until the next find change or a selection the session didn't make (Monaco's `FindOptionOverride`s); the remembered toggles keep their own values.
  - **Selection highlights:** during a Cmd/Ctrl+D session they use its search text, whole word and match case, so after Cmd/Ctrl+D on a word only whole-word occurrences light up (Monaco `SelectionHighlighter._createState`).
  - **Layout:** Monaco's `findWidget.css`: 419px wide, the replace toggle a full-height column at the left, the case, word and regex toggles inside the find input and preserve case inside the replace input, the close button at the top right, `--vscode-cornerRadius-large` corners and a `widget.shadow` shadow. Narrow editors get the reduced (no matches count), narrow (257px) and collapsed (170px, no navigation or replace buttons and no find toggles) layouts of `_tryUpdateWidgetWidth`, and the widget never goes past the editor's left edge. The sash is drawn in `editorWidget.resizeBorder`, and dragging and double-clicking it follow Monaco's `onDidChange` and `onDidReset`.
  - **View zone:** its height is Monaco's `_getHeight` from the inputs' own lines and 19px line height (33px, 62px with replace), less the editor's top padding, and follows the widget's measured height after each frame.
  - **Details:** "Find" and "Replace" placeholders (`input.placeholderForeground`), titles with keybindings on every button and toggle, the match-limit title on the count, disabled previous/next/replace/find-in-selection buttons in `disabledForeground`, `focusBorder` on the focused input and control, the regex error message under the find input (`inputValidation.*`), `input.border`, `toolbar.hoverBackground` and `inputOption.*` colors.
  - **Keyboard and accessibility:** the 200ms slide-in; Tab and Shift+Tab walk the inputs, toggles and buttons in Monaco's order with its jumps, skipping disabled ones, and Space or Enter presses the focused one; Cmd/Ctrl+Down in either input focuses the editor (the new `FindInputFocusEditor` action); Shift+Enter is Find Previous in the find input only and inserts a line break in the replace input; Cmd+Enter is Replace All in the replace input only (macOS). Screen readers hear each new count, "{line}, 2 of 5 found for 'foo', at 3:7" (Monaco `_getAriaLabel`).
  - **Tests:** GPUI tests for each item, and Monaco's multicursor tests "the default Find session survives returning focus to the editor", "Find-focused mixed-case initial selections respect Find match case false/true" and the Find part of "switching focus transfers matching rules between selection and Find sessions".
  - **Breaking:**
    - Select All Matches no longer closes the find widget. `editor.action.selectAllMatches`, `editor.action.replaceOne` and `editor.action.replaceAll` need the find widget (`trigger` does nothing without it, and their keys fall through).
    - Shift+Enter, Alt+Enter and Cmd/Ctrl+Enter are no longer bound in every single-line editor (the quick input and rename input no longer run find commands with them), and Insert Line Below/Above (Cmd/Ctrl+Enter, Cmd/Ctrl+Shift+Enter) do nothing in single-line editors.
    - Cmd/Ctrl+D and Cmd/Ctrl+Shift+L change the find search string (and, with `find.global_find_clipboard`, the find pasteboard), and a session from a word temporarily sets whole word and match case on the find options.
    - The find view zone is 33px (62px with replace) instead of the old estimate.
- **Scrollbars and overview ruler (#256):** the scrollbars, the overview ruler and the scroll shadow follow Monaco's `DecorationsOverviewRuler`, `ScrollDecorationViewPart` and scrollable element.
  - **Placement:** overview ruler marks use Monaco's formula, `lineTop * rulerHeight / scrollHeight`, where the line's top counts view zones (code lenses included) and `padding.top`, and the scroll height includes scrolling beyond the last line and is at least the viewport. They line up with the slider; in a short file the last line's mark is no longer at the bottom. The ruler sits between the scrollbar arrows (`layout_info().overview_ruler`).
  - **Marks:** a mark spans its first to last line, at least 6px tall and centered on short ranges, kept on the ruler. Marks are grouped by color, painted by z-index then color, and marks of a color in the same lane that touch are merged, so overlapping translucent marks no longer darken. Positions are in device pixels, as on Monaco's canvas.
  - **Diagnostics:** error, warning and info markers are marked in the right lane in the theme's `editorOverviewRuler.errorForeground`, `warningForeground` and `infoForeground`.
  - **Cursors:** cursor marks are 2px tall at the top of the cursor's line, kept on the ruler, in `editorCursor.foreground` at 0.7 alpha, or `editorMultiCursor.primary/secondary.foreground` with several cursors.
  - **Lanes, border and background:** lanes start 1px in, and with three lanes the side lanes are floored thirds and the center takes the rest. The border is Monaco's half-covered 1px stroke on the ruler's left and top edges. `editorOverviewRuler.background` fills the ruler, or the editor background with the minimap on the right, under the marks (it used to cover them).
  - **Scroll shadow:** once scrolled down, the top shadow is Monaco's 6px `box-shadow: scrollbar.shadow 0 6px 6px -6px inset`, across the editor except a vertical scrollbar right of the minimap, under the sticky scroll, overlay widgets and the minimap. The 1px line at 0.6 opacity and the left shadow are gone.
  - **Scrollbars:** slider sizes are Monaco's `round(max(20, floor(...)))`. The sliders fade in over 100ms (Monaco's `.visible` transition) and fade out over 800ms from wherever they are. With `scroll_by_page`, a track click pages by the viewport's height and then drags the slider while the button is held. The scrollbars paint under overlay widgets, as Monaco's do.
  - **Diff editor:** the overview ruler's change zones go through Monaco's `OverviewZoneManager` (a 4px minimum, centered, in device pixels) and overlapping ones merge (`_renderOneLane`).
  - **Diff editor ruler colors:** without `diffEditorOverview.*Foreground`, the change zones now use `diffEditor.inserted/removedTextBackground` at twice their alpha as intended; GPUI's `opacity` multiplies, so they were squaring it.
  - **Performance:** the ruler's decoration groups are cached by the decorations' version (`TextModel::decorations_version`, new) and the view lines (`ViewLines::generation`, new), and the marks by the vertical layout, so a frame without changes no longer collects every decoration.
  - **Tests:** Monaco's `overviewZoneManager.test.ts`, unit tests for the lanes, grouping, mark and cursor geometry and the fade-in, and GPUI tests for each item.
  - **Breaking:** `scrollbar.arrow_size` is no longer clamped to a quarter of the bar; with `scroll_by_page` a track click keeps dragging until the button is released; there is no longer a shadow at the text's left edge when scrolled sideways.
- **Hover widget fidelity (#247):** the hover follows Monaco's `ContentHoverWidget`, `MarkerHoverParticipant` and `ResizableContentWidget` more closely.
  - **Card shape:** the hover and parameter hints have VS Code's `cornerRadius.large` (8px) corners and `shadow-lg` (`0 0 12px` at 14% black); the suggest list has the 8px corners and its `0 2px 8px widget.shadow` (none in high contrast), and the suggest details the corners. The inline suggestion toolbar is unchanged, as in Monaco.
  - **Theme keys:** with the color registry (#263), the hover, parameter hints and the new status bar paint `editorHoverWidget.background`, `.foreground`, `.border`, `.statusBarBackground` and `.highlightForeground` with Monaco's defaults (the widget colors; the status bar lightened by 0.2 in dark themes and darkened by 0.05 in light ones), and links and code spans `textLink.foreground` and `textCodeBlock.background`.
  - **Problem hover status bar:** the marker actions are one status bar row at the bottom of the card, for the most severe marker, on `editorHoverWidget.statusBarBackground` in 12px text on 22px rows: "View Problem" for errors, warnings and infos (no longer hints), then the quick fix entry. Quick Fix is computed from the quick fix code actions for that marker's range: a blank placeholder, "Checking for quick fixes..." after 200ms, then "Quick Fix..." (which opens the code action menu with those actions) or "No quick fixes available", right away for a marker known to have none. A valid AI quick fix is offered as its own sparkle action that applies it. Read-only editors get no quick fix entry.
  - **Problem hover body:** the marker's message, source, code and related information use the editor's font; the code is a link (`(code)` underlined) when the marker has a target, in the hover and the problem zone; in related information only `file(line, col): ` is the link and the message is plain text; the copy button copies Monaco's accessible content (the message and `file(line, col): message` lines).
  - **Hovered range:** while a hover shows, the union of its parts' ranges (a provider hover's range, a decoration's, a marker's on the hovered line, the color) is highlighted with `editor.hoverHighlightBackground` (Monaco's `hoverHighlight` decoration).
  - **Screen readers:** the hover is a tooltip whose accessible label is Monaco's accessible content (the parts, then "This is a hover status bar." and its actions with their keybindings), read when the hover takes focus. Show or Focus Hover focuses a keyboard hover whenever the editor is optimized for screen readers (`accessibility_support: Auto` with a screen reader too, not only `On`).
  - **Separators and padding:** each row carries its own top border across the full card width, and parts have Monaco's `4px 8px` padding, with the copy button at `4px` from the row's corner (they were 1px rules inside the card's 6px padding).
  - **Resizing:** the hover resizes from its full east edge and the full edge away from the line (north above it, south below it), each changing one dimension, and from the corner between them (Monaco's `ResizableHTMLElement` sashes), instead of an 8px corner only.
  - **LSP:** `gpui-monaco-lsp` maps a diagnostic's `codeDescription.href` to `Marker::code_target` and back.
  - **Tests:** GPUI tests for the status bar (no fixes, quick fixes for the marker, checking and remembered results, read-only editors and hints, the most severe marker, AI fixes), the code and related information links in the hover and the problem zone, the hover highlight with a theme's color, focus announcements, edge resizing and the full-width rows, and a unit test for the card tokens.
  - **Breaking:** `Marker` has a new public field, `code_target` (use `..Default::default()` in struct literals). A hover with markers in a writable editor now always shows a quick fix entry ("No quick fixes available" when no provider has one), and View Problem no longer shows for hints.
- **Minimap fidelity (#255):** the minimap follows Monaco's `Minimap`/`InnerMinimap` (`browser/viewParts/minimap/`).
  - **Scroll and slider:** a port of `MinimapLayout.create` places the slider and picks the lines drawn, with the top padding lines, partially visible lines, view zones in the viewport and Monaco's anti-flicker start line. At the end of a long file the last line shows at the slider's top instead of the slider and lines running off the bottom. The slider shows only when needed (Monaco `sliderNeeded`).
  - **Dragging:** pressing the slider no longer jumps; it follows the pointer by `initialScrollTop + delta / computedSliderRatio` (`getDesiredScrollTopFromDelta`), keeps going outside the editor, and on Windows snaps back 140px away from the minimap as Monaco's does.
  - **Clicks:** with `minimap.size: Proportional` a click off the slider reveals the clicked line in the center through the editor's reveal (smooth, view zone and padding aware) and doesn't start a drag; with `Fill` and `Fit` it drags the slider's center to the pointer. Sampled minimaps map clicks to the sampled line (`MinimapSamplingState`).
  - **Characters:** Monaco's prebaked glyph bitmaps (`minimapPreBaked.ts`, `MinimapCharRenderer`) replace the hand-made approximation: softened 12/15 (50/60 on light themes), blended from `minimap.background` by `minimap.foregroundOpacity`, at the canvas's 0.9 opacity, after the 8px gutter, centered in taller lines, full width characters in two cells and tabs per `_renderLine`; blocks mode draws Monaco's half-color blocks. Where Monaco copies the lines it already drew when scrolling (`_renderUntouchedLines`), the canvas is kept in tiles of 128 lines: scrolling moves them, and only a tile coming into view is drawn (ahead of time, on a low-priority background thread, so it doesn't compete with the frame) and uploaded; Page Down and typing cost the same as before.
  - **Selections and decorations:** `minimap.selectionHighlight` bands and spans, then decorations with the new `DecorationOptions::minimap` (`MinimapDecoration`: a color, `MinimapPosition::Inline` or `Gutter`, and section headers), highest z-index first for the line bands as in Monaco. Find matches (`minimap.findMatchHighlight`, except when there are too many), occurrences and selection highlights (`minimap.selectionOccurrenceHighlight`) and error, warning and info markers (`minimap.{error,warning,info}Highlight`) are marked. `DecorationsChange::affects_minimap` is now set.
  - **Section headers:** decorations can add them (Monaco `sectionHeaderStyle`/`sectionHeaderText`). Headers use Monaco's geometry: the label baseline at the line's top plus the font size, a band in the minimap background at 70%, the label at the 8px gutter, fitted with a measured ellipsis, and a thin separator in the foreground color; headers starting above the first drawn line show too.
  - **Slider and shadow:** `minimapSlider.hoverBackground` over the slider and `activeBackground` while dragging; it stays shown mid-drag and fades in and out over 100ms with `show_slider: mouseover`. A 6px shadow shows left of the minimap while the text scrolls right (Monaco `minimap-shadow-visible`).
  - **Width:** the minimap width formula and `fill`/`fit` width multiplier from #262 were checked against `EditorLayoutInfoComputer`.
  - **Tests:** Monaco's `minimapCharRenderer.test.ts` is ported, with unit tests for `MinimapLayout`, sampling, x offsets and header fitting, and GPUI tests for the end of long files, slider drags, clicks in each size mode, selection, decoration, marker, find match and section header marks, slider colors and visibility, and the shadow.
  - **Breaking:** `DecorationOptions` gains `minimap` (use `..Default::default()` in literals). The minimap's pixels, slider position and click behavior change as above.
- **Commands, keybindings and context keys (#261):** the editor's commands, preconditions and context keys follow Monaco's command registrations.
  - **Providers by uri:** the `editorHas…Provider` preconditions match the model's uri as well as its language, so a provider registered for a scheme or path pattern enables go to definition, peek, references and the rest, in the palette, the context menu, `trigger` and their keys (go to lookups already did). Format Document shows when only a range formatter exists, as Monaco formats documents with one.
  - **Keybindings:** Alt+\ no longer triggers inline suggestions (Monaco binds nothing to it; it types « on macOS). Shift+Escape is Monaco's secondary Escape: it closes the suggest widget, parameter hints, the problem zone, the reference peek and the find widget, leaves snippet mode and linked editing, then removes the other cursors and the selection, leaving ghost text, the hover, messages and the selection anchor alone (the new `SecondaryEscape` action). Each action's precondition is part of its default keybindings, as Monaco folds it into `when`: in a read-only editor Cmd/Ctrl+/ and the other editing keys do nothing and reach the host, F12 needs a definition provider, Cmd/Ctrl+Shift+F2 a rename provider, Cmd/Ctrl+F2 a writable editor. On Windows, Ctrl+Left/Right do Monaco's accessibility word moves while a screen reader is in use. After a drop, Cmd/Ctrl+. picks another drop type.
  - **Preconditions:** the built-in actions' preconditions are Monaco's, as predicates over the context keys: the selection anchor actions need an anchor, folding actions `folding`, Go to Next/Previous Symbol Highlight highlights, scrolling the hover a focused hover, Insert Color a focused picker, Trigger Suggest a closed suggest widget, Start Linked Editing a rename provider, Go to Symbol symbols (a provider or the `tags.scm` outline, here and in the context menu), the peek actions and Go to References not being in a peek preview, and Organize Imports, Fix All and Auto Fix a code action provider declaring that kind (`provided_kinds`); the context menu's Refactor... and Source Action... need one too. Remove Brackets and Select to Bracket have none, as in Monaco. A failing precondition makes `trigger` do nothing.
  - **Commands by id:** `trigger` and `trigger_with_payload` run every command Monaco registers for the editor (a test checks the ids from Monaco's source), with Monaco's arguments: the handler commands `type`, `replacePreviousChar`, `compositionType`, `compositionStart`, `compositionEnd`, `paste` (with `text`, `pasteOnNewLine`, `multicursorText`, `mode`) and `cut` and their `default:` ids; `cursorMove` (every `to`, `by` and `value`), `editorScroll`, `revealLine`, `setSelection`, `createCursor`, `columnSelect`, `scrollLeft`/`scrollRight` and the mouse commands (`_moveTo`, `_wordSelect`, `lastCursorLineSelect`, …); the contributions' commands without a key of their own (`acceptSelectedSuggestion`, `hideSuggestWidget`, `insertBestCompletion`, `insertNext/PrevSuggestion`, the snippet placeholder commands, `leaveSnippet`, `acceptSnippet`, the rename input commands, `cancelLinkedEditingInput`, `leaveEditorMessage`, `closeFindWidget`, `closeMarkersNavigation`, `editor.gotoNextSymbolFromResult(.cancel)`, `editor.action.unicodeHighlight.showExcludeOptions`, `cursorWordAccessibility*`, `editor.action.findReferences`, `editor.hideDropWidget`, the legacy `editor.action.format`); and Monaco's aliases (`editor.action.goToDeclaration`, `editor.action.previewDeclaration`, `acceptSelectedSuggestionOnEnter`, `editor.action.smartSelect.grow`, `cursorWordPartStartLeft`, the `…FromEmbeddedEditor` ids). It also runs commands from `register_command`, `add_editor_action` and `add_command`.
  - **Arguments:** `editor.fold` and `editor.unfold` take `FoldingArguments` (`levels`, `direction`, `selectionLines`; `CodeEditor::fold_with_args`), `editor.action.refactor` and `sourceAction` take `kind`, `apply` and `preferred`, `editor.action.pasteAs` `kind` or `preferences`, `editor.action.showHover` `focus` (focusing a new hover with `autoFocusImmediately` or with accessibility support on), and `editor.action.inlineSuggest.trigger` `explicit`.
  - **Context keys:** the editor sets Monaco's `EditorContextKeys` (`editorTextFocus`, `editorFocus`, `editorReadonly`, `editorLangId`, `editorHasSelection`, `editorHas…Provider`, `editorSelectionHasDiagnostics`, `canUndo`, `isComposing`, …) and the contributions' keys (`suggestWidgetVisible`, `suggestWidgetHasFocusedSuggestion`, `suggestionInsertMode`, `inSnippetMode`, `hasNextTabstop`, `inSnippetChoice`, `messageVisible`, `hasWordHighlights`, `renameInputVisible`, `LinkedEditingInputVisible`, `referenceSearchVisible`, `inReferenceSearchEditor`, `selectionAnchorSet`, `foldingEnabled`, `markersNavigationVisible`, `hasSymbols`, `pasteWidgetVisible`, `dropWidgetVisible`, `cancellableOperation`, `supportedCodeAction`, `findWidgetVisible`, `findInputFocussed`, `parameterHintsVisible`, `inlineSuggestionVisible`, …) on its key context, so keybinding rules can test them; `CodeEditor::context_keys` returns them. `create_context_key` (Monaco `createContextKey`) adds the host's own, with `ContextKey::set`, `reset` and `get`.
  - **Host commands and actions:** `CodeEditor::add_command(keystrokes, handler, when, cx)` is Monaco's per-editor `addCommand`: the keybinding works in that editor while `when` holds, and it returns the command's id and a `Disposable`. `add_action`'s keybindings now apply in its editor only (they were bound app-wide again for every editor), and it returns a `Disposable`; `remove_action` and dropping the editor unbind them. `execute_command` and `execute_commands` (Monaco `executeCommand`/`executeCommands`) run an `EditorCommand` with Monaco's edit operation builder, tracked selections and inverse edit ranges.
  - **API commands:** `api_commands` has Monaco's `_executeLinkProvider`, `_executeSignatureHelpProvider`, `_executeSelectionRangeProvider`, `_executeCompletionItemProvider` and `_provideDocument(Range)SemanticTokens(Legend)` as functions returning the providers' answers.
  - **Word highlights:** they're computed only while the text has focus, start when it gains focus, and go when focus leaves the editor and its widgets for something other than another editor (Monaco `WordHighlighter`).
  - **Screen readers:** the multicursor commands announce the cursors they add, Escape announces "Removed secondary cursors", F7 / Shift+F7 announce the line and the highlight's position, a rename announces its summary, and every inline message is read, as in Monaco.
  - **Go to Offset:** a "Toggle Zero-Based Offset" button in `::` mode, remembered app-wide (`quick_access::set_goto_offset_zero_based`).
  - **Tests:** GPUI tests for each of the above, including every Monaco command id resolving through `trigger` (from `script/porting/monaco_command_ids.py`) and the per-platform bindings.
  - **Breaking:**
    - `Fold`, `Unfold`, `Refactor`, `SourceAction`, `PasteAs`, `ShowHover` and `TriggerInlineSuggest` are structs with Monaco's arguments: use `Fold::default()` where `Fold` was a unit value.
    - `CodeEditor::add_action` and `DiffEditor::add_action` return a `Disposable`, and an action's keybindings apply only in the editor it was added to.
    - The default keybindings include their actions' preconditions, so keys whose precondition fails now reach the host instead of the editor (for example editing keys in read-only editors, F12 without a definition provider, Organize Imports without a provider declaring `source.organizeImports`).
    - Alt+\ is no longer bound.
    - `trigger` of a built-in action whose precondition fails returns `Ok(())` without running it (it used to fail with "unknown action").
- **Input: gestures, IME and editing commands (#260):** mouse gestures, drag autoscroll, IME composition and three editing commands follow Monaco.
  - **Move Line Up/Down:** with `autoIndent: full` (the default) the moved block, and the line moved over it, are reindented with the language's onEnter and indentation rules (Monaco `MoveLinesCommand`). New `cursor::move_lines_up_with_language` / `move_lines_down_with_language`.
  - **Delete word left:** Ctrl/Alt+Backspace (and `deleteWordStartLeft`, `deleteWordEndLeft`, `deleteWordPartLeft`) between an auto-closed pair `(|)` deletes both characters, as `autoClosingDelete` allows (Monaco `WordOperations.deleteWordLeft`). New `cursor::delete_word_with_language` / `delete_word_part_with_language`.
  - **F7 / Shift+F7:** move the caret to the start of the next / previous word highlight instead of selecting it, reveal it in the center if it's outside the viewport, and read "line, i of n for 'word'" to screen readers. From outside every highlight, F7 goes to the first, and Shift+F7 keeps Monaco's arithmetic (`(-1 - 1 + n) % n`, the second to last).
  - **Mouse gestures:** clicks go through Monaco's `dispatchMouse` matrix: a quadruple click selects all; Shift+double-click and Shift+triple-click extend the selection by words and lines; Shift+click after a double or triple click extends by words or lines; Shift+click on the line numbers extends by lines, and drags from them select lines; the multi-cursor modifier adds a cursor or removes the one clicked (`createCursor`), and with a double or triple click selects the new cursor's word or line. Column selection follows `multiCursorModifier`: Shift plus the multi-cursor modifier (Shift+Alt, or Shift+Cmd/Ctrl with `ctrlCmd`) click or drag, an Alt drag when Cmd/Ctrl adds cursors, a middle drag, and any drag with `columnSelection`; Shift+Alt+click column-selects from the cursor. Drags follow the current modifiers. A double click on an inlay hint selects nothing.
  - **Click counting:** Monaco's `MouseDownState.trySetCount`: the platform's click count starts again at a different position or after 400ms, and never jumps more than one.
  - **Drag autoscroll:** drag scrolling uses the time since the last frame (Monaco `_tick`), not a fixed 16ms. Clicks and drags inside the text reveal the cursor minimally (Monaco `NavigationCommandRevealType.Minimal`; line selection vertically only).
  - **IME:** a composition with several cursors types at every cursor and commits at all of them (Monaco `compositionType`), replacing their selections first, with the IME's caret moving each cursor. The IME's selected range within the composition (its active clause while converting) is underlined 2px, the rest 1px (Monaco `edit-context-composition-primary` / `-secondary`), and compositions spanning lines are underlined on each. GPUI's `EntityInputHandler` doesn't pass the IME's clause boundaries or underline styles, so only the selected clause is distinguished.
  - **Tests:** the move lines tests with indentation and onEnter rules from `moveLinesCommand.test.ts`, `deleteWordLeft - issue #91855` from `wordOperations.test.ts`, and GPUI tests for each gesture, click counting, drag autoscroll, F7 navigation and IME case.
  - **Breaking:** F7 / Shift+F7 no longer select the highlight; Move Line Up/Down changes indentation with `autoIndent: full`; with `columnSelection`, a click no longer starts a column selection by itself (the drag does); a platform double or triple click no longer counts unless the clicks before it landed at the same position within 400ms.
- **Multi diff editor fidelity (#258):** `MultiDiffEditor` follows Monaco's `MultiDiffEditorWidget` more closely.
  - **Scrollbars:** the list has Monaco's `SmoothScrollableElement` scrollbars: a 10px vertical bar and, when a file is wider than the list, a horizontal one, both `auto` (shown while the mouse is over the list, while dragging and briefly after scrolling, then fading) in the editor's `scrollbarSlider.*` colors. Dragging the slider follows the mouse outside the list (window-level listeners), and pressing the track centers the slider there and drags it (Monaco's default, not paging). The files' editors no longer show their own overview ruler strip and cursor mark at the right, as Monaco's ruler goes with their hidden scrollbars (`overviewRulerLanes` 0, `overviewRulerBorder` off).
  - **Virtualization:** diff editors are created only for the expanded files in view (Monaco `VirtualizedItemManager`), not one per file up front. A file scrolled out gives its editor back to a pool that keeps up to five unused ones (Monaco `VirtualizedTemplatePool`), and keeps its view state (selections, scroll, collapsed unchanged regions) and measured height, restored when it shows again; a file whose editor has focus keeps it. Heights are measured only once a file's diff is computed and laid out, so files no longer jump to their full height while their diff is computed.
  - **Headers:** Monaco's standard header: the chevron, a file icon, the file name with its folder as a dimmed description, `A`/`D`/`R` semibold at 0.75 opacity, a struck-through original path for renames and a struck-through name for deleted files, and a toolbar of host buttons from the new `add_file_action`/`remove_file_action` (Monaco's `MultiDiffEditorFileToolbar` menu, with `MultiDiffFileAction`). A header stuck at the top gets Monaco's `scrollbar.shadow` shadow, and is pushed up by the next file's header instead of overlapping it. Borders: `multiDiffEditor.border` above each header and under each file's diff, `sideBarSectionHeader.border` under an expanded header, and `focusBorder` for the active file's (Monaco's `.active`). The chevron and file icon are text glyphs until #246.
  - **Active file and navigation:** `active_item` is the file last focused, revealed, navigated to or collapsed with its chevron (Monaco `activeDiffItem`). `go_to_next_change` and `go_to_previous_change` move through the changes of every file, expanding collapsed files and wrapping around, and `go_to_file` goes to a file's first change (Monaco `goToNextChange`, `goToPreviousChange` and `_goToFile`); they put the modified editor's cursor on the change, focus it and center it in the list. `reveal` makes the file active, and `reveal_range` centers a line of either side (Monaco `reveal` with a range). `scroll_left`/`set_scroll_left` are new.
  - **Colors:** `multiDiffEditor.background` (defaulting to the editor background) around the files, the workbench `foreground` for the header text.
  - **Tests:** GPUI tests for scrollbar wheel and drag (vertical, horizontal and track presses), the editor count while scrolling 100 files, view state kept across scrolling, the header chevron and toolbar, next/previous change across files, `go_to_file` and `reveal_range`; unit tests for Monaco's `ScrollbarState` math.
  - **Breaking:** `MultiDiffEditor::diff_editor` returns `None` for files out of view or collapsed (Monaco `tryGetCodeEditor`), and the editor it returns may show another file later. `EditorColors` gains `multi_diff_background`, `multi_diff_header_foreground` and `multi_diff_header_border`.
- **Diff editor fidelity (#257):** the diff editor follows Monaco's `DiffEditorWidget` much more closely.
  - **Sub-editors:** both editors have no minimap or folding, no sticky scroll while unchanged regions are hidden, and, inline, the original editor has no sticky scroll, unicode highlighting or glyph margin (Monaco `_adjustOptionsForSubEditor`). The diff editor re-applies these when a host changes the editors' options, and restores the host's sticky scroll and unicode highlighting when they no longer apply.
  - **Alignment:** alignment counts every view zone except the diff editor's own (code lenses, peek views, the find and problem zones, host zones, moved-block captions), as Monaco's `getAdditionalLineHeights` does. It's recomputed when view zones change, when the layout changes (wrapping after a sash drag or resize) and when the options change (line height, font zoom), and only rebuilt when it differs. While a diff is recomputed, edits move its line ranges (Monaco's `applyEditToLineRange`), so typing Enter in a change no longer misaligns the sides for 200ms.
  - **Inline view:** the original editor shows as a column of its line numbers left of the modified editor, with the deleted lines' numbers on the removed gutter color and Monaco's `gutter-delete` padding; the new `hide_original_line_numbers` option (and `compact_mode`) turns it off. Deleted lines wrap with the modified editor's line breaks, the original column pads for their wrapped rows, and they widen the modified editor's horizontal scroll range (Monaco `minWidthInPx`, through a new zone min width). They can be selected with the mouse and copied, and a light bulb in their margin opens Monaco's menu: "Copy deleted lines", "Copy deleted line (N)" and "Revert this change". The original editor scrolls with the modified one inline too.
  - **Accessible diff viewer:** clicks and the wheel no longer reach the editors under it, and long lines scroll sideways.
  - **Overview ruler:** Monaco's 30px diff overview at the right: the original's changes in its left half, the modified's in its right, in `diffEditorOverview.*Foreground` (or the inserted and removed colors at twice their alpha), with the modified editor's viewport as a slider; pressing it scrolls (grabbing the slider or jumping to it) and the wheel scrolls the modified editor. The change marks are no longer in each editor's own ruler.
  - **Sash:** a 4px hit area in `focusBorder` on hover and while dragging, a 100px minimum per side computed without the overview ruler (Monaco `SashLayout`), delta-based dragging, a double click (or `reset_sash`) back to `split_view_default_ratio`, and a second sash left of the gutter. The boundary gets Monaco's `scrollbar.shadow` and `diffEditor.border`. The gutter and moved-lines column now take their width from the original side, as in Monaco.
  - **Gutter:** a click on it focuses the modified editor.
