Its menu is Monaco's vertical toolbar: the built-in revert plus host buttons from the new `DiffEditor::add_hunk_action` (Monaco's `DiffEditorHunkToolbar`/`DiffEditorSelectionToolbar` menu items, with the mapping and Monaco's `originalWithModifiedChanges` staged text), up to 3 (1 for small items), `toolbar.hoverBackground` on hover, a 0.7s fade out, Monaco's 1px line with a 2px border, and shown while there are actions even for a read-only modified side.
  - **Changed characters:** inner changes spanning lines fill to the line's end (the new `DecorationOptions::should_fill_line_on_line_break`, Monaco `shouldFillLineOnLineBreak`). The `+`/`−` signs are at 0.7 opacity.
  - **Hidden regions:** fully shown regions get Monaco's "Fold Unchanged Region" glyph on both sides. Dragging or clicking a bar's bottom edge keeps the code below in place (Monaco `_runWithLowerScrollAnchor`), so the bar follows the mouse. The edges use `n-resize`, `s-resize` or `ns-resize` as Monaco does, also on the whole editor while dragging; the label uses the UI font and the unfold button `editorLink.activeForeground` on hover. Bars show Monaco's `diffEditorBreadcrumbs`: the symbols (from the modified model's document symbol providers, or its tags) that start in the hidden lines and end after them, outermost first; clicking one reveals it.
  - **Moved blocks:** the lines run from the original editor's scrollbar into the modified editor's gutter, ending at the code on Monaco's background rectangle. Comparing a moved block aligns its own changes (Monaco `alignmentsSyncedMovedText`) and hides the margin revert arrows. The caption sits 4px up, clear of the scrollbar, with the Compare toggle in `actionBar.toggledBackground`. The margin revert arrows keep the other changes' arrows while there's a selection.
  - **Accessibility signals:** `go_to_diff` and cursor moves onto a change send `DiffLineInserted`, `DiffLineDeleted` or the new `AccessibilitySignal::DiffLineModified`.
  - **API:** `DiffEditor::line_changes` (Monaco `getLineChanges`, with `LineChange` and `CharChange`), `identical` (Monaco `getDiffComputationResult().identical`), `set_model(None)` and `model` with the new `DiffEditorModel` (Monaco `setModel`/`getModel`), `wait_for_diff`, `add_action` and `add_command` (Monaco `IStandaloneDiffEditor`), `reset_sash`; options `is_in_embedded_editor` (sets the `isEmbeddedDiffEditor` key context), `original_aria_label` and `modified_aria_label`. The inline breakpoint is now inclusive (`width <= breakpoint`, as Monaco's `couldShowInlineViewBecauseOfSize`).
  - **Tests:** GPUI tests for each of the above, and unit tests ported from Monaco's `SashLayout`, `ScrollbarState`, `applyEditToLineRange` and the breadcrumbs source.
  - **Breaking:** `DiffEditorOptions` gains `hide_original_line_numbers`, `is_in_embedded_editor`, `original_aria_label` and `modified_aria_label`; `DecorationOptions` gains `should_fill_line_on_line_break`; `AccessibilitySignal` gains `DiffLineModified`. Struct literals need the new fields (or `..Default::default()`), and exhaustive matches the new variant. The diff editor overrides its editors' `minimap.enabled`, `folding`, and in some cases `sticky_scroll.enabled`, `glyph_margin` and `unicode_highlight`.
- **Widget infrastructure (#245):** the editor now handles the wheel, clicks, clipping, paint order and focus around its zones and widgets once, for every widget.
  - **Wheel:** the wheel over an overlay widget (find, quick input, context menu), an overflowing widget (below) or a zone widget (the peek view, the problem zone) no longer scrolls the editor, as Monaco's are outside its scrollable element; scrollable widgets scroll instead. The suggest list and the quick input list scroll with the wheel, with a scrollbar on the suggest list; parameter hints get Monaco's maximum height (a quarter of the editor, at least 250px) and scroll; the hover, suggest details, code action menu, rename suggestions and peek results scroll without the editor. Plain view zones, content widgets and the gutter still scroll the editor, as in Monaco. The diff editor's gutter and moved-lines column scroll the modified editor (Monaco `delegateScrollFromMouseWheelEvent`).
  - **Clicks:** right- and middle-clicks inside widgets no longer reach the editor: it doesn't take focus (which closed the quick input, rename box, code action menu and context menu), open its context menu, start middle-click scrolling or follow a link under the widget. GPUI's click-to-focus no longer focuses the editor for clicks on its widgets either.
  - **Overflowing widgets:** `ContentWidget` and `OverlayWidget` gain `allow_editor_overflow` (Monaco `allowEditorOverflow`). Such widgets are painted above everything in the window (GPUI deferred draws, by Monaco's z-order), can extend past the editor, take the mouse from what's under them, and a content widget fits above or below its line against the window with Monaco's 22px clearance (`_layoutBoxInPage`). The hover, suggest widget, parameter hints, code action menu, context menu, read-only and other messages, rename box, post-edit widget, standalone color picker, inline suggestion toolbar and inspect tokens widget overflow, so they show in full in a short editor (a peek preview, a diff side, a one-line editor), and the hover, suggest list, parameter hints, code action menu and context menu are capped to the room in the window and flip to the side with room. The inline suggestion toolbar's "⋯" menu is a popup over everything, so the hover no longer clips it.
  - **Clipping and paint order:** zones and widgets take clicks only where they show: parts scrolled out of the editor, or under the sticky scroll and the scrollbars, no longer take clicks over the host's UI, the sticky lines or the scrollbars. Content and glyph margin widgets are clipped to the text area and the gutter and paint under the sticky scroll, as in Monaco; content widgets are kept within the text area horizontally. The long-distance inline edit hint and the gutter indicators no longer paint over the suggest list and parameter hints or take their clicks.
  - **Focus:** focusing the hover (Cmd/Ctrl+K Cmd/Ctrl+I twice) no longer closes it, so its keyboard scrolling and Escape work. The hover, suggest, parameter hints and inline suggestions now close when the focus leaves the editor and its widgets (Monaco `onDidBlurEditorWidget`), not when it moves into one of them; suggest and parameter hints used to stay open.
  - **Widget inputs:** the find, replace, quick input and rename inputs are plain inputs, as Monaco's `<input>`s: no quick or triggered suggestions, ghost text, parameter hints, hovers, inlay hints, code lenses, lightbulb, links, occurrence and selection highlights, bracket colors, unicode highlights or drag and drop. They paint `input.background` and don't follow the font zoom; the find, replace and quick inputs use the UI font at 13px, and rename keeps the editor's font, as in Monaco.
  - **Registries:** those inputs and the editor's placeholder models (the peek view's "no preview available", the multi diff editor's missing sides) are no longer in `editors()`, `models()`, `on_did_create_editor` or `on_did_create_model` (Monaco `isSimpleWidget`); they still follow global themes. The peek view's preview editor is still listed, as Monaco's embedded editors are.
  - **Pointer:** the arrow over the gutter, scrollbars, minimap and widgets, and a hand over folding controls, instead of the I-beam everywhere.
  - **Drags:** selection drags, scrollbar thumb and minimap slider drags, text drag and drop and the diff editor's sash keep following the mouse once it leaves the editor (window-level mouse listeners). Monaco's drag-select autoscroll (`TopBottomDragScrolling`, `LeftRightDragScrolling`) is ported: outside the text, the editor scrolls every frame while the mouse is held still, faster the farther away it is (Monaco's lines per second), left and right too, and the selection follows to the edge line.
  - **Tests:** GPUI tests that draw frames cover the wheel over the hover, the suggest list, the context menu and peek results, right- and middle-clicks in the quick input and context menu, focusing and scrolling the hover, suggest and parameter hints closing on blur, overflowing and flipping widgets, plain widget inputs, the registries and drags out of the editor (selection autoscroll and the scrollbar thumb), and zones clipped to the editor.
  - **Breaking:**
    - `ContentWidget` and `OverlayWidget` gain `allow_editor_overflow` (`false` keeps the old clipping).
    - Content widgets are clipped to the text area and placed within it horizontally, and paint under the sticky scroll; glyph margin widgets are clipped to the gutter.
    - Mouse targets no longer report a zone or widget where it's clipped away (scrolled out of the editor, under the sticky scroll or a scrollbar), and widgets painted above the editor take the mouse from it, so the editor's mouse events no longer fire under overflowing widgets.
    - Clicks on content and overlay widgets and zone widgets no longer focus the editor.
- **Options: validation, defaults and layout widths (#262):**
  - **Validation:** `EditorOptions::validated` ports Monaco's `validate` methods, and the editor renders with it while `CodeEditor::options` still returns the options as set (Monaco's `getRawOptions`). `font_size` is clamped to 6–100px with 0 meaning the default; `line_height` to 0–150, where `None` or 0 is automatic and a value below 8 multiplies the font size, rounded and at least 8px (`resolved_line_height`, so `Some(px(1.5))` is no longer a 1.5px line and `Some(px(0.))` no longer a zero line height the layout divided by). `fast_scroll_sensitivity` of 0 or less is 5 and `mouse_wheel_scroll_sensitivity` of 0 is 1 (0 used to stop wheel scrolling). `minimap.scale` (1–3), `minimap.max_column`, `minimap.section_header_font_size`, `sticky_scroll.max_line_count` (1–20), `folding_maximum_regions`, `color_decorators_limit`, `line_numbers_min_chars`, `code_lens_font_size`, `padding`, the scrollbar sizes, `overview_ruler_lanes`, `reveal_horizontal_right_padding`, `multi_cursor_limit`, the hover and inline suggestion delays, `font_weight`, ruler columns and the suggest and inlay hint font sizes get Monaco's limits too; whole-number options are truncated as Monaco's `clampedInt` does.
  - **Tab and indent sizes:** `ModelOptions::validated`, which `TextModel` applies on creation and in `update_options`, keeps `tab_size` and `indent_size` at least 1 (Monaco `TextModelResolvedOptions`). A tab size of 0 used to panic with a division by zero when typing or drawing a tab. The new `indent_size_is_tab_size` is Monaco's `indentSize: 'tabSize'`: the indent size follows the tab size, and models created with `detect_indentation` start with it on, as Monaco's do (`detect_indentation`, Indent Using Spaces/Tabs set a number).
  - **Layout widths:** the gutter and minimap are laid out by a port of Monaco's `EditorLayoutInfoComputer.computeLayout`, which `layout_info()` now reports as computed rather than read back. The glyph margin is a line height wide (was 18px). The folding column is Monaco's 16px, and absent with `show_folding_controls: Never` (was up to 18px whenever `folding` was on; collapsed regions' chevrons no longer show with `Never`). The minimap takes Monaco's proportional share of the width plus its 8px gutter, at most `max_column` characters (was `max_column × scale`, up to a quarter of the editor, about twice Monaco's width in narrow editors), and `minimap.size: Fill`/`Fit` widen its scale as Monaco's do, remembering it across frames while wrapping (`ComputeOptionsMemory`). Viewport wrapping uses Monaco's column count, leaving 2px for the cursor (one more column than before in most editors).
  - **`lineDecorationsWidth` in characters:** `line_decorations_width` is a `LineDecorationsWidth`, `Pixels` (from `px(…).into()`) or `Chars(n)` for Monaco's `"Nch"` strings.
  - **Occurrence highlights:** `occurrences_highlight_delay` defaults to Monaco's 0 (was 250ms). As in Monaco's `WordHighlighter`, highlights are computed once the cursor has rested 50ms and shown no sooner than the delay after it moved, so they appear about 200ms sooner.
  - **Cursor width:** `cursor_width` defaults to Monaco's 0, which draws a 2px caret (a host passing 0 used to get an invisible one), and the caret is at most a character wide.
  - **Platform defaults:** `render_final_newline` is `Dimmed` on Linux and `default_eol` CRLF on Windows, as in Monaco.
  - **Standalone options:** `stable_peek` (Monaco `stablePeek`) keeps peek views open on Escape and on double clicks in their preview. `set_auto_detect_high_contrast` (Monaco `autoDetectHighContrast`, on) switches editors to `hc-black`/`hc-light` and back as the OS's high contrast mode changes; GPUI doesn't report that mode, so hosts report it with `set_os_high_contrast`. `accessibility_help_url` is stored for when accessibility help exists. `tab_index` (Monaco `tabIndex`) sets the editor's place in the Tab order, -1 removing it.
  - **gotoLocation:** `alternative_tests_command` joins `multiple_tests` for hosts' go to test commands, and `GotoLocationOptions::with_multiple` stands in for the deprecated `multiple`, setting every `multiple_*` as VS Code's migration does.
  - **Docs:** the book's options page documents validation, the layout, global options and how option shapes differ from Monaco's (`suggest.show*` as `hidden_kinds`, `suggestFontSize`, the scrollbar sizes, `bracketPairColorizationOptions`), and which Monaco options are absent because they would do nothing.
  - **Tests:** Monaco's `editorLayoutProvider.test.ts` is ported (all 20 tests) with the wrapping tests of `editorConfiguration.test.ts`, plus unit tests for each option family's validation, the line height rules, the cursor width, the folding column, glyph margin lanes, `"Nch"` widths and the fill memory; GPUI tests cover a tab size of 0, `indentSize: 'tabSize'`, rendering with validated options, the gutter widths, the word highlighter's timing, `stablePeek` and high contrast detection.
  - **Breaking:**
    - `EditorOptions::line_decorations_width` is a `LineDecorationsWidth` instead of `Pixels` (`px(10.).into()`).
    - `EditorOptions` gains `tab_index`, `stable_peek` and `accessibility_help_url`, `ModelOptions` gains `indent_size_is_tab_size` and `GotoLocationOptions` gains `alternative_tests_command` (use `..Default::default()` in literals).
    - Defaults changed to Monaco's: `occurrences_highlight_delay` 0, `cursor_width` 0 (still drawn 2px), and on Linux `render_final_newline: Dimmed`, on Windows `default_eol: Crlf`.
    - Layout widths changed as above, so gutter, minimap and wrapping positions move.
- **Theme colors and token rules (#263):**
  - **Color registry:** theme colors now resolve as Monaco's `StandaloneTheme.getColor` does. A theme's own colors come first, then the defaults for its kind (dark, light, hcDark, hcLight) from a port of the `registerColor` calls in Monaco's `editor/**` and VS Code's `platform/theme/common/colors/*.ts` (at the vendored commit), with their `transparent`, `lighten`, `darken`, `oneOf`, `ifDefinedThenElse` and `lessProminent` expressions. The built-in themes are `themes.ts`' few colors over those defaults. A default that derives from another color follows it when a theme or `Theme::define` sets that color: `editor.selectionBackground` moves the inactive selection, fold background and minimap selection, `foreground` the widget border and menu separators, `focusBorder` the active input option, `scrollbarSlider.*` the minimap slider, `editorInfo.foreground` the peek border and banner icon, `list.activeSelection*` the menu, quick input and banner, `badge.background` inlay hints, and so on. Deprecated ids (`editorActiveLineNumber.foreground`) are defaults too.
  - **Stale defaults:** vs `editor.selectionHighlightBackground` is `#ADD6FF4D`; `editorWidget.border` and `menu.separatorBackground` are the foreground at 20% (`#CCCCCC33` / `#61616133`); `editorInfo.foreground` is `#59A4F9` / `#0063D3`, which the peek border, banner icon and info markers use; vs `peekViewTitleDescription.foreground` is `#616161` (it was a translucent teal); vs code lens `#919191`, snippet tab stops `#0A326433`, active input option `#0090F133`, minimap slider half the scrollbar's, find range `#B4B4B44D`; hc-light `multiDiffEditor.border` `#CCCCCC`; light inline edit indicator background 50% and a darkened modified border; `editor.compositionBorder` (`ime_underline`) white or black.
  - **Markers:** squiggles, overview ruler marks and the marker navigation zone take the theme's `editorError/Warning/Info/Hint.*`, `editorOverviewRuler.{error,warning,info}Foreground` and `editorMarkerNavigation*` colors instead of fixed dark-theme ones, so light themes get Monaco's light squiggles. `DecorationOptions::severity` marks a marker's decorations for this, and infos are marked in the overview ruler too, as in Monaco. `editor*.background` fills marker ranges when a theme sets it.
  - **Widgets:** the context menu uses `menu.*` (Monaco's dropdown colors, `menu.separatorBackground` separators, a `menu.selectionBorder` outline in high contrast); hovers and parameter hints `editorHoverWidget.*` (the active parameter in `highlightForeground`, the status bar in `statusBarBackground`); the code action list `editorActionList.*`; rename candidates `list.activeSelection*`; quick access `quickInput.*`, `quickInputTitle.background`, `quickInputList.*`, `list.highlightForeground`, `pickerGroup.*` and `keybindingLabel.*` keybindings; find toggles `inputOption.hoverBackground` and `activeForeground`, the invalid regex border `inputValidation.errorBorder`; toolbar buttons (find, hover copy, marker zone) `toolbar.hoverBackground` / `hoverOutline`; the suggest widget `selectedIconForeground`, `focusOutline` and `editorSuggestWidgetStatus.foreground`.
  - **High contrast:** find matches, find scopes, word, selection, range and symbol highlights, peek matches and code action highlights are outlined with `editor.findMatchBorder`, `editor.wordHighlightBorder` and the other border keys instead of the translucent fills that approximated them; focused list rows get `list.focusOutline`.
  - **Editor surfaces:** indent guides by level (`editorIndentGuide.background1`–`6`, cycling over the visible ones), bracket pair guides (`editorBracketPairGuide.*`, else the bracket color), `editor.inactiveLineHighlightBackground` when unfocused (and a 1px current line border in high contrast), the minimap slider's hover and active colors, `editorOverviewRuler.background` and `rangeHighlightForeground`, `editor.foldPlaceholderForeground`, `editorGutter.foldingControlForeground`, `editorStickyScroll.shadow`, `.border` and `editorStickyScrollGutter.background`, the peek editor's gutter and sticky scroll, `editor.wordHighlightText*` for textual occurrences and `diffEditorOverview.*` are drawn. The current line border follows Monaco: dropped when a theme sets a visible `editor.lineHighlightBackground` without it.
  - **New keys:** `EditorColors` gains a field for each of these and for cursor (`editorCursor.background`, `editorMultiCursor.*`), `editorLineNumber.dimmedForeground`, `editorBracketMatch.foreground`, `editorLightBulbAi.foreground`, `editor.findMatch*Foreground`, `editor.hoverHighlightBackground`, minimap highlights, `widget.shadow`, `widget.border`, `editorWidget.resizeBorder`, `input.*`, `inputValidation.*`, `button.*`, `checkbox.*`, `icon.foreground`, `descriptionForeground`, `disabledForeground`, `contrastBorder`, `contrastActiveBorder` and `inlineEdit.*` (`gutterIndicator.background`, `tabWillAccept*Border`). All of them are read from VS Code theme JSON, which also now maps `editorCodeLens.foreground`, `editor.linkedEditingBackground`, `editor.rangeHighlightBackground`, `diffEditor.move*.border` and `multiDiffEditor.*`.
  - **VS Code themes:** a theme's unset colors get the registry defaults for its `type`, so `hcDark`/`hc` and `hcLight` themes start from the high contrast defaults rather than `vs-dark`'s and `vs`'s.
  - **Token rules:** rules resolve as Monaco's `TokenTheme` trie: a rule inherits the color or font style it leaves unset from its parent (a `fontStyle`-only `comment.doc` keeps `comment`'s color, vs-dark's `metatag.php` is bold in `metatag`'s color), equal selectors merge, `fontStyle: ''` clears an inherited style, and `''` rules (and `tokenColors` entries without a scope) set the default. VS Code `tokenColors` and semantic token fallbacks resolve the same way. `fontStyle` is split on spaces into whole words, and Monaco rule colors must be `RRGGBB`/`RRGGBBAA` (alpha ignored).
  - **Language-specific rules:** a tree-sitter capture's Monaco token carries its language, as Monaco's tokens do, so `keyword.json`, `delimiter.html`, `attribute.value.html`, `string.yaml` and other language rules apply: JSON `true`/`false`/`null` are `#CE9178` in vs-dark and `#0451A5` in vs, JSON string values `string.value.json`, HTML punctuation `#383838` in vs. The editor, `colorize`, `colorize_model_line` and "Inspect Tokens" use the language of the injection a span is in. `Theme::style_for_language` and `monaco_token_for_capture_in` expose it; `monaco_token_type` no longer doubles the suffix of `string.key.json`. The built-in themes now include all of Monaco's language rules (`*.pug`, `*.scss`, `metatag.*`, `attribute.value.*`, `string.html/sql/yaml`, `operator.*`, `predefined.sql`) and its `''` default rule.
  - **`Theme::define`:** with `inherit: false`, only the new colors apply over the registry defaults for the base's kind (Monaco drops the base's `themes.ts` colors), and with `inherit` a custom base keeps its colors except those the new keys change. `editor.foreground` becomes a `''` token rule, as in Monaco.
  - **Semantic tokens:** with a standalone theme (one with Monaco rules), a semantic token no rule matches gets the default token style, overriding the syntax color, as Monaco's `getTokenStyleMetadata` does.
  - **Tests:** Monaco's `tokenization.test.ts` is ported (deeper matches, matching, parsing, defaults, merging, inheritance); unit tests cover the registry's transforms and references, the defaults per built-in theme for the changed keys, markers, menus, lists and hovers, VS Code JSON mapping and high contrast types, derived defaults through `Theme::define`, `inherit: false`, custom bases, language-specific rules, rule inheritance and `''` rules, color and font style parsing, and the semantic token fallback; GPUI tests cover high contrast find outlines, marker severities and theme colors, and JSON colorizing.
  - **Breaking:**
    - `EditorColors` gains many fields (build it with `EditorColors::vs_dark()` and the like, or `Theme::define`, rather than a literal), and `indent_guide` / `indent_guide_active` are replaced by `indent_guides` / `indent_guides_active` (`[Option<Hsla>; 6]`) and `indent_guide_colors`. Its defaults changed as above.
    - `InlineEditColors` fields are resolved colors instead of `Option`s, and the unused `inline_edit_original_background`, `inline_edit_original_changed_line_background` and `inline_edit_modified_changed_line_background` methods are gone (read the fields).
    - `TokenRule`'s `style: TokenStyle` is replaced by `foreground: Option<Hsla>` and `font_style: Option<TokenFontStyle>`, where `None` inherits; `TokenRule::monaco` builds one from Monaco's strings.
    - `DecorationOptions` gains `severity` (use `..Default::default()` in literals).
    - The built-in themes' default rule gives unmatched tokens the editor foreground explicitly (`style_for` returns `Some` rather than `None`).
    - `MarkerSeverity::color` returns vs-dark's `#59A4F9` for infos and a translucent `#EEEEEE` for hints.
- **Languages API: provider ranking, registration and types (#265):**
  - **Provider ranking:** every provider lookup now ranks providers as Monaco's `LanguageFeatureRegistry.ordered` does: by selector score (an exact language, scheme or path 10, `*` 5), then the most recently registered first. They used to run in registration order, so an older `*` or `LanguageSelector::All` provider beat a newer or language-specific one for rename, formatting ("the first one registered is used"), on-type formatting, signature help, document highlights and linked editing, and hover sections came out in registration order. Links and inlay hints are asked worst first, as Monaco's `ordered(model).reverse()`, so the best-ranked links win; semantic tokens and drop and paste providers no longer reverse the list themselves.
  - **Selectors:** `LanguageSelector::score` and the free `providers::score` (Monaco `languages.score`) are public, as is `LanguageFilter::score`. `*` in `LanguageSelector::Languages` matches any language (score 5, as `All` now scores), empty filter fields count as unset, and `LanguageFilter::exclusive` (Monaco `exclusive`) makes a matching provider the only one of its kind. `hasAccessToAllModels` and `notebookType` don't apply.
  - **Completion groups:** the suggest widget asks the providers in groups of equal score (`completion_provider_groups`, Monaco `orderedGroups`) and stops at the first group with items, so a `*` provider is skipped when the language's own providers answer (Monaco `provideSuggestionItems`). Ghost-text suggestions (`quick_suggestions` `Inline`) do the same.
  - **Trigger characters and incomplete lists:** a trigger character asks only the providers that declare it, and keeps the open session's items from the others (Monaco `providerFilter` and `providerItemsToReuse`); it doesn't trigger when it's a word character starting quick suggestions (`checkTriggerCharacter`). Typing on after an incomplete list asks only the providers whose lists were incomplete and keeps the others' items, and starting a new word while the widget shows keeps every provider's items and asks only providers that had none. Kept items keep the ranges they were given (or the session's default ones).
  - **Registering a language again:** `LanguageRegistry::register` with an existing id merges into it, as Monaco's `LanguagesRegistry._mergeLanguage`: extensions, file names, patterns, aliases and MIME types are added; the name, first-line pattern, grammar and highlighter are replaced only when the new registration has them; and configuration fields it sets replace the ones it was registered with. It used to replace the language, dropping its grammar, configuration and any Monarch highlighter.
  - **Language configurations:** `set_language_configuration` layers a configuration over the language's, as Monaco's `LanguageConfigurationRegistry`: each field from the newest configuration that sets it, so `{ comments }` alone keeps the brackets and auto-closing pairs. It returns a `Disposable` that removes it, restoring what it replaced. `LanguageConfiguration::combine` is Monaco's `combineLanguageConfigurations`, and `LanguageRegistry::register_configuration`/`unregister_configuration` keep the stack. An explicit `"autoClosingPairs": []` now turns auto-closing off instead of falling back to the brackets, as in Monaco. Monaco's deprecated `__electricCharacterSupport.docComment` is parsed (`LanguageConfiguration::electric_character_support`) and auto-closed like an auto-closing pair, as Monaco's `CharacterPairSupport` does.
  - **Location links:** `Location::origin_selection_range` and `target_selection_range` (Monaco `LocationLink`) for definition, declaration, type definition, implementation and reference results: Cmd/Ctrl+hover underlines the origin range (grown to cover every result's), navigating selects and highlights the target selection range, and peek uses it as Monaco's `OneReference.range`, while the definition hover shows the whole `range`. `Location::new` and `selection_range` help build and read them. The LSP crate keeps `LocationLink`s' ranges; it used to use only the target selection range.
  - **Workspace edits:** `WorkspaceEdit` gains `file_edits` (`WorkspaceFileEdit` with `WorkspaceFileEditOptions`, Monaco `IWorkspaceFileEdit`), `snippet_edits` (`WorkspaceSnippetEdit`, Monaco's `insertAsSnippet` text edits), `versions` (Monaco `versionId`) and `metadata` (`WorkspaceEditMetadata`, Monaco `needsConfirmation` and `label`). Applying one checks the versions first ("bad state - model changed in the meantime", as standalone Monaco), sends the file operations to the host's `set_workspace_file_edit_handler` (standalone Monaco rejects them; without a handler the edit fails), then edits the text; a single snippet edit in the editor starts a snippet session with the other edits as one undo step, and the others insert their plain text. Edits that need confirmation go through the preview handler, as VS Code's bulk edit service does. `CodeEditor::apply_workspace_edit` is now asynchronous. The LSP crate converts file operations and change annotations (`lsp-types` reads annotated text edits as plain ones, so only file operations' annotations and edits it parses as annotated carry metadata).
  - **Tokens providers:** `set_tokens_provider` (Monaco `languages.setTokensProvider`) highlights a language with a line-state `syntax::TokensProvider` (`initial_state`, `tokenize(line, state)` returning `LineTokens` of `Token`s, Monaco `TokensProvider`), run by `syntax::TokensProviderHighlighter`, which re-tokenizes edits until a line ends in the state it had before. `register_tokens_provider_factory` (Monaco `registerTokensProviderFactory`) creates a `LanguageTokenizer` (a highlighter or a Monarch grammar) when the language is first needed, for Monaco's `Thenable` providers too. Both, and `set_monarch_tokens_provider`, return a `Disposable` restoring the previous highlighting. `EncodedTokensProvider` has no equivalent, like `setColorMap`.
  - **Language events:** `on_language_encountered` (Monaco `onLanguageEncountered`) runs once when a language is first needed for basic features: when a model uses it, or when a Monarch grammar's `nextEmbedded` meets it while tokenizing (`LanguageRegistry::note_language_encountered`), which is where to set an embedded language's tokenizer lazily. `on_language` returns a `Disposable` that cancels the callback (Monaco `onLanguage`).
  - **Monarch before registration:** `set_monarch_tokens_provider` no longer needs the language registered, as in Monaco: the grammar applies once it is (`LanguageRegistry::set_highlighter` keeps highlighters for unregistered ids, `pending_highlighter` reads them).
  - **Rename:** rename locations and edits are asked for one provider at a time, best first, stopping at the first answer (Monaco `RenameSkeleton`), instead of starting every provider up front.
  - **Provider fields:** `Command::tooltip` (shown on hover over code lenses) and `Command::new`; `CodeAction::is_ai` and `ranges`; `CodeActionProvider::display_name` and `extension_id`; `DocumentSymbol::container_name` (the LSP crate fills it for flat symbols); `DocumentSymbolProvider::display_name`; `CodeLens::id`; `InlayHintsProvider::display_name`; `CompletionItem::action` (run by ghost-text suggestions without a `command`). Monaco's internal `extensionId`, `CompletionList.duration` and `FoldingRangeProvider.id` aren't ported.
  - **Drop and paste:** `DocumentDropEditProvider::id`, `provided_drop_edit_kinds` and `resolve_document_drop_edit`; `DocumentPasteEditProvider::id`, `resolve_document_paste_edit` and `provide_document_paste_edits_with_context` with a `DocumentPasteContext` (Monaco's, with `only`, the kind "Paste As..." asks for); `DocumentDropOrPasteEdit::additional_edit` (Monaco `additionalEdit`, a `WorkspaceEdit` applied after the edit). The edit being applied is resolved first, as Monaco's `PostEditWidgetManager` does.
  - **Tests:** Monaco's `languageSelector.test.ts` scores are ported, and `suggestModel.test.ts`'s "#21484: Trigger character always force a new completion session", "registerCompletionItemProvider with letters as trigger characters block other completion items to show up #127815", "Snippets gone from IntelliSense #173244" (incomplete re-queries) and "Trigger (full) completions when (incomplete) completions are already active #99504"; GPUI tests cover provider ranking (score, newest, `*`, exclusive), completion groups, the best-ranked formatter, location links (selection, highlight, underline, hover), lazy rename resolution, workspace file operations, versions, snippet edits and confirmation, configuration layering and disposing, merged registration, `set_tokens_provider`, tokens provider factories, `on_language_encountered` for models and embedded languages, cancelling `on_language`, Monarch grammars set before registration, paste edit resolution and `only`, and code lens tooltips; unit tests cover the tokens provider highlighter (states across lines, incremental re-tokenizing, token order), configuration combining and parsing, registry merging and configuration stacks.
  - **Breaking:**
    - Providers are ranked by score, then newest first, instead of registration order: where several match, a different one may now win.
    - `LanguageConfiguration`'s `comments`, `brackets`, `auto_closing_pairs`, `surrounding_pairs` and `on_enter_rules` are `Option`s (wrap literals in `Some`; read them with the `comments()`, `brackets()`, `auto_closing_pairs()`, `surrounding_pairs()` and `on_enter_rules()` accessors), and it gains `electric_character_support`.
    - `set_language_configuration` returns `anyhow::Result<Disposable>` instead of `bool`, and merges instead of replacing; `LanguageRegistry::set_configuration` is replaced by `register_configuration` and `unregister_configuration`.
    - `LanguageRegistry::register` merges into an existing language instead of replacing it.
    - `on_language` returns a `Disposable`.
    - `set_monarch_tokens_provider` succeeds for languages that aren't registered yet.
    - `CodeEditor::apply_workspace_edit` returns a `Task<anyhow::Result<()>>`.
    - `Location`, `WorkspaceEdit`, `Command`, `CodeAction`, `DocumentSymbol`, `CodeLens`, `CompletionItem`, `DocumentDropOrPasteEdit` and `LanguageFilter` gain fields (use `..Default::default()` in literals; `Location` and `Command` now implement `Default`).
- **Core rendering: cursor, selection, control characters, wrapping (#259):**
  - **Block cursor:** a block cursor (and a line cursor wider than 2px) draws the character under it in `editorCursor.background`, else the caret color's opposite, as Monaco's cursor `textContent` does; it used to hide the character. On a tab it's half a character wide, and a 2px or wider bar is shifted 1px left to straddle the character boundary (`viewCursor.ts`).
  - **Cursor view positions:** cursors keep Monaco's view state (`CursorState.viewState`). End on a wrapped line leaves the caret at the end of that view line (a second End goes to the line's end), Home goes to the view line's start first, and a click or drag past a wrapped segment's end keeps the caret on that segment; the caret, current line highlight, overview ruler mark and reveal follow it. Left and Right step over wrap points as Monaco's `normalizePosition` does, `cursorLineStart`/`cursorLineEnd` (Ctrl+A/E on macOS) move on model lines, and edits, `set_selections` and folding changes take the view position from the model again.
  - **Control characters:** with `render_control_characters`, bidi formatting characters (U+202A–202E, U+2066–2069, U+200E/F, U+061C) show as `[U+202E]`, eight columns wide, so they can no longer reorder the line (Trojan Source); the BOM, U+2028, U+2029 and NEL show as U+FFFD. Control characters are drawn in Monaco's `mtkcontrol` style, white on dark red.
  - **Paint order:** selections are under indent guides, rulers, find and word highlights and other decoration backgrounds, rendered whitespace and the wrap indicator, as in Monaco's `view.ts`; an opaque selection used to hide them.
  - **Selection shape:** a selection continuing past a line end extends a full character (an empty selected line is a character wide, not half). With `rounded_selection`, inner corners get Monaco's reverse-rounded pieces (`selections.ts` corner styles, flush within a quarter character), several selections are inset 1px at their first and last lines, and high contrast themes draw square selections.
  - **Cursors:** several cursors use `editorMultiCursor.primary.*` and `secondary.*`, and `cursor_smooth_caret_animation` slides every cursor, not just the first on screen (a secondary cursor no longer inherits the primary's slide). In high contrast themes the cursors get 1px sides in their background color while there's a selection, and the IME composition is boxed rather than underlined.
  - **Whitespace:** rendered whitespace is drawn as Monaco's default SVG marks (`whitespace.ts`), a dot a seventh of a space wide and a vector arrow, instead of font glyphs. With `boundary`, only spaces count as neighbours, so a tab next to a single space no longer makes it boundary whitespace, and a single space at a wrap isn't drawn.
  - **Guides and wrapping:** indent and bracket pair guides stop at the wrapping column (Monaco `_maxIndentLeft`). Wrapping at Monaco's column and per-level guide colors came with #262 and #263.
  - **"Show more" chip:** Monaco's `.mtkoverflow` look: `button.background`, `button.hoverBackground` while hovered, a 1px `contrastBorder`, a 2px radius and a pointer.
  - **Blinking:** the smooth, phase and expand blinking styles repaint only while the caret is changing, skipping the keyframes' flat parts (about 60% fewer frames for `smooth`) instead of repainting every 16ms for 10s.
  - **Tests:** Monaco's `cursor.test.ts` wrapped-line cases (#144041 up/down, #36740 no extra step at wrap points, #110376 and #98320 multi-cursor with wrapping) are ported, with the `viewLineRenderer.test.ts` whitespace cases (`ws-middle`, `ws-all-middle`, `ws-mixed`, `ws-faux-indent`, `ws-trail-*`) as whitespace columns; unit tests cover control character substitution, selection corner styles and insets, and blink repaint timing; GPUI tests cover End/Home on wrapped lines, clicks past a wrapped segment, cursor geometry and control character widths.
  - **Breaking:**
    - `view::guides::whitespace_columns` takes a view line (its text, start column, wrapped-line indent and whether it continues) instead of a whole model line, and follows Monaco's boundary rule.
    - `view::animation::rounded_corners` is replaced by `view::selections::corner_styles`.
- **Editor API correctness and gaps (#264):**
  - **Events only when something changed:** `ScrollChanged` is reported only when the scroll position or the scrollable size changed (Monaco's `Scrollable`), and `SelectionsChanged` only when the selections did (Monaco's cursor state comparison), so Monaco's two-editor sync (`a.onDidScrollChange(e => b.setScrollTop(e.scrollTop))` and back, or the same with selections) settles instead of looping. A no-op `set_selections` no longer closes the message or re-queries signature help, code actions and highlights. Scrolls a frame makes (revealing the cursor) and scroll size changes are reported after the frame.
  - **Event payloads:** `ScrollChanged` carries a `ScrollEvent` (Monaco `IScrollEvent`: top, left, width, height and which changed). `SelectionChange` adds the primary and secondary selections, the old selections and the model versions (Monaco `ICursorSelectionChangedEvent`), and the new `CursorPositionChanged` (Monaco `onDidChangeCursorPosition`) reports the positions right before it.
  - **Editor-level model events:** `ModelContentChanged` (Monaco `onDidChangeModelContent`, with `ModelContentChange`: changes, `eol`, version, `is_undoing`/`is_redoing`/`is_flush`/`is_eol_change`), `ModelDecorationsChanged`, `ModelOptionsChanged`, `ModelTokensChanged` and `ModelLanguageConfigurationChanged` forward the shown model's events, following `set_model`.
  - **Model event payloads:** `TextModelEvent::OptionsChanged` says which options changed (`ModelOptionsChange`, Monaco `IModelOptionsChangedEvent`) and `DecorationsChanged` what the decorations affect (`DecorationsChange`: overview ruler, glyph margin, line numbers; Monaco `IModelDecorationsChangedEvent`).
  - **Read-only `execute_edits`:** returns `false` without the "Cannot edit in read-only editor" message or `ReadOnlyEditAttempt`, which are for the user's own edits (Monaco `executeEdits`).
  - **Monaco offsets:** `TextModel::get_offset_at` and `get_position_at` are Monaco's `getOffsetAt`/`getPositionAt`: UTF-16 units with line breaks counted as the model's line ending, as `modify_position` and `value_length` count. `ContentChange` gains Monaco's `range_offset_utf16` and `range_length_utf16` beside the byte `range_offset`/`range_length`. `offset_at`/`position_at` stay byte offsets into the stored `\n` text, and the docs say which is which.
  - **Line ending changes:** `set_line_ending` (Monaco `setEOL`), `push_line_ending` (`pushEOL`) and undoing or redoing them are a new version and a content change: `TextModelEvent::Edited` with an empty patch, a change replacing the whole text with itself, `is_eol_change` and the new `eol` field (on every `Edited`), as Monaco's are; `Buffer::set_eol` bumps the version. A mirror of the text or the version no longer misses them. The LSP client skips a `didChange` for them (the server's copy has `\n` line breaks either way).
  - **`setValue` and decorations:** `TextModel::set_value` removes every decoration, as Monaco's does, and detects the BOM of the new text. An editor removes its own decorations from a model it stops showing (Monaco `removeAllDecorationsWithOwnerId`, `TextModel::remove_all_decorations_with_owner`). Decoration ids are unique across models, as Monaco's are.
  - **Decorations collections:** a `DecorationsCollection` from `create_decorations_collection` acts on the editor's current model, so the usual one collection with `set_model` per tab decorates the shown model; `on_did_change` is Monaco's `onDidChange`.
  - **Text getters:** `value_in_range` takes an `EndOfLinePreference` (Monaco's default `TextDefined` gives the model's line ending), and `TextModel::value(eol, preserve_bom)`, `value_length_with`, `value_length_in_range` and `character_count_in_range` are Monaco's `getValue`, `getValueLength`, `getValueLengthInRange` and `getCharacterCountInRange`.
  - **`execute_edits`:** `execute_edit_operations` takes `SingleEditOperation`s with `force_move_markers` and an `EndCursorState` (selections, or a `CursorStateComputer` given the undo edits), as Monaco's `executeEdits`. Its edits (and `TextModel::push_edit_operations`') join the open undo step, typing's included, as Monaco's do (`text::JOIN_OPEN_UNDO_STEP`), and `CodeEditor::pop_undo_stop` is Monaco's `popUndoStop`.
  - **Hidden areas by source:** `set_hidden_areas_from(source, areas)` keeps each source's areas apart and hides their union (Monaco `setHiddenAreas(ranges, source)`); the diff editor uses its own source, so a host's areas on its editors no longer clobber the unchanged regions.
  - **Mouse targets:** `MouseTarget` gains `range`, `mouse_column` and `detail` (`MouseTargetDetail`, Monaco's `detail`): the view zone, content widget, overlay widget or glyph margin widget under the mouse, with the zone's line, the margin's layout and lane, and the distance past a line's end.
  - **Global openers:** `register_link_opener` and `register_editor_opener` (Monaco `registerLinkOpener`/`registerEditorOpener`) open followed links and other models for every editor, peek and diff editors' included; the editor emits `OpenLink`/`OpenLocation` only when no opener did. The playground opens links this way.
  - **Decoration queries:** `TextModel::decoration_range`, `decoration_options`, `line_decorations`, `lines_decorations`, `decorations_in_point_range`, `all_model_decorations`, `overview_ruler_decorations`, `injected_text_decorations`, `all_margin_decorations` and `delta_decorations_at` work in positions (`ModelDecoration`, Monaco `IModelDecoration`), and `CodeEditor::line_decorations`, `decorations_in_range`, `delta_decorations` and `remove_decorations` are the editor's (Monaco's owner and `filterValidationDecorations`).
  - **`findMatches` scopes:** `find_matches_in` merges only overlapping scopes; touching ones stay apart, so no match spans two (Monaco `Range.areIntersecting`).
  - **Conveniences:** `CodeEditor::position`/`set_position`, `selection`/`set_selection`, `value`/`set_value`, `configured_word_at_position` (the editor's `word_separators`), `font_info` (Monaco `FontInfo`), `update_options_with` (a partial update, as Monaco's `updateOptions` merges), `visible_ranges_plus_viewport_above_below` and `scrolled_visible_position_in_editor` (Monaco `getScrolledVisiblePosition`: relative to the editor, with the line's height). `with_language` and `set_language` take a MIME type too (Monaco `getLanguageIdByMimeType`). `content_height`'s difference from Monaco's `getContentHeight` is documented.
  - **Tests:** GPUI tests cover syncing two editors' scroll and selections both ways, the cursor and scroll event payloads, read-only `execute_edits`, Monaco offsets, the EOL mirror (Monaco `editableTextModel.test.ts` "issue #1580"), `setValue` and decorations, collections across `set_model`, `forceMoveMarkers`, the cursor state computer, the undo stack, hidden area sources, forwarded model events, decoration queries, touching search scopes, mouse target details, the openers and MIME types; Monaco's `textModel.test.ts` "getValueLengthInRange" (both), `model.test.ts` "getValueInRange" and the BOM snapshot test are ported, with unit tests for `set_eol`, the joined undo step, forced decoration moves and decoration ids.
  - **Breaking:**
    - `CodeEditorEvent::ScrollChanged` carries a `ScrollEvent` (match `ScrollChanged(_)`), and it and `SelectionsChanged` aren't emitted when nothing changed (an edit that leaves the cursors where they were reports no selection change, as in Monaco).
    - `SelectionChange` gains fields and is no longer `Copy`; `MouseTarget` and `EditorMouseEvent` gain fields and are no longer `Copy`.
    - `TextModelEvent::Edited` gains `eol` and `is_eol_change`; `OptionsChanged` and `DecorationsChanged` carry payloads. `ContentChange` gains `range_offset_utf16` and `range_length_utf16`, and `events::content_changes` takes the old text's line ending.
    - Line ending changes bump the version and emit `Edited`.
    - `TextModel::value_in_range` takes an `EndOfLinePreference`; pass `EndOfLinePreference::Lf` for the old `\n` text.
    - `TextModel::set_value` removes decorations; `set_model` removes the editor's decorations from the old model; `DecorationsCollection` follows the editor's model.
    - `execute_edits` no longer pushes undo stops around its edits (call `push_undo_stop`), and `push_edit_operations` joins a typing step that's still open.
    - `CodeEditor::hidden_areas` returns a `Vec` of every source's areas, merged.
- **Codicons and markdown rendering (#246):**
  - **Codicons:** `codicons::Codicon` has every id of Monaco's `codiconsLibrary.ts` and `codicons.ts` with its code point (`id`, `code_point`, `from_id`, `$(icon)` parsing), and `codicons::icon` draws one. The icon font itself is optional: the new default-off `codicons` cargo feature adds the new `gpui-monaco-codicons` crate, Microsoft's codicon font (`@vscode/codicons` 0.0.46-43, the version the vendored Monaco uses), licensed **CC-BY-4.0**, so apps that enable it must ship its attribution (the crate's `NOTICE`). With the feature, `init` (or the first icon drawn) registers the font with GPUI's text system; without it, or if the font can't be loaded, icons are drawn as Unicode stand-ins (`Codicon::fallback`). The font's cmap gains an `m` (the crate's `patch-codicon-font.py`), since GPUI's macOS text system skips fonts without one. `gpui-monaco` itself stays MIT OR Apache-2.0. The playground and web examples enable the feature.
  - **Icons:** the Unicode and emoji stand-ins are replaced by the codicons Monaco uses: suggest kinds (`CompletionItemKind::icon`, `SymbolKind::icon`, Monaco's `toIcon`) and the read-more and details close buttons; quick access symbols; the lightbulb (`light-bulb`, `lightbulb-autofix` and the sparkle variants) and code action rows (`wrench`, `surround-with`, `symbol-file`, `sparkle`, else `light-bulb`); folding chevrons (`chevron-down` / `chevron-right` at 140% of the font size); the find widget's toggles and buttons (`case-sensitive`, `whole-word`, `regex`, `preserve-case`, `arrow-up/down`, `selection`, `close`, `replace`, `replace-all`, `chevron-right/down`); the peek view's close button and file chevrons; the problem zone's severity icon and actions; parameter hints' `chevron-up/down`; hover verbosity `add`/`remove` and `copy`; the inline suggestion toolbar's `chevron-left/right`, `toolbar-more` and menu `check`; the inline edit gutter indicator (`check`, `keyboard-tab`, `arrow-up/down`) and long-distance hint; rename's `sparkle` and `stop-circle`; the context menu's submenu chevron; the unicode highlight banner's `warning` and `close`; the color picker's and accessible diff viewer's `close`; the diff editor's `add`/`remove` signs (11px at 70% opacity), `arrow-right` and `discard` revert buttons, `unfold` and `compare-changes`; the diff editor's hidden-region breadcrumbs (symbol kind codicons and `chevron-right`) and "Fold Unchanged Region" glyph (`fold`), its lightbulb, and its gutter toolbar, whose `DiffHunkAction::icon` now draws `$(name)` codicons (the default revert action is `$(discard)` / `$(arrow-right)`); the multi diff editor's header chevrons; and the post-edit widget's chevron. Decoration glyphs and line decoration text made of codicon characters draw with the codicon font.
  - **Markdown:** `markdown.rs` now renders GFM tables (content-sized columns, a bold header, row separators, column alignment), block quotes (`textBlockQuote.*`), nested lists with hanging markers (Monaco's `padding-left: 20px`, so wrapped lines and code blocks in items line up), strikethrough, images (`http(s):` and base64 `data:` URIs through GPUI's `img`, else the alt text with a `file-media` icon), `$(icon)` codicons when `support_theme_icons` (with `\$(icon)` kept as text), and with `support_html` a sanitized HTML subset (`markdown::HTML_TAGS`: `span` colors, `br`, `img`, `a`, emphasis and code tags; `script` and `style` are dropped). Without `support_html`, tags are dropped and their text kept. Fenced code blocks are Monaco's tokenized `div.code`: syntax highlighted in the editor font, with no background or box. Inline code is padded on both sides in `textCodeBlock.background`. Links use `textLink.foreground` (also in the problem zone), and `command:` links run their command when the string is trusted.
  - **`MarkdownString`:** gains Monaco's `is_trusted`, `support_theme_icons` and `support_html`, with `new`, `from_plain_text` (Monaco `appendText`'s escaping), `trusted`, `with_theme_icons` and `with_html`.
  - **Plain-text documentation:** `providers::Documentation` is Monaco's `string | IMarkdownString`: completion, signature and parameter documentation and inlay hint tooltips can be `PlainText`, shown as written (line breaks and indentation kept) instead of being parsed as markdown. The LSP adapter returns `plaintext` documentation and tooltips as plain text instead of escaping it.
  - **Line height:** hovers, parameter hints and the suggest details use the editor's line height (the suggest line height in the details) instead of GPUI's default, as Monaco sets `line-height` to it over the font size.
  - **Not done:** text in hovers, details and parameter hints still can't be selected, as GPUI has no selectable text outside inputs; hovers copy through their copy button and details through `editor.action.suggestWidgetCopy`. Inline code isn't rounded, an image inside a paragraph goes on its own line, and links don't change color on hover.
  - **Tests:** unit tests for the codicon table (every id unique, aliases and derived ids, the font's range), the Unicode fallbacks without the `codicons` feature and the font data with it, `$(icon)` parsing, the markdown parser (tables, quotes, nested lists, code blocks, images and data URIs, theme icons, the HTML subset and tokenizer, `command:` links, plain-text escaping), and GPUI tests drawing hovers with code blocks and tables that check rows and lines take the editor's line height.
  - **Breaking:**
    - `MarkdownString` is a struct with `value` and the flags instead of a tuple struct (`MarkdownString::new(text)` or `text.into()`; read `.value` instead of `.0`).
    - `CompletionItem::documentation`, `SignatureInformation::documentation`, `ParameterInformation::documentation`, `InlayHint::tooltip` and `InlayHintLabelPart::tooltip` are `Option<Documentation>`; strings convert to plain text, as in Monaco, so wrap markdown in `MarkdownString` (`Some(MarkdownString::new("**x**").into())`).
    - `markdown::render_markdown` takes a `&MarkdownString` and the app instead of a `&str` and a language registry; `MarkdownStyle` gains `line_height`, `separator`, `quote_background` and `quote_border`.
    - `EditorColors` gains `text_link_foreground`, `text_link_active_foreground`, `text_code_block_background`, `text_block_quote_background`, `text_block_quote_border` and `text_separator_foreground`.
    - `CodeEditor::show_markdown_message` takes `impl Into<MarkdownString>`.
- **Marker navigation zone (F8 / Alt+F8, "View Problem"):** it's now a port of Monaco's `MarkerNavigationWidget`.
  - **Before:** the zone guessed its height from the message length, so related information was cut off by the bottom border. Its content couldn't scroll; the wheel scrolled the editor instead. The source and code were shown in literal backticks.
  - **Now:**
    - A header with the severity icon, the file name, "N of M problems" and next/previous/close buttons.
    - Monaco's arrow under the marker and a 1px frame.
    - A body in the editor font, with the source and code dimmed after the message and related information as `file(line, col):` links. It scrolls on its own, in both directions, without scrolling the editor.
    - Monaco's height: 3 lines plus up to 17 lines of content, at most 80% of the editor.
    - Clicks in the zone no longer reach the text behind it.
- **wordSegmenterLocales (#234):**
  - **Option:** `word_segmenter_locales` (Monaco `wordSegmenterLocales`, empty by default) takes BCP 47 tags such as `ja`, `zh-CN`, `zh-Hant-TW` or `th`. Word movement (including word part and accessibility movement), word deletion, Delete Inside Word, double-click and drag selection (and `cursor::word_at`, which Cmd+D and find seeding use too) and the word the case transforms act on (`getConfiguredWordAtPosition`, now ported as Monaco's `getWordAtPosition`) then find words with ICU4X's word segmenter, so Japanese, Chinese and Thai text, written without spaces, splits into words (`これ|は|テスト|です`). As in Monaco's `WordCharacterClassifier`, the segmenter's word-like segments take precedence over `word_separators`, a line's segments are cached, and auto-closing and whole-word search don't use it. `CursorConfig::word_segmenter_locales` carries it to the cursor functions.
  - **Locales:** the first tag that parses as a BCP 47 locale is the segmenter's content locale, and invalid tags (like `""`) are ignored. Monaco hands every supported locale to `Intl.Segmenter`, which also picks one; it checks support with `Intl.Segmenter.supportedLocalesOf`, which can reject well-formed tags.
  - **Cargo feature:** the new default `word-segmenter` feature adds `icu_segmenter` 2.3 (Unicode-3.0) with its compiled data and its `auto` model (dictionaries for Chinese and Japanese, LSTM models for Thai, Lao, Khmer and Burmese), about 2.5 MB of binary. With `default-features = false` the option is accepted and has no effect. It builds for `wasm32-unknown-unknown`.
  - **Tests:** Monaco's `cursorWordLeft - Recognize words` and `cursorWordRight - Recognize words` are ported (and the "Does not recognize words" tests now set `wordSegmenterLocales: ''` as Monaco's do), with unit tests for deleting words, Delete Inside Word, accessibility movement, Chinese and Thai, the double-click range, the segmenter's words and locale validation, and the option's lack of effect without the feature; GPUI tests cover movement, deletion, selection, double clicks, case transforms and invalid locales through the editor.
  - **Breaking:** `EditorOptions` and `CursorConfig` gain `word_segmenter_locales` (use `..Default::default()` in literals).
- **Monarch tokenizer and token-level APIs (#235):**
  - **Monarch tokenizer:** `syntax::MonarchTokenizer` ports Monaco's `monarchCompile.ts` and `monarchLexer.ts`, so grammars written for `languages.setMonarchTokensProvider` work unchanged, given as the JSON form of `IMonarchLanguage` (`serde_json::Value`, regexes as strings). Everything Monarch has is supported: states and their dotted parents, `include`, `cases` (`@attr` word lists, `~regex`, `==`/`!=`, `$n`/`$Sn`/`$#` scrutinees, `@eos`, `@default`), groups, `next` (`@push`, `@pop`, `@popall`, `$n` states), `switchTo`, `goBack`, `@rematch`, `nextEmbedded`, `@brackets`, `log`, `ignoreCase`, `includeLF`, `tokenPostfix`, `defaultToken`, `start`, `brackets`, and `@attr`, `@@`, `$Sn` and `$n` substitutions. Invalid grammars are a `MonarchError`, with Monaco's messages; errors while tokenizing give the line one `""` token and keep its state, as Monaco's model tokenization does. Lines of `maxTokenizationLineLength` (20,000 UTF-16 units by default) or more aren't tokenized.
  - **Regexes:** JavaScript regex sources are translated for `fancy-regex`: `\d`, `\w` and `\s` keep JavaScript's sets (ASCII digits and word characters), `.` doesn't match line terminators, `{`, `}` and `[` are literal where JavaScript treats them so, `[]`/`[^]`, identity escapes, `\0`, `\cX`, `\uXXXX` (with surrogate pairs) and `\u{X}` work, and lookaround (variable-length lookbehind too), backreferences, named groups and `\p{...}` are supported. Differences: `\b`/`\B` use Unicode word characters, matching is by code point rather than UTF-16 code unit, `ignoreCase` uses Unicode case folding, and octal escapes aren't supported.
  - **Highlighting:** `MonarchHighlighter` implements `syntax::Highlighter` with Monaco's per-line states: each line's end state is kept, and edits re-tokenize from the first changed line until a line ends in the state it had before. `nextEmbedded` finds other Monarch languages in the registry by id, name, alias or MIME type; other languages get Monaco's `""` token for languages without a tokenizer. `Language::with_monarch_grammar` sets one up, `LanguageRegistry::set_highlighter` replaces a registered language's highlighter, and `Highlighter::as_any` (defaulting to `None`) lets hosts downcast highlighters.
  - **`set_monarch_tokens_provider`:** `gpui_monaco::set_monarch_tokens_provider(cx, language_id, &grammar)` (Monaco `setMonarchTokensProvider`) highlights a registered language with a grammar, replacing its tree-sitter or custom highlighting, and re-tokenizes its models (and those of Monarch languages that embed others). It fails, as Monaco throws, for unknown languages and invalid grammars; disposing the returned `Disposable` restores the previous highlighting.
  - **Themes:** Monarch tokens are interned with `HighlightId::intern_monaco_token` (`is_monaco_token` tells them from tree-sitter captures of the same name) and resolved as Monaco's `TokenTheme` does: the longest matching Monaco rule (`keyword.rust`, then `keyword`), then VS Code `tokenColors` matching the token as a scope, then the TextMate scopes of the capture it names.
  - **Tokens and colorize:** `colorize::tokenize` gives a Monarch language's exact tokens, as Monaco's `editor.tokenize` (empty lines may have none; embedded languages name their tokens), and `colorize`, `colorize_element`, `colorize_model_line` and `TextModel::line_tokens` use Monarch and other custom highlighters, which they used to skip for languages without a tree-sitter grammar. `TextModel::language_at` reports a Monarch grammar's embedded languages, and "Developer: Inspect Tokens" shows Monarch tokens as they are.
  - **Token-level APIs:** `remeasure_fonts` (Monaco `editor.remeasureFonts`) is a documented no-op, since GPUI lays text out with the current fonts every frame. `setColorMap` and `getEncodedLanguageId` have no equivalent: they serve encoded token metadata from `EncodedTokensProvider`s, while highlighters here give named tokens the theme resolves by name.
  - **Tests:** Monaco's `monarch.test.ts` is ported (all nine tests: `@rematch` with `nextEmbedded`, `nextEmbedded: "@pop"` in cases, empty lines, leaving a state at the end of a line, `@attr` expansion limits and `@@`, the maximum line length, `$S2` in rules and C++ raw strings), with tests of keywords and brackets, includes and substitutions, `switchTo`, `goBack` and groups, `ignoreCase` and `@eos`, embedded languages, error handling, invalid grammars, the Monarch documentation's sample grammar, the regex translation and the highlighter's incremental updates; GPUI tests cover `set_monarch_tokens_provider` (models, edits, line tokens, embedded languages, errors, disposing), `tokenize`, `colorize` and theme matching.
- **Suggest and find extras (#226):**
  - **Details focus:** `toggleSuggestionFocus` (Ctrl+Alt+Space, `ToggleSuggestionFocus`, `toggle_suggestion_focus`) moves the focus from the suggest list to its details panel, opening it if needed, and back (Monaco's `State.Details`). While the panel has it (`is_suggestion_details_focused`, key context `suggest_widget_details_focused`), Up/Down scroll it by Monaco's 8px, Page Up/Page Down by 80px and `selectFirstSuggestion`/`selectLastSuggestion` to its top and bottom; it gets a focus border, and typing or closing the widget returns the focus to the list.
  - **Copying details:** `suggestWidgetCopy` (Cmd/Ctrl+C, and Ctrl+Insert on Windows, while the details panel has the focus; `SuggestWidgetCopy`, `copy_suggestion_details`) copies the panel's detail and docs markdown. Monaco copies the text selected in the panel, which has no selection here.
  - **Inline quick suggestions:** `quick_suggestions` values are now `QuickSuggestionsValue` (`On`, `Inline`, `Off`, `OffWhenInlineCompletions`; `From<bool>`), with `QuickSuggestions::INLINE`, `all`, `is_all_on`, `is_all_off` and `value_for`. `Inline` shows the completion items as ghost text instead of the suggest widget (Monaco `SuggestInlineCompletions`): at the end of a word or after a trigger character, the matching items, the one `suggest_selection` remembers first, join the inline completions, after the registered providers; results are refiltered while the word grows. Tab accepts, Alt+]/Alt+[ cycle, and Ctrl+Space still opens the widget.
  - **Off when inline completions show:** `OffWhenInlineCompletions`, Monaco's default for code, waits for inline completions (up to 750ms) before a quick suggestion and keeps the suggest widget closed when ghost text shows; after 750ms it opens the widget and stops the inline completions, as in Monaco's `_waitForInlineCompletionsAndTrigger`. Quick suggestions also stay closed when ghost text shows that asks to suppress suggestions after the delay (`canShowQuickSuggest`).
  - **Multi-line find inputs:** Ctrl+Enter (on macOS too) inserts a line break in the find and replace inputs (`FindInputNewline`), pasting keeps line breaks (CRLF normalized), and the inputs grow with their lines up to Monaco's 118px (`flexibleHeight`), as does the space above the text (`find.add_extra_space_on_top`). In an input with several lines, Up and Down move the cursor until it's at the start or end, then walk the history (Monaco `stopPropagationForMultiLineUpwards`). Ctrl+Enter used to replace all on Linux and Windows, where Monaco binds Replace All only to Ctrl+Alt+Enter.
  - **Global find clipboard:** `find.global_find_clipboard` (Monaco `find.globalFindClipboard`, off) seeds an empty search string from the find clipboard when find opens (Cmd/Ctrl+F, Find with Arguments, Find Next/Previous, and Replace unless seeding is `Never`), writes the search string to it whenever it changes and on Find with Arguments and Find with Selection, and takes a different search string from it when the editor gets the focus while find is open, selecting it. On macOS this is the system find pasteboard (GPUI's `read_from_find_pasteboard`/`write_to_find_pasteboard`); elsewhere a string shared by the app's editors, as Monaco's browser clipboard service keeps it.
  - **Tests:** GPUI tests cover the details focus, scrolling and copying, inline quick suggestions (refiltering, accepting, per token type, cycling with registered providers), `OffWhenInlineCompletions` (ghost text, no ghost text, the 750ms timeout), multi-line find and replace inputs (Ctrl+Enter, growing, Up/Down, pasting) and the global find clipboard (seeding, writing, focus, off). Monaco's `suggestInlineCompletions.test.ts` is ported, with result reuse.
  - **Breaking:**
    - `QuickSuggestions` fields are `QuickSuggestionsValue`s instead of `bool`s (`true`/`false` convert with `.into()`), and its default is now Monaco's: `OffWhenInlineCompletions` in code, so with an inline completions provider the suggest widget waits for it and stays closed while ghost text shows (set `QuickSuggestions::ON` for the old behavior).
    - `FindEditorOptions` gains `global_find_clipboard` (use `..Default::default()` in literals).
    - In the find inputs Ctrl+Enter inserts a line break instead of replacing all on Linux and Windows (Ctrl+Alt+Enter replaces all).
- **Go to, commands and smaller items (#227):**
  - **Go to locations:** Monaco's `editor.action.goToLocations` and `editor.action.peekLocations` API commands (and the `editor.action.showReferences` alias), as `CodeEditor::go_to_locations` and `peek_locations`, the `GoToLocations` and `PeekLocations` actions for `trigger_with_payload` (Monaco's JSON shapes: `CommandPosition`, `CommandRange`, `CommandLocation`), and commands for `execute_command` with Monaco's positional arguments, registered by `init`. The cursor moves to the position, then the locations go to, peek or both as `multiple` (or `multiple_references`) says, in a peek view titled "Locations"; without locations, only a given `noResultsMessage` shows. A command runs in the focused editor showing the uri, else the first one; a uri no editor shows does nothing, as in standalone Monaco. Keybinding rules with `args` now pass them to commands that take a payload. `GotoLocationOptions::multiple_tests` (Monaco `gotoLocation.multipleTests`, `Peek`) is there for a host's go to test commands.
  - **Symbol icon colors:** `EditorColors::symbol_icons` (a `SymbolIconColors`, Monaco's 33 `symbolIcon.*Foreground` colors, with `for_completion_kind` and `for_symbol_kind`) colors the suggest widget's and quick outline's icons. Most default to the theme's `foreground`, which VS Code themes can set, so light themes' icons are now darker; `Customcolor`, `User`, `Issue` and `Tool` items take the row's color, as their codicons have none.
  - **Developer and theme actions:** "Developer: Force Retokenize" (`editor.action.forceRetokenize`, `TextModel::force_retokenize`) reparses the whole model right away and logs how long it took. "Developer: Inspect Tokens" (`editor.action.inspectTokens`) shows the token at the cursor below it: its text (tabs as `→`, spaces as `·`) and length, language, standard token type, font style, foreground and background, the Monaco-style token type (`keyword.rust`), and the tree-sitter capture and its TextMate scopes. It follows the cursor and closes on Escape or a model or language change; `inspect_token_at` and `token_inspection` read it. "Toggle High Contrast Theme" (`editor.action.toggleHighContrast`) switches every editor between `hc-black`/`hc-light` (by the theme's kind) and the theme before. `Theme::high_contrast` marks the high contrast themes, those defined from them and VS Code themes of `type` `hc`, `hcDark` or `hcLight`.
  - **Go to offset:** "Go to Offset..." (`editor.action.gotoOffset`) opens quick access with `::`.
  - **Copy-time paste data:** `DocumentPasteEditProvider::prepare_document_paste` and `copy_mime_types` (Monaco `prepareDocumentPaste` and `copyMimeTypes`) run when text is copied or cut with `paste_as.enabled`, with the copied ranges (whole lines for empty selections, as `getPlainTextToCopy` gives them). The clipboard metadata names the copy, and pasting it, in any editor, waits for the data, merges it into the paste's `DataTransfer` (higher priority providers winning) and asks the providers that handle it, in pastes and "Paste As...". A new copy cancels the previous one's. `DataTransfer::iter` lists its items.
  - **Escape cancels requests:** Monaco's `editor.cancelOperation` (`CancelOperation`): Escape cancels the newest pending go to or peek, format, rename, paste or drop request, after the widgets Escape closes and before the cursor commands; `has_cancellable_operation` says whether one is pending.
  - **Long lines helper:** pressing on text at or past column `stop_rendering_line_after` (Monaco's `longLinesHelper`) sets the option to `None`, so the whole line draws.
  - **Unicode highlighter banner:** with too many characters to highlight, a banner above the text (Monaco's `BannerController`, 26px, `banner.*` colors, now `EditorColors::banner_background`, `banner_foreground` and `banner_icon_foreground`) says "This document contains many non-basic ASCII / ambiguous / invisible unicode characters" for the most common kind, with a link that turns that kind off and a close button. Closed, it stays away until the model changes; new options hide it until the highlights are in. The `editor.action.unicodeHighlight.disableHighlightingOfAmbiguousCharacters`, `…OfInvisibleCharacters` and `…OfNonBasicAsciiCharacters` commands change the editor's options. `unicode_highlight_banner` and `close_unicode_highlight_banner` read and close it, and `CodeEditor::set_banner`/`has_banner` (Monaco `setBanner`) show a host's banner.
  - **Tests:** GPUI tests cover the go to locations actions and commands (several, `goto`, no results, other models and uris, peek, `showReferences`), force retokenize, inspect tokens, toggling high contrast, go to offset, Escape cancelling a pending request, copy-time paste data, the long lines helper, the unicode banner and its commands, and symbol icon colors; unit tests cover token text, font styles, line tokens, the banner's kind, and Monaco's `getPlainTextToCopy` cases for the copied ranges.
  - **Breaking:**
    - `EditorColors` gains `symbol_icons`, `banner_background`, `banner_foreground` and `banner_icon_foreground`.
    - `GotoLocationOptions` gains `multiple_tests` (use `..Default::default()` in literals).
    - The suggest widget's and quick outline's icons follow the theme rather than fixed colors.
- **Hover, code action and rename extras (#225):**
  - **Definition preview hover:** the Cmd/Ctrl+hover definition link's hover shows the definition's code in a code block in its model's language, or "Click to show N definitions." for several (Monaco `goToDefinitionAtPosition`'s `getPreviewValue`: ranges of 8 lines or more stop at the next line indented like the first, and common indentation is removed). New `editor.action.showDefinitionPreviewHover` ("Show Definition Preview Hover", `ShowDefinitionPreviewHover`, `show_definition_preview_hover`) shows it at the cursor until the cursor moves or a key is pressed.
  - **Previews:** `set_workspace_edit_preview_handler` (Monaco `IBulkEditService.setPreviewHandler`) and `has_workspace_edit_preview_handler`. The handler gets a `WorkspaceEdit` and its label and returns the edits to apply, or `None` to cancel. Without one, previews apply directly, as in standalone Monaco.
  - **Code action menu:** Cmd/Ctrl+Enter previews the focused action (`CodeActionMenuPreview`, Monaco `previewSelectedCodeAction`); a cancelled preview skips the action's command. Commands asking for a kind get Monaco's "Show Disabled" / "Hide Disabled" toggle when some actions are disabled, remembered across menus. `CodeActionProvider::documentation` (Monaco `CodeActionProviderMetadata.documentation`) adds documentation links, chosen as Monaco's `getDocumentationFromProvider` does. Both are in an action bar under the list, not shown for the lightbulb's menu (`code_action_menu_bar_actions`, `run_code_action_menu_bar_action`). Rows get Monaco's "Enter to Apply, Cmd/Ctrl+Enter to Preview" tooltips, or the disabled reason.
  - **Rename suggestions:** `NewSymbolNamesProvider` (`register_new_symbol_name_provider`, `NewSymbolName`, `NewSymbolNameTag`, `NewSymbolNameTriggerKind`) fills the rename box's suggestion list, automatically when the box opens for providers that support it, or from its ✨ button (`request_rename_suggestions`). Up/Down (`FocusNextRenameSuggestion`, `FocusPreviousRenameSuggestion`) put a suggestion in the box, typing unfocuses it, and clicking one renames to it, as in Monaco's `renameWidget.ts`. `rename_suggestions` lists them.
  - **Rename preview:** Cmd/Ctrl+Enter in the rename box (`AcceptRenameInputWithPreview`, Monaco `acceptRenameInputWithPreview`) previews the edits with the label `Renaming 'foo' to 'bar'`. As in Monaco, the box shows "Enter to Rename, Cmd/Ctrl+Enter to Preview" only when previews are possible, so the "Enter to Rename" label is gone without a preview handler. The box's keys are now the `AcceptRenameInput`, `CancelRenameInput` and suggestion actions, bound in `RenameInput > CodeEditor`.
  - **Tests:** GPUI tests cover the definition hover (here, in another model, several results, from the keyboard), the code action preview, "Show Disabled" and documentation, and rename suggestions and previews; unit tests cover the preview range, code fences, documentation choice and which actions can be previewed.
- **Remaining editor and diff options (#228):**
  - **Word wrap indicator:** `word_wrap_indicator` (Monaco `wordWrapIndicator`, off) draws `↩` at the wrapping column of each soft-wrapped line, in the new `EditorColors::word_wrap_indicator` (`editorWordWrapIndicator.foreground`, defaulting to the whitespace color). Wrapping at the viewport then stops a column earlier, as in Monaco's layout. Monaco's `wordWrapIndicator.test.ts` and layout test are ported.
  - **Occurrences from all providers:** `occurrences_highlight_from_all_providers` (Monaco `occurrencesHighlightFromAllProviders`, off) asks every document highlight provider at once and merges their answers in provider order, the first highlight of a range winning; the textual occurrences are then only used when no provider is registered. Changing it computes the highlights again. Without it, providers are now asked one at a time and the first answer wins even when empty, as in Monaco (an empty answer used to fall through to the next provider and the textual occurrences; `None` still does). Monaco's `wordHighlighter.test.ts` provider cases are ported.
  - **Horizontal scrollbar and content height:** without `scroll_beyond_last_line`, a shown horizontal scrollbar now adds its height to the content height and scroll range, so the last line can scroll above it (Monaco `_getContentHeight`); `content_height()` includes it. `scrollbar.ignore_horizontal_scrollbar_in_content_height` (off) leaves it out.
  - **Accessibility support:** `accessibility_support` (Monaco `accessibilitySupport`: `Auto`, `On`, `Off`). Auto follows whether assistive technology is connected; `is_screen_reader_optimized()` says how the last frame ran. Optimized, wrapping is measured and continuation lines aren't indented, a model dominated by lines of 10 000+ characters (the new `TextModel::is_dominated_by_long_lines`) wraps at the viewport unless `word_wrap_override1` is set, and a narrow diff editor stays side by side. Off gives screen readers no text, only Monaco's "The editor is not accessible at this time." label.
  - **Announcements:** Monaco's `alert` is an assertive AccessKit live region under the editor, alternating like Monaco's two alert containers; `CodeEditor::announce` sends one. With `screen_reader_announce_inline_suggestion` (on), screen readers hear new ghost text (`renderForScreenReader`) or where a next edit suggestion is once the suggest widget is closed. Toggling tab focus mode now announces the new mode, as in Monaco.
  - **Inertial scrolling:** `inertial_scroll` (Monaco `inertialScroll`, off). The vendored source lacks `base/browser`, so it's ported from Monaco's `scrollableElement.ts`: each wheel event sets a speed (`|units|^1.02`), the view scrolls by it every 1/60s and it decays by a tenth until under 0.01.
  - **Two-cell full-width characters:** `fullwidth_character_width: TwoCells` (Monaco `fullwidthCharacterWidth`, with a monospace font as `effectiveFullwidthCharacterWidth` requires) centers full-width characters in two cells by moving the shaped glyphs; carets, clicks, measured wrapping and everything after them follow the cells. Ghost text and the inline diff's deleted lines keep the font's width.
  - **Diff editor:** `DiffEditorOptions` gains `diff_code_lens` (off; both editors' `code_lens`, so code lenses no longer show in diff editors by default), `accessibility_verbose` (off; a navigation tip in both editors' `aria_label`, naming the accessible diff viewer since there's no accessibility help), `show_empty_decorations` (on; a 3px bar where text was inserted or deleted opposite an empty range, which used to be skipped) and `use_true_inline_view` (off; in the inline view, changes within single lines show the deleted text struck through before the inserted text instead of deleted lines above, and count as simple for `compact_mode`).
  - **Decorations:** `DecorationOptions::left_border` (a `LeftBorder`) draws a bar at a range's start, for empty ranges too (Monaco's CSS `border-left`).
  - **Tests:** GPUI tests cover the word wrap indicator and its wrapping column, the content height with the horizontal scrollbar, inertial scrolling, two-cell characters (positions, clicks and wrapping), accessibility support and wrapping, announcements, merged occurrences and the four diff options; unit tests cover the inertial speed and the highlight merging.
  - **Breaking:**
    - `EditorOptions` gains `word_wrap_indicator`, `occurrences_highlight_from_all_providers`, `accessibility_support`, `screen_reader_announce_inline_suggestion`, `inertial_scroll` and `fullwidth_character_width`; `ScrollbarOptions` gains `ignore_horizontal_scrollbar_in_content_height`; `DiffEditorOptions` gains `diff_code_lens`, `accessibility_verbose`, `show_empty_decorations` and `use_true_inline_view` (use `..Default::default()` in literals).
    - `DecorationOptions` gains `left_border`, and `EditorColors` gains `word_wrap_indicator`.
    - A `DocumentHighlightProvider` answering `Some(vec![])` now stops the search (return `None` to defer to the next provider).
- **Inline edits: remaining features (#230):**
  - **Long-distance hint:** while an inline edit is scrolled away, Monaco's hint box sits in the free space beside the lines near the cursor (Monaco's `WidgetPlacementContext`, towers and flex box layout, else over the text at the right). It previews the edit's line with the edit applied (or the original line, for a deletion or a jump), with line numbers and the enclosing symbol, and "Tab to jump ↑/↓"; clicking it jumps. Options `inline_suggest.edits.show_long_distance_hint` (on) and `long_distance_hint_context_line_count` (0, up to 10 lines around the target).
