  - **Item kinds:** `InlineCompletion` gains Monaco's `jump_to_position` (a "Tab to jump" label at the target; Tab jumps there, which accepts it), `hint` (`InlineCompletionHint` with `range`, `style` (`Code` or `Label`) and `content`, shown instead of the diff in Monaco's custom view, at the end of its line or above or below it; it follows edits, and accepting leaves the cursor alone) and `uri`. Each makes the item an inline edit.
  - **Cross-file edits:** an item whose `uri` names another model is diffed against it (`model_for_uri`, so the model resolver can supply it) and shows the long-distance hint with the file name and "Tab to open →". Tab or accepting emits `CodeEditorEvent::OpenLocation`, as `open_location` does for definitions, and moves the edit to an editor showing that model (Monaco `transplantCompletion`): at once if one exists, else when the host's new editor first shows it.
  - **Alternative actions:** `InlineCompletion::alternative_action` (`InlineCompletionAlternativeAction`: label, command, optional count) is Monaco's `InlineSuggestAlternativeAction`, which VS Code only fills in for renames. Such an edit shows as a word replacement with the label beside it; Shift+Tab (`editor.action.inlineSuggest.commitAlternativeAction`, the new `AcceptInlineSuggestionAlternativeAction` action), clicking the label or the gutter menu's new entry runs the command instead of the edit and asks again. The key context gets `inline_suggestion_alternative_action_visible`.
  - **`show_inline_edit_menu`:** ghost text from such an item gets the gutter indicator and its menu (Monaco `showInlineEditMenu`); such items count as inline edits for `include_inline_edits` and sort last.
  - **Undo and redo:** as in Monaco, undoing an inline edit just accepted doesn't ask again (`_didUndoInlineEdits`); showing an inline edit ends the undo step, and undoing back to it asks for inline edits only (`include_inline_completions: false`); undo, redo and accepting a word keep the shown completion ahead of new results (`preserveCurrentCompletion`), and requests after undo or redo use the cursor they restore. Accepting a word is its own undo step.
  - **After accepting:** accepting with the commit command asks again even if the text didn't change (Monaco's `noDelay` trigger), while clicking a view or the pill doesn't, as in Monaco. No `min_show_delay` wait in the accept flow. The accepted text's highlight fades out over a second (Monaco `FadeoutDecoration`, skipped with `App::reduce_motion`). The accept flow now survives the edit's own change event.
  - **Gutter pill:** docked, it spans the gutter and shows the edit's line number as the line numbers would (`On`, `Relative`, `Interval`, `Custom`), then its icon; hovering the edit's view shows the check mark and pulses it (Monaco `triggerAnimation`, via GPUI's `with_animation`).
  - **Tests:** GPUI tests cover jumps, hints, alternative actions, the menu on ghost text, the pill's line number and pulse, the fade-out, each undo and redo case (porting Monaco's "when accepting undo"), the long-distance hint and its options, and cross-file edits with and without an open editor; Monaco's `layout.test.ts`, `scrollToReveal.test.ts` and `longDistanceWidgetPlacement.test.ts` are ported.
  - **Breaking:**
    - `InlineCompletion` gains `uri`, `jump_to_position`, `hint`, `show_inline_edit_menu` and `alternative_action` (use `..Default::default()` in literals).
    - `InlineCompletionEndOfLifeReason::Accepted` is now `Accepted { alternative_action }` (match it with `Accepted { .. }`).
    - `InlineEditViewKind` gains `Custom` and `JumpTo`, and `InlineSuggestEditsOptions` gains `show_long_distance_hint` and `long_distance_hint_context_line_count`.
- **API leftovers: events, model and languages (#229):**
  - **Editor events:** `CodeEditorEvent` gains Monaco's `onDidCompositionStart`/`onDidCompositionEnd` (`CompositionStarted`/`CompositionEnded`, from the IME's marked text: committing, cancelling with empty text or unmarking ends it; `in_composition()` is Monaco's `inComposition`), `onDidChangeHiddenAreas` (`HiddenAreasChanged`, when folding or `set_hidden_areas` changed the hidden lines, reported when the next frame applies them), `onDidChangeViewZones` (`ViewZonesChanged`, for zones added, removed or resized, the editor's own included) and `onBeginUpdate`/`onEndUpdate` (`BeginUpdate`/`EndUpdate`, once around edits, typing, pasting, undo, redo, `set_selections` and `set_model`, however nested, as Monaco's `_beginUpdate`/`_endUpdate` counter does; `set_model` with the current model reports just the pair, as in Monaco).
  - **Status bar column:** `CodeEditor::statusbar_column` (Monaco `getStatusbarColumn`): 1-based, tabs to the next tab stop and every other character one column. Monaco's `cursorColumns.test.ts` "toStatusbarColumn" is ported.
  - **Attached editors:** `TextModel::is_attached_to_editor` and `attached_editor_count` (Monaco `isAttachedToEditor`/`getAttachedEditorCount`), with `TextModelEvent::AttachedChanged` (Monaco `onDidChangeAttached`) when the first editor shows the model or the last one stops: editors attach their model when created and in `set_model`, and detach it in `set_model` and when released.
  - **Undoable line ending:** `TextModel::push_line_ending` (Monaco `pushEOL`; `text::Buffer::push_line_ending`) changes the line ending as an undo step, joining the open step like an edit (so typing then changing the line ending undoes together), or its own step. As in Monaco's `_applyUndo`, every undo step now records the line ending before and after it and undo and redo restore it, emitting `LineEndingChanged`. Monaco's `cursor.test.ts` "issue #23539" and "can undo typing and EOL change in one undo stop" are ported.
  - **Line tokens:** `TextModel::line_tokens(row, cx)` (Monaco `tokenization.getLineTokens`): the line's `colorize::Token`s as `tokenize` gives them, with each token's language (the injected one in e.g. a Markdown code fence). Like Monaco after `forceTokenization`, text not parsed yet is tokenized right away.
  - **Indentation helpers:** `TextModel::normalize_indentation` (Monaco `normalizeIndentation`), `line_first_non_whitespace_column`/`line_last_non_whitespace_column` (`getLineFirstNonWhitespaceColumn`/`getLineLastNonWhitespaceColumn`, 0-based, `None` for blank lines), `line_indent_column` (`getLineIndentColumn`) and `line_indent_level` (`computeIndentLevel`, `None` for blank lines). Monaco's `textModel.test.ts` "normalizeIndentation 1/2" and the non-whitespace column tests are ported.
  - **Folding context:** `FoldingRangeProvider::provide_folding_ranges` gets Monaco's `FoldingContext` (empty in Monaco too, `#[non_exhaustive]` so it can grow).
  - **Choosing a formatter:** `providers::set_formatter_selector` (Monaco `FormattingConflicts.setFormatterSelector`) chooses among several matching formatters, for example by asking the user: it gets a `FormatterSelection` (the formatters' `FormatterInfo`, the model, `FormattingMode::Explicit`/`Silent` and `FormattingKind::File`/`Selection`) and answers a task with the index, or `None` to not format. Without one the first formatter runs, as in the standalone editor (`editor.api.ts`). Formatters gain Monaco's `display_name` and `extension_id` (default `None`); Format Document offers the range formatters after the document formatters unless a document formatter has the same extension id (Monaco `getRealAndSyntheticDocumentFormattersOrdered`), and a range formatter used for the whole document now gets `provide_document_range_formatting_edits` with the full range, as in Monaco, rather than `provide_document_ranges_formatting_edits`. Format on paste asks silently; on-type formatting doesn't ask, as in Monaco. VS Code's `editor.action.formatDocument.multiple` picker isn't in the vendored editor, so there's no built-in picker.
  - **Tests:** GPUI tests cover the composition, hidden-area, view-zone and update events, the status bar column, attaching and detaching editors, `push_line_ending` with undo, redo and typing, line tokens (with an injected language), the indentation helpers, the folding context, and formatter selection (synchronous, asynchronous, declined, stacked selectors, extension ids, silent pastes); unit tests cover the status bar column and the buffer's line ending undo.
  - **Breaking:**
    - `FoldingRangeProvider::provide_folding_ranges` takes a `&FoldingContext` after the `ProviderContext`.
    - `CodeEditorEvent` gains `CompositionStarted`, `CompositionEnded`, `HiddenAreasChanged`, `ViewZonesChanged`, `BeginUpdate` and `EndUpdate`, and `TextModelEvent` gains `AttachedChanged` (for exhaustive matches).
    - Undoing or redoing a step restores the line ending it was made with, also after `set_line_ending`, as Monaco does.
- **Inline edits (#216):** Monaco's inline edits (next edit suggestions), which suggest a change away from (or around) the cursor rather than ghost text at it.
  - **Model:** an `InlineCompletion` with `is_inline_edit` is diffed into small replacements (Monaco `InlineEditItem`, `getDiffedStringEdit` and `reshapeInlineEdit`) that follow typing: typing the edit's own text moves it along, edits elsewhere shift it, and a change inside it drops it, as does ignoring it for 20 changes. The last inline edit shows when no completion is visible at the cursor, and completions win when one is (Monaco `inlineEditState`). `show_range` (Monaco `showRange`) limits where an item shows, for ghost text too. Moving the cursor keeps an inline edit, and blurring keeps one asked for explicitly.
  - **Views:** Monaco's view choice (`_determineView`): a changed word shows in a box below it, a changed line in a box below the line, inserted text in place, inserted lines in a zone that pushes the text down, deleted text marked in a bordered block, and several changed lines in a card beside them when it fits (`render_side_by_side`). Views that would cover a cursor move beside the text. An edit shows collapsed, as a marker, with `show_collapsed` or once other typing moved it, until it's jumped to. Clicking a view accepts it. The new `inlineEdit.*` theme colors (`EditorColors::inline_edit`, derived from the diff colors as in Monaco) color them.
  - **Gutter indicator:** a pill beside the edit, colored by what Tab does, or pinned to the top or bottom with an arrow while the edit is scrolled away. Clicking it accepts the edit, or jumps to it when pinned. Hovering it shows Monaco's menu: the provider's `display_name`, Go To / Accept, Reject, Show Collapsed or Show Expanded, the list's commands and the item's `gutter_menu_link_action`.
  - **Commands and keys:** Tab jumps to the edit while the cursor is away from it (`editor.action.inlineSuggest.jump`, `JumpToNextInlineEdit`), then accepts it (`editor.action.inlineSuggest.commit`), following Monaco's `tabShouldJumpToInlineEdit` and `tabShouldAcceptInlineEdit`, including the accept flow (after accepting one, Tab accepts the next on screen). Escape rejects it (`editor.action.inlineSuggest.hide`). `editor.action.inlineSuggest.toggleShowCollapsed` toggles `show_collapsed`. The key context gets `inline_edit_visible`, `cursor_at_inline_edit`, `tab_should_jump_to_inline_edit` and `tab_should_accept_inline_edit`. `handle_end_of_lifetime` reports `Accepted`, `Rejected` (and `handle_rejection`) or `Ignored`, as for ghost text, and `handle_item_did_show` reports an inline edit once it shows.
  - **Options:** `inline_suggest.edits` (Monaco `inlineSuggest.edits`): `enabled` (on; providers see it as `InlineCompletionContext::include_inline_edits`), `show_collapsed` (off), `render_side_by_side` (`Auto`, `Never`) and `allow_code_shifting` (`Always`, `Horizontal`, `Never`).
  - **Playground:** the demo provider offers an inline edit documenting the first undocumented `pub fn`.
  - **Tests:** GPUI tests cover view selection, Tab jumping and accepting, Escape, the gutter indicator and its menu, the pinned indicator, `show_range`, typing through an edit, the options and the commands; Monaco's `inlineEdits.test.ts` is ported, and unit tests cover the diffing, the edit tracking and the view choice.
  - **Breaking:**
    - `InlineCompletion` gains `is_inline_edit`, `show_range` and `gutter_menu_link_action` (use `..Default::default()` in literals).
    - `InlineCompletionContext` gains `include_inline_edits` and `include_inline_completions`.
    - `InlineSuggestOptions` gains `edits`, and `EditorColors` gains `inline_edit`.
- **Language extras (#206, part 2):**
  - **File associations:** `Language` gains `filename_patterns`, `first_line` and `mimetypes` (Monaco's `register`). `LanguageRegistry::language_for_path` follows Monaco's priority: an exact file name (now ignoring case), then the longest glob pattern (on the whole path when it has a `/`), then the longest extension, with the language registered last winning ties. New `language_for_path_or_first_line`, `language_for_mime_type` and `syntax::glob_matches`. A model with a uri and no language guesses its language this way when first shown, as Monaco's `createModel` does. The bundled languages get VS Code's mimetypes, first lines and file names. Monaco's `languagesAssociations.test.ts` is ported.
  - **Runtime:** `set_language_configuration(cx, id, config)` (Monaco `setLanguageConfiguration`) updates every model of the language, which emits the new `TextModelEvent::LanguageConfigurationChanged`. `on_language(cx, id, callback)` runs once, when a model first uses the language (Monaco `onLanguage`).
  - **Language filters:** `LanguageSelector::Filters(Vec<LanguageFilter>)` matches by language, uri scheme and path glob, with Monaco's scoring rules. Provider lookups take a `ProviderTarget` (language and uri; `Option<&str>` still converts), and the editor passes the model's uri.
  - **Signature help context:** `SignatureHelpProvider::provide_signature_help_with_context` gets a `SignatureHelpContext` with `is_retrigger` and `active_signature_help`. Monaco's "Provider should be retriggered if already active" test is ported.
  - **Provider change events:** `refresh_folding_ranges(cx)` and `refresh_code_lenses(cx)` (Monaco's provider `onDidChange`).
  - **Multi-range formatting:** `DocumentRangeFormattingProvider::provide_document_ranges_formatting_edits` formats every selection in one request.
  - **Breaking:**
    - `Language::grammar` is now an `Arc<Grammar>`, so `Language` is `Clone`, and `Language` has new public fields.
    - `LanguageSelector` and `TextModelEvent` have new variants.
- **Editor events, geometry and ScrollType (#205):**
  - **Events:** `CodeEditorEvent` gains Monaco's `onWillChangeModel`/`onDidChangeModel` (`WillChangeModel`/`ModelChanged` with the old and new uri; `set_model` with the current model now does nothing, as in Monaco), `ModelLanguageChanged` (the model's `LanguageChanged`, forwarded), `ConfigurationChanged` (only when an option changed; `has_changed(|o| &o.font_size)` says which), `LayoutChanged` (a `LayoutInfo`, after the frame that changed it) and `ContentSizeChanged`. Also `WillType`/`DidType`, `WillPaste`/`DidPaste` (the pasted range and the copied language, now stored in the clipboard metadata), `WillCopy`/`WillCut`, `KeyDown` (before key bindings run)/`KeyUp`, `MouseWheel`, `MouseDrag`/`MouseDrop`/`MouseDropCanceled` (dragging the selection) and `WidgetFocused`/`WidgetBlurred` (the editor or a widget in it, like the find input; `has_widget_focus`). **Breaking** for exhaustive matches on `CodeEditorEvent`.
  - **Geometry:** `layout_info()` returns a `LayoutInfo` (Monaco `EditorLayoutInfo`: glyph margin, line numbers, decorations and content positions, minimap, scrollbars, overview ruler) read off the last frame. `top_for_line_number`/`bottom_for_line_number` (with or without the view zones next to the line), `top_for_position`, `line_height_for_position`, `offset_for_column`, `width_of_line`, `visible_column_from_position` (0-based), `content_width`, `scroll_width`, `scroll_height`, `scroll_top`/`scroll_left` and `set_scroll_top`/`set_scroll_left`.
  - **ScrollType:** `ScrollType::Smooth`/`Immediate` for `set_scroll_top`, `set_scroll_left`, `set_scroll_position_with_scroll_type`, `reveal_range_with_scroll_type` and `reveal_lines_with_scroll_type`; `has_pending_scroll_animation`. As in Monaco, reveals default to smooth and `set_scroll_position` to immediate (it now stops a running animation), and a reveal moving a line or less jumps. The missing reveal variants are added (`reveal_line_near_top`, `reveal_lines*`, `reveal_position_in_center_if_outside_viewport`, `reveal_position_near_top`, `reveal_range_in_center_if_outside_viewport`, `reveal_range_near_top_if_outside_viewport`). `reveal_line*` no longer scroll horizontally and a range over several lines scrolls to their start, as in Monaco; API reveals now keep `cursor_surrounding_lines` of context too. **Breaking:** `view::reveal::RevealRequest` gains `minimal`.
  - **`reveal_all_cursors(reveal_horizontal, minimal_reveal)`:** one cursor is revealed like a position; several are boxed from the first selection to the last, and nothing scrolls if they don't fit (Monaco `revealAllCursors`).
  - **View state:** `ViewState` keeps the first visible position (Monaco `firstPosition`/`firstPositionDeltaTop`), so restoring scrolls back to the same line when view zones or wrapping moved it, and `folding` (`FoldingViewState`): the collapsed regions and manual ranges, restored when their lines still match Monaco's checksum. **Breaking:** `save_view_state` takes `cx`, and `ViewState` literals need the new fields (or `..Default::default()`).
  - **Tests:** GPUI tests for each event, the layout info, the geometry queries, scroll types, reveals, `reveal_all_cursors` and the folding view state; unit tests for minimal and multi-cursor reveals and Monaco's `stringHash`.
- **Model helpers (#206, part 1):**
  - **Lines and positions:** `TextModel::lines_content`, `value_length` (UTF-16, line breaks as the model's line ending), `modify_position` and `full_model_range`. Monaco's `modifyPosition` test is ported.
  - **Search:** `find_next_match` and `find_previous_match` return a `FindMatch` with regex captures when asked. `find_matches_in` searches within scopes, which are merged first. Monaco's capturing-matches search tests are ported.
  - **Edits:**
    - `apply_edits` returns the undo edits. Unlike Monaco, it also records the edit for undo, which keeps history consistent.
    - The public `push_edit_operations` joins the open undo step until `push_stack_element`, restores the before cursor state on undo, and takes a cursor state computer whose selections redo restores.
    - `pop_stack_element` reopens the last undo step, in the text crate too (`Buffer::pop_stack_element`).
    - `alternative_version_id` / `Buffer::alternative_version` return to the earlier value on undo and redo. Monaco's issue #90973 test is ported.
  - **Brackets and guides:** `bracket_pairs_in_range`, `match_bracket`, `find_enclosing_brackets`, `lines_indent_guides` and `active_indent_guide` (Monaco's `model.bracketPairs` and `model.guides`). Brackets in strings and comments are ignored.
- **Global registries and events (#204, breaking):** Monaco's global `monaco.editor.*` registries and events, as free functions taking the app.
  - **Editors:** `editors` and `diff_editors` list the live editors (weak handles, so released ones drop out), as `getEditors`/`getDiffEditors` do; a diff editor's two code editors are listed too. `on_did_create_editor` and `on_did_create_diff_editor` announce new ones after they're built. Listeners return a gpui `Subscription`.
  - **Models:** `models`, `on_did_create_model` and `on_will_dispose_model` (called with the model's id and state as it's released). `init` starts tracking models; ones created before it join when an editor shows them or they get markers. `model_for_uri` now finds any live model with the uri, as `getModel` does.
  - **Language changes:** `on_did_change_model_language` reports the old language id. **Breaking:** `TextModelEvent::LanguageChanged` now carries `old_language` and `new_language` (match it with `{ .. }`).
  - **Markers across models:** `model_markers(cx, &MarkerFilter { owner, resource, take })` returns `ModelMarker`s (Monaco `IMarker`, with owner and uri) as they were set, in model then owner order. `remove_all_markers(cx, owner)` clears an owner everywhere. `on_did_change_markers` gets the changed uris, merged per update like Monaco's debounced event. `set_markers` now follows Monaco's `changeOne`: markers without a message are dropped, an end before the start moves to the start's line, and clearing an owner that had none emits nothing. Ported from `markerService.test.ts`.
  - **Themes by name:** `define_theme` (refusing names other than letters, digits and `-`) and `set_theme`, which switches every editor and the ones created later; unknown names get `vs`. `theme_named` and `global_theme` read them.
  - **Commands, actions and keybindings:** `add_command` returns a `Disposable`; handlers for one id stack, and disposing one brings back the previous. `add_editor_action` adds an action to every editor, now and later: it runs by id, keybinding and context menu, but isn't in the command palette (it's a command in Monaco). `add_keybinding_rule(s)` bind keystrokes to a command (an editor action, a Monaco command such as `cursorDown`, a registered command with `command_args`, or a GPUI action) through the new `RunCommand` action, or unbind them with `KeybindingRule::unbind`. Disposing removes exactly those bindings, so whatever they shadowed applies again.
  - **`colorize_model_line`:** a model's line highlighted with its own tokens and tabs expanded, as Monaco's `colorizeModelLine`. It doesn't wait for a background parse.
- **Inline completions toolbar, options and provider hooks (#201):**
  - **Toolbar:** Monaco's inline suggestion toolbar. It has previous and next buttons with the "1/3" count, Accept (Tab) and Accept Word (Cmd/Ctrl+Right), and a "⋯" menu with Accept Line, Always Show Toolbar and the list's commands. `inline_suggest.show_toolbar` chooses whether it shows in the hover over ghost text (`OnHover`, the default), above the ghost text (`Always`) or not at all (`Never`). Showing it asks explicitly so the count is known. The new `editor.action.inlineSuggest.toggleAlwaysShowToolbar` toggles `Always`.
  - **Options:** `inline_suggest.syntax_highlighting_enabled` (on) colors ghost text like code, at 70% opacity. Monaco tokenizes from the line's state; here the line with the ghost text, plus the extra lines, is parsed on its own. `inline_suggest.font_family` sets the ghost text's font.
  - **Provider hooks:**
    - `handle_end_of_lifetime` gets an `InlineCompletionEndOfLifeReason` once per item. Accepting gives `Accepted`. Escape or hide gives `Rejected`, but only if the item showed. Anything else, including being replaced by a newer equal item (`superseded_by`), gives `Ignored`.
    - `handle_rejection` is called on rejection.
    - `group_id` and `yields_to_group_ids` make a provider wait for the groups it yields to, and skip it when one of them has a visible completion. Cycles are dropped, as in Monaco.
  - **Commands:** `InlineCompletionList::commands` adds entries to the toolbar menu. **Breaking:** `InlineCompletionList` literals need the new field, or `..Default::default()`.
- **Suggest, find and peek extras (#202):**
  - **Suggestion focus:** `suggest.selection_mode` (Monaco `suggest.selectionMode`: `Always`, `Never`, `WhenTriggerCharacter`, `WhenQuickSuggestion`) can open the list by itself without a focused suggestion. Enter, Tab and commit characters then type as usual; the arrow keys, or Ctrl+Space (Monaco `focusSuggestion`), focus one. `focusSuggestion` and `focusAndAcceptSuggestion` run by id, and `selected_suggestion` is `None` while nothing is focused. Enter on another line now always closes the list, as in Monaco.
  - **Suggest commands:** `toggleExplainMode` (Cmd/Ctrl+/ while suggestions show) shows the focused item's score, prefix, word, distance, index and commit characters in the details, with the new `CompletionProvider::debug_display_name`. "Reset Suggest Widget Size" (`editor.action.resetSuggestSize`) forgets the resized size.
  - **Suggest options:** `suggest.snippets_prevent_quick_suggestions` (off) stops quick suggestions inside a snippet. **Breaking:** `CompletionItemKind` gains Monaco's `Customcolor`, `User`, `Issue` and `Tool`, in Monaco's order.
  - **Find:** `find.find_on_type` (on); when off, the find input searches on Enter. "Find with Arguments" (`editor.actions.findWithArgs`, the `FindWithArgs` action and `find_with_args`) opens find with a search string, replace string and toggles, seeding an empty search string first as Monaco does. "Go to Match..." (`editor.action.goToMatchFindAction`) asks for a match number in the quick input (negative from the end) and selects it as you type.
  - **Payloads:** `CodeEditor::trigger_with_payload` passes Monaco's JSON payload to commands that take one, like Monaco's `trigger(source, id, payload)`. `FindWithArgs` and `TriggerCodeAction` are also JSON-buildable GPUI actions.
  - **Go to and peek:** "Open Definition to the Side" (`editor.action.revealDefinitionAside`, Cmd/Ctrl+K F12, and Cmd/Ctrl+Alt+click) emits the new `CodeEditorEvent::OpenLocationToSide` for the host, since there are no editor groups (standalone Monaco opens it in place). **Breaking** for exhaustive matches on `CodeEditorEvent`. `definition_link_opens_in_peek` (off) shows Cmd/Ctrl+click results in a peek view, and `peek_widget_default_focus` (`Tree` or `Editor`) picks what a new peek view focuses. `togglePeekWidgetFocus` (Cmd/Ctrl+K F2) moves focus between the preview and the list, and F12 in the list goes to the next result.
  - **Resizable peek view:** drag the line between preview and list, or the bottom edge (5 to 35 lines). Later peek views reuse the size, like Monaco's `peekViewLayout`. Navigation inside a peek preview no longer opens a nested peek view.
  - **Code actions:** the `editor.action.codeAction` command (`TriggerCodeAction`, `code_action_with_args`) takes Monaco's `kind`, `apply` (`ifSingle`, `first`, `never`) and `preferred` arguments.
  - **Misc:** "Insert Final New Line" (`editor.action.insertFinalNewLine`) and "Show CodeLens Commands for Current Line" (`codelens.showLensesInCurrentLine`), which lists the line's lens commands in the quick input.
  - **Tests:** GPUI tests cover each selection mode, focusing, explain mode, the new kinds, snippets and quick suggestions, find on type, find with arguments, go to match, revealing to the side, definition links in peek, peek focus, the code action arguments, inserting a final new line and the code lens picker.
- **Missing editor and diff options (#203):**
  - **Line numbers:** `LineNumbers::custom(|n| …)` labels lines with a host function, like Monaco's function-valued `lineNumbers` (an empty label leaves a line unnumbered). **Breaking:** `LineNumbers` is no longer `Copy`. `Interval` now shows the last line instead of the first, as Monaco does, and sticky scroll numbers follow the mode like Monaco's.
  - **Gutter clicks:** `select_on_line_numbers` (Monaco `selectOnLineNumbers`, on) controls whether clicking a line number selects its line. Clicks elsewhere in the gutter (glyph margin, line decorations) no longer select the line, as in Monaco.
  - **Cursor styles:** `CursorStyle::BlockOutline` (a 1px outlined box) and `UnderlineThin` (a 1px underline).
  - **Mouse:** `mouse_style` (Monaco `mouseStyle`: `Text`, `Default`, `Copy`) sets the cursor over the text. `mouse_middle_click_action` (Monaco `mouseMiddleClickAction`): `OpenLink` opens links on a middle click, and `CtrlLeftClick` makes it act like Cmd/Ctrl+click (links, then go to definition).
  - **Minimap:** `minimap.autohide` (`None`, `Mouseover`, `Scroll`) fades the minimap in and out over 0.5s, as in Monaco. A hidden `Scroll` minimap lets clicks through.
  - **Guides and brackets:** **Breaking:** `guides.highlight_active_indentation` is now a `HighlightActiveIndentation` (`Off`, `On`, `Always`); `Always` also highlights the active indent guide on lines with bracket pair guides. **Breaking:** `bracket_pair_colorization` is now a `BracketPairColorizationOptions` with `enabled` and `independent_color_pool_per_bracket_type`, which gives each bracket type its own nesting colors (for bracket pair guides too).
  - **Occurrences:** **Breaking:** `occurrences_highlight` is now an `OccurrencesHighlight` (`Off`, `SingleFile`, `MultiFile`). `MultiFile` highlights in the current editor only, since editors don't share highlights. `selected_text_match_mode` (Monaco `selectedTextMatchMode`: `FindOptions`, `CaseSensitive`, `CaseInsensitive`) sets how Cmd/Ctrl+D, select all occurrences and selection highlights match selected text.
  - **Word wrap:** `word_wrap_override1`/`word_wrap_override2` (with `EditorOptions::effective_word_wrap`), `word_wrap_break_before_characters`/`word_wrap_break_after_characters`, `word_break` (`KeepAll` keeps CJK text together) and `wrap_on_escaped_line_feeds` (breaks after literal `\n`s on lines with a quote). **Breaking:** `view::view_lines::WrapSettings` gains `classifier` and `wrap_on_escaped_line_feeds`, and `view::line_breaks::compute_measured_line_breaks` takes `wrap_on_escaped_line_feeds`.
  - **Model options:** `ModelOptions::large_file_optimizations` (on) makes models created with more than 20M characters or 300K lines skip tokenization, folding, sticky scroll, code lenses, occurrence and selection highlights and wrapping (`TextModel::is_too_large_for_tokenization`). `ModelOptions::default_eol` sets the line ending of text without line breaks. `TextModel::set_value` now detects the line ending from the new text, like Monaco's `setValue`, and emits `LineEndingChanged` when it changes.
  - **Diff options:** `DiffEditorOptions` gains `max_file_size` (MB; larger models show without a diff), `compact_mode` (insertion- and deletion-only diffs show inline; thin hidden-lines separators), `diff_word_wrap` and `diff_algorithm` (`DiffAlgorithm::Legacy` is a port of Monaco's legacy diff computer, `gpui_monaco::diff::compute_legacy_diff`, checked against Monaco's legacy fixtures and `diffComputer.test.ts`).
  - **Diff commands and API:** `DiffEditor::switch_side` and `map_to_other_side` (`diffEditor.switchSide`, the `SwitchSide` action), `reveal_first_diff`, `save_view_state`/`restore_view_state` (`DiffEditorViewState`, with the collapsed unchanged regions) and `DiffEditorEvent::ModelChanged` from `set_models`. The `diffEditor.toggle*` commands are also GPUI actions.
  - **Tests:** Monaco's escaped line feed line-break test and break character cases, the legacy diff computer's tests and fixtures, and GPUI tests for each option and diff command.
- **Hover options and keyboard focus (#199):**
  - **Options:** `EditorOptions::hover` (Monaco `hover`): `enabled` (`On`, `Off`, `OnKeyboardModifier`), `delay`, `sticky`, `hiding_delay` and `above`. Mouse moves follow Monaco's `ContentHoverController`: a sticky hover waits `hiding_delay` for the mouse to reach it, and a non-sticky one hides when the mouse moves onto it.
  - **Keyboard:** Show or Focus Hover focuses a visible hover. The new hover actions (`hideHover` and the scroll, page and top/bottom actions) are bound to Monaco's keys while it has focus, and Escape returns to the editor.
  - **Gutter hovers:** `DecorationOptions::glyph_margin_hover_message` shows on the row's glyph margin or line number, like Monaco's glyph hover.
  - **Verbosity:** `Hover::can_increase_verbosity` / `can_decrease_verbosity` show + and − beside a provider's part, and `HoverProvider::provide_hover_with_context` receives a `HoverContext` with the `HoverVerbosityRequest` (the delta and the previous hover). Repeated requests before an answer add up, as in Monaco. The new `editor.action.increaseHoverVerbosityLevel` and `decreaseHoverVerbosityLevel` act on the clicked part, else the first that supports them. **Breaking:** `Hover` literals need the two new fields (or `..Default::default()`).
  - **Copy button:** each hover part except the color picker has Monaco's copy button, shown while the mouse is over the part.
  - **Long line warnings:** hovering past `stop_rendering_line_after`, or on a line of `max_tokenization_line_length` or more, shows Monaco's warning with "Don't Show Again" (`editor.action.hideLongLineWarningHover`, turning off the new `hover.show_long_line_warning`).
  - **Resizing:** drag the hover's corner to resize it (at least 150px wide). Later hovers may grow to the last size, and the default maximum is Monaco's (the larger of 750px or two-thirds of the editor wide, 250px or a quarter of it high) instead of a fixed 500×250.
  - **Labels:** the marker hover's "View Problem" shows Alt+F8 (⌥F8 on macOS), the key that runs it.
- **Signature help and inlay hints (#200, breaking):**
  - **Parameter hint options:** `EditorOptions::parameter_hints` ports Monaco's `parameterHints`. `enabled` (on) controls opening while typing; Cmd/Ctrl+Shift+Space still works, as in Monaco. `cycle` (on) controls whether going past the last or first signature wraps around or closes the hints.
  - **Parameter hint commands:** `CloseParameterHints` (Escape, Shift+Escape) and `ShowNextParameterHint`/`ShowPrevParameterHint` (Down/Up, Alt+Down/Alt+Up, and Ctrl+N/P on macOS) are bound only while hints show, through the new `parameter_hints_visible` and `parameter_hints_multiple_signatures` key contexts. They replace the ad-hoc Up/Down handling; while several signatures show, Alt+Up/Down goes through them instead of moving lines, as in Monaco. `trigger` runs them by their Monaco ids.
  - **Overload arrows:** with several signatures, the widget shows Monaco's previous/next arrows around the `n/m` counter, and they can be clicked.
  - **Inlay hint label parts:** `InlayHint::label` is now an `InlayHintLabel`, either text or `InlayHintLabelPart`s, each with its own `tooltip`, `location` and `command`. `From<&str>` keeps `label: "…".into()` working. `InlayHint` gains `text_edits` and `Default`. `InlayHintsProvider::resolve_inlay_hint` is new.
  - **Inlay hint interaction:** hovering a part shows the hint's and the part's tooltips after the hover delay, resolving the hint first. Cmd/Ctrl+click opens a part's location or runs its command, and double-clicking a hint applies its text edits, as in Monaco's `inlayHintsController`.
  - **Inlay hint font:** `inlay_hints.font_size` and `font_family` are honored. `InjectedText` gains `font_family` and `font_size` for this.
  - **LSP:** `convert::inlay_hint` takes the queried uri and keeps label parts (with their locations, commands and tooltips) and text edits.
  - **Tests:** Monaco's parameter hints model tests for trigger characters, retrigger characters and multiple providers are ported. New GPUI tests cover overload cycling, the options, the commands and every inlay hint interaction.
- **Indentation detection and commands (#197):**
  - **Detection:** a port of Monaco's `guessIndentation`, with all 40 of Monaco's guessing assertions. New models guess their indentation from their text (`ModelOptions::detect_indentation`, on by default as in standalone Monaco; `TextModel::with_options` sets creation options).
  - **API:** `TextModel::detect_indentation` guesses again.
  - **Commands:** `indentationToSpaces`, `indentationToTabs`, `indentUsingSpaces`, `indentUsingTabs`, `changeTabDisplaySize` (a 1–8 picker) and `detectIndentation`. They come with Monaco's conversion tests.
- **Commands by Monaco id (#194):**
  - **`trigger`:** `CodeEditor::trigger` runs about 100 of Monaco's unlabelled commands by id, as Monaco's `trigger` does: cursor and word movement, scrolling, deletion, `undo`/`redo`, clipboard, find toggles, suggest and peek navigation, paste/drop, inline suggestions and others.
  - **Palette:** 17 labelled actions that were missing are now listed and runnable with `run_action`: `deleteInsideWord`, `actions.findWithSelection`, the next/previous selection match finds, the six `inlineSuggest.*` actions, the color picker actions, the four sticky scroll actions, and `changeAll`.
  - **Diff editor:** `DiffEditor::trigger` adds the `diffEditor.*` ids: `revert`, the unchanged-region and toggle commands, `exitCompareMove`, and the accessible diff viewer.
- **trimAutoWhitespace (#196):** indentation that Enter or Tab inserted is removed again by the next edit if the line is still blank and the edit is near the cursors, as in Monaco. `ModelOptions::trim_auto_whitespace` (default on) turns it off. Monaco's `removeAutoWhitespace` and `issue #15118` tests are ported.
- **Bracket matching like Monaco (#195, breaking):** `match_brackets` is now a `MatchBrackets` enum: `Always` (default), `Near` or `Never`.
  - **Always:** when no bracket is next to the cursor, the enclosing pair is highlighted, as in Monaco's default.
  - **Ruler mark:** a pair next to the cursor gets an overview-ruler mark (`editorOverviewRuler.bracketMatchForeground`).
  - **Focus:** highlights show only while the editor has focus.
- **Word-based suggestions (#198):** the default, `OffWithInlineSuggestions`, now turns word-based suggestions off while an inline completions provider serves the language, as in Monaco. It used to act like `MatchingDocuments`.
- **Options exposed (#198):** `use_tab_stops` (default on) and `trim_whitespace_on_delete` (default off) are now `EditorOptions`. They were built into the cursor code but stuck at their defaults.
- **Stale docs (#198):** `differences.md` no longer lists the diff editor, code actions, rename, formatting, inlay hints, color decorators and links as missing, or says that clicking `⋯` doesn't unfold. `MONACO_PARITY.md` now marks hover, bracket matching, inline completions and indentation detection as partial or missing. The `TabCompletion::OnlySnippets` doc comment explains that standalone Monaco also treats it as off.
- **Keybindings match Monaco (#193):**
  - **In-place replace:** Cmd/Ctrl+Shift+, now runs `inPlaceReplace.up` (+1) and Cmd/Ctrl+Shift+. runs `.down`; they were swapped.
  - **Next/previous problem:** `marker.next`/`prev` are on Alt+F8 / Shift+Alt+F8. F8 / Shift+F8 run the new `marker.nextInFiles`/`prevInFiles`, which act the same within one model.
  - **Suggest list:** Cmd+Up/Down move to the previous/next suggestion, as Monaco's `selectPrev/NextSuggestion` (new actions, plus page variants). They were bound to first/last, which Monaco leaves unbound.
  - **Trigger completion:** also on Cmd/Ctrl+I, and Alt+Esc on macOS.
  - **macOS:** Monaco's Emacs-style secondaries are bound: Ctrl+B/F/P/N, Ctrl+Shift+A/E, Ctrl+Backspace/Delete, and Ctrl+N/P in the suggest list.
  - **Platform-specific keys:** Shift+Delete / Ctrl+Insert (cut and copy) and Ctrl+Shift+Up/Down (select) are bound on Windows only. On Linux, Ctrl+Shift+Up/Down add cursors.
- **Docs:** `docs/MONACO_AUDIT.md` records a full parity audit against Monaco; its findings are tracked as #193–#206.

### Changed
- **gpui from crates.io snapshots (#188, breaking):** gpui, gpui_platform and sum_tree now come from the `gpui-pre` crates (`=0.3.8`, zed@279fe07), the same snapshot gpui-component pins, instead of zed's git. Apps depend on `gpui = { package = "gpui-pre", version = "=0.3.8" }`, and the `[patch.crates-io]` block is gone. No API changes were needed.
- **Web example (#58):** `examples/web` runs the editor in the browser through gpui's web platform (`gpui-pre-web`), with tree-sitter highlighting compiled to wasm. The page passes in a font, since the web platform ships none. Building it needs a nightly toolchain with `llvm-tools` and the `wasm-bindgen` CLI (see its README).
- **wasm linking:** `script/wasm/ar` archives tree-sitter's C objects with LLVM's `ar`. macOS's `ar` silently dropped them, which `cargo check` never noticed.
- **Using gpui-component (#188):** `examples/with-gpui-component` shows gpui-component buttons above an editor in a gpui-component `Root`. A test checks typing, focus and the editor's keybindings there.

### Added
- **Accessibility signals (#181):** `DiffEditorEvent::AccessibilitySignal` (`DiffLineInserted`, `DiffLineDeleted`) fires as the accessible diff viewer moves onto changed lines, like Monaco's `playSignal`. Standalone Monaco ignores these; a host can play VS Code's sounds.
- **Diff editor gutter without lag (#181):** the gutter menu and the moved-block lines follow scrolling in the same frame. They used to lag one frame behind.
- **Hover titles (#181):** the diff editors' buttons show Monaco's titles on hover, in the editor widget colors. They cover "Revert Block", "Revert Change", "Show Unchanged Region", "Click or drag to show more above", "Double click to unfold", "Compare" and "Close".
- **Glyph margin widgets and margin revert arrows (#181):**
  - **Widgets:** `CodeEditor::add_glyph_margin_widget` (`GlyphMarginWidget`, Monaco `addGlyphMarginWidget`) puts a host view in the glyph margin beside a line, following edits.
  - **Arrows:** with `render_gutter_menu` off, the diff editor shows Monaco's older margin revert arrows (`render_margin_revert_icon`, on by default). There's one beside each change, one in a deletion's padding, or one for the selection.
- **Dragging hidden-lines bars (#181):** like Monaco, dragging a hidden-lines bar's top or bottom edge shows or hides lines as the mouse moves. A click still shows 20 more lines.
- **Comparing moved blocks (#181):** each moved block's caption has a "Compare" toggle (Monaco `movedTextToCompare`). While it's on, only that block's own changes show, and the two sides scroll with its two places lined up.
  - **Leaving:** Escape (`ExitCompareMove`, Monaco `diffEditor.exitCompareMove`) or the toggle turns it off.
  - **API:** `set_move_to_compare` and `move_to_compare`.
- **Multi-diff editor (#181):** `MultiDiffEditor` ports Monaco's `MultiDiffEditorWidget`: diffs of many files in one scrolling list.
  - **Layout:** each file is a `DiffEditor` under a sticky, collapsible header, sized to its content with unchanged regions hidden. Only the visible part of each is laid out.
  - **Files:** `MultiDiffDocument` covers created, deleted and renamed files.
  - **API:** `set_documents`, `set_collapsed`/`set_all_collapsed`, `reveal`, `diff_editor(i)` and `MultiDiffEditorEvent::CollapsedChanged`.
  - **Editor additions:** `CodeEditor::content_height` and `DiffEditor::content_height` (Monaco `getContentHeight`).
  - **Theme colors:** `multiDiffEditor.headerBackground` and `multiDiffEditor.border`.
- **Moved blocks (#52, part 7):** with `show_moves`, the diff editor shows moved code like Monaco's `MovedBlocksLinesFeature`.
  - **Display:** each moved block has a border and a "Code moved to/from line X-Y" caption on both sides, and a line with an arrow in a column between the sides joins the two places. The block at the cursor is highlighted (`diffEditor.moveActive.border`).
  - **Block decorations:** the new `DecorationOptions::block` (`BlockDecoration`, Monaco `blockClassName` + `blockPadding`) draws a bordered box around whole lines and the view zones above them.
- **Accessible diff viewer (#52, part 6):** F7 and Shift+F7 in a diff editor open Monaco's accessible diff viewer, a keyboard-driven list of each change with three lines of context.
  - **Keys:** F7 and Shift+F7 move between changes, Up and Down between lines, Enter or Space reveals the line in its editor, and Escape closes the viewer.
  - **Screen readers:** rows carry Monaco's labels ("+ text modified line 12", "Difference 1 of 3: …"), and the modified cursor follows the current line, so `Revert` applies to it.
  - **API:** `accessible_diff_viewer_next` and `accessible_diff_viewer_prev`, and `DiffEditorOptions::only_show_accessible_diff_viewer`.
- **Revert buttons (#52, part 5):** the diff editor's gutter menu (Monaco `renderGutterMenu`, on by default when the modified side is editable) is a 35px column beside the modified editor.
  - **Buttons:** "Revert Block" (`→`, or `↺` inline) for the change at the cursor, and for every change while the gutter is hovered. With text selected, "Revert Selection" reverts only the selected character changes.
  - **API:** `revert`, `revert_range_mappings` and `revert_focused_range_mappings`, and the `Revert` action (Monaco `diffEditor.revert`). Each revert is one undo step.
  - **Theme colors:** `editorGutter.itemBackground`, `editorGutter.itemGlyphForeground` and `menu.separatorBackground`.
- **Hide unchanged regions (#52, part 4):** `DiffEditorOptions::hide_unchanged_regions` (Monaco `hideUnchangedRegions`) collapses long runs of unchanged lines into "N hidden lines" bars, keeping `context_line_count` lines around each change.
  - **Revealing:** a bar's top or bottom edge reveals `reveal_line_count` more lines, and its unfold button or a double click shows them all. Moving the cursor into hidden lines reveals them, and revealed lines stay revealed when the diff updates.
  - **Commands:** `collapse_all_unchanged_regions` and `show_all_unchanged_regions` (actions `CollapseAllUnchangedRegions` and `ShowAllUnchangedRegions`).
  - **Theme colors:** `diffEditor.unchangedRegionBackground`, `unchangedRegionForeground`, `unchangedRegionShadow` and `unchangedCodeBackground`.
  - **Hidden areas:** `CodeEditor::set_hidden_areas` (Monaco `setHiddenAreas`) hides rows besides folds.
- **Inline diff view (#52, part 3):** `DiffEditorOptions::render_side_by_side = false` shows deleted lines inline in the modified editor, like Monaco's inline diff. They appear as syntax-highlighted view zones with the changed characters marked and `−` in the margin.
  - **Narrow editors:** below `render_side_by_side_inline_breakpoint` (900px), diffs show inline unless `use_inline_view_when_space_is_limited` is off.
  - **Mode:** `renders_side_by_side()` reports which mode is showing.
- **Side-by-side diff editor (#52, part 2):** `DiffEditor` ports Monaco's `DiffEditorWidget`.
  - **Display:** the original and modified models side by side, with changed lines and characters highlighted and `+`/`−` gutter signs. Monaco's `computeRangeAlignment` view zones (striped) keep the sides aligned, wrapped lines included.
  - **Behavior:** synchronized scrolling, a resizable split, overview ruler marks, and `go_to_diff` (Alt+F5 / Shift+Alt+F5). The diff is recomputed in the background after edits.
  - **Options:** `DiffEditorOptions`.
  - **Decorations:** gain `margin_background` (Monaco `marginClassName`) and `lines_decoration_text` (an icon in the line decorations area).
  - **Theme colors:** the `diffEditor.*` colors.
- **Diff computation (#52, part 1):** `gpui_monaco::diff::compute_diff` ports Monaco's `DefaultLinesDiffComputer`: line changes with character-level inner changes, moved blocks, `ignore_trim_whitespace`, sub-word extension and the time limit.
  - **Conformance:** all 58 of Monaco's diffing fixtures pass.
  - **Coordinates:** results keep Monaco's 1-based line ranges, with helpers to convert to `PointUtf16`.
- **wasm32 builds (#58, part 1):** `gpui-monaco` and `gpui-monaco-languages` compile for `wasm32-unknown-unknown`, checked in CI.
  - **Grammars:** they compile against tree-sitter's wasm libc headers through `script/wasm/cc`, set up in `.cargo/config.toml`.
  - **Time:** timing uses `web_time::Instant`, which works in browsers and is std's `Instant` elsewhere.
  - **Running in a browser** through `gpui_web` comes in part 2.
- **Accessibility (#53):** the editor is a text input for screen readers, through GPUI's AccessKit support.
  - **Content:** the lines of the selection's page (`accessibility_page_size`, 500 lines) are exposed as text runs, with the selection (Monaco's screen reader content).
  - **Interaction:** assistive technology can move the caret and selection.
  - **Labels:** `aria_label` ("Editor content") and `aria_required`. Read-only editors report it.
  - **High-contrast themes:** `Theme::hc_black()` and `Theme::hc_light()` port Monaco's `hc-black` and `hc-light`. With `EditorColors::selection_foreground` (`editor.selectionForeground`), selected text takes the theme's selection text color, as Monaco's `inline-selected-text` does.
  - **Tab focus mode:** `editor.action.toggleTabFocusMode` (Ctrl+M; Ctrl+Shift+M on macOS) and the `tab_focus_mode` option. Tab and Shift+Tab move focus between elements instead of indenting, and the editor is a tab stop.
- **Huge files and long lines (#59):**
  - **Options:** `EditorOptions::stop_rendering_line_after` (Monaco `stopRenderingLineAfter`, default 10 000) ends very long lines with a "Show more (1.9 MB)" chip. `ModelOptions::max_tokenization_line_length` (Monaco `maxTokenizationLineLength`, default 20 000) leaves longer lines unhighlighted.
  - **Chunked shaping:** long lines are shaped in content-defined chunks, so typing reshapes one chunk.
  - **Background work:** fold ranges and bracket pairs compute one pass at a time instead of piling up a pass per keystroke.
  - **Faster lookups:** view zone rows come from one forward pass. `Rope::offset_to_point` no longer walks characters, and `Rope::offsets_to_points` converts sorted offsets in one pass. Cursor validation and bracket matching no longer copy whole long lines; `CursorModel::line_slice` slices the rope.
  - **Results:** a 2MB minified line no longer hangs (11ms per keystroke), and a 1M-line file types in about 2ms per keystroke.
- **Migration notes (#60):** the book's new "Migrating from Monaco" page maps Monaco's editor, model, event, provider and option APIs to gpui-monaco's.
- **Colorize and tokenize without an editor (#108):** `gpui_monaco::colorize` ports Monaco's `colorize`, `colorizeElement` and `tokenize`.
  - **`tokenize(text, language, registry)`:** each line's tokens, with UTF-16 offsets and Monaco token types such as `keyword.rust`.
  - **`colorize(...)`:** each line's text, with tabs expanded, and its highlights.
  - **`colorize_element(...)`:** an element ready for host UI.
  - **`colorize_highlights(...)`:** the highlights for a `StyledText`.
  - **Markdown:** code blocks now use `colorize_highlights`.
- **Clipboard extras (#107):**
  - **`EditorOptions::selection_clipboard`** (Monaco `selectionClipboard`, Linux): selected text becomes the primary selection, and a middle click pastes it at the click.
  - **Middle click:** without the selection clipboard (or off Linux), a middle click and drag column-select, as in Monaco, unless `scroll_on_middle_click` is on.
  - **`auto_indent_on_paste`** and **`auto_indent_on_paste_within_string`** (Monaco `autoIndentOnPaste`): pasted lines are re-indented by the language's indentation rules, as a separate undo step. `cursor::auto_indent_on_paste` ports `AutoIndentOnPasteCommand` and `getIndentMetadata`, with Monaco's tests.
  - **Copied files:** pasting copied files gives the paste providers a uri-list, so "Insert Path" pastes their paths.
- **Drop and paste edit providers (#92, part 2):** ports `dropOrPasteInto`.
  - **Providers:** `DocumentDropEditProvider` and `DocumentPasteEditProvider` offer edits (`DocumentDropOrPasteEdit`) for dropped or pasted data (`DataTransfer`). Register them with `register_document_drop_edit_provider` and `register_document_paste_edit_provider`. Edits are ordered by `yield_to`, as in Monaco.
  - **Built-in providers:** plain text, paths from a `text/uri-list` ("Insert Path"), and HTML (for Paste As only).
  - **Drops:** files dropped on the editor are inserted by the drop providers (`CodeEditor::drop_files`, `drop_data`). A caret follows the drag.
  - **Pastes:** when providers other than the plain text one handle the clipboard, they decide how it's pasted.
  - **Options button:** when several edits apply, a "Paste options"/"Drop options" button switches between them (`editor.changePasteType`/`changeDropType`, Cmd/Ctrl+.; Escape hides it).
  - **Commands:** `editor.action.pasteAs` ("Paste As...", `CodeEditor::paste_as`) and `editor.action.pasteAsText`.
  - **Options:** `drop_into_editor` and `paste_as` (`enabled`, `show_drop_selector`/`show_paste_selector`).
- **Drag and drop (#92, part 1):**
  - **`EditorOptions::drag_and_drop`** (Monaco `dragAndDrop`, on by default): dragging the selection moves it, and holding Alt (Ctrl elsewhere) copies it. A dotted caret shows the drop position, and the mouse cursor shows a copy. Escape cancels the drag.
  - **Clicks:** a click on the selection without dragging places the cursor, as in Monaco. The edits and the resulting selection port `DragAndDropCommand`.
  - **`double_click_selects_block`** (Monaco `doubleClickSelectsBlock`, on by default): double-clicking just inside brackets or quotes selects their content.
- **Unicode highlighting (#86):** `EditorOptions::unicode_highlight` (Monaco `unicodeHighlight`) borders ambiguous characters (such as Cyrillic `а`) and invisible ones (such as zero-width spaces). With `non_basic_ascii`, it borders every non-ASCII character.
  - **Options:** `include_comments`, `include_strings`, `allowed_characters` and `allowed_locales` (`_os` and `_vscode` mean the system's locale). Defaults follow Monaco in a trusted workspace.
  - **Hover:** says why a character is highlighted. Its "Adjust settings" link offers to allow the character or the locale, or to turn the kind off, by changing the editor's options.
  - **Colors:** `editorUnicodeHighlight.border` and `.background`.
  - **`gpui_monaco::unicode_highlight`:** ports `AmbiguousCharacters`, `InvisibleCharacters` and `UnicodeTextModelHighlighter`, with Monaco's data and tests.
- **Unusual line terminators (#86):** `EditorOptions::unusual_line_terminators` (Monaco `unusualLineTerminators`) handles Line Separator and Paragraph Separator characters.
  - **`Prompt` (the default):** asks whether to remove them; Ignore remembers the choice for the model. **`Auto`** removes them, and **`Off`** leaves them.
  - **`CodeEditor::remove_unusual_line_terminators`** removes them as one undoable edit.
- **Quick pick:** `CodeEditor::show_quick_pick` shows a filterable list in the quick input, with an optional title (Monaco `quickInputService.pick`).
- **Placeholder and read-only message (#89):**
  - **`EditorOptions::placeholder`** (Monaco `placeholder`) shows while the model is empty, in the theme's `editor.placeholder.foreground`. Each line is cut off with an ellipsis.
  - **Read-only edits:** typing, IME input, editing commands and `execute_edits` in a read-only editor emit `CodeEditorEvent::ReadOnlyEditAttempt` (Monaco `onDidAttemptReadOnlyEdit`). They also show `read_only_message` (markdown) at the cursor, or Monaco's default "Cannot edit in read-only editor".
  - **Messages:** `CodeEditor::show_markdown_message` renders markdown messages. `message_text` returns the visible one. Messages now use Monaco's overlay message styling.
  - **Theme:** VS Code themes' `editorGhostText.*` colors are now read.
- **Variable line heights (#87, part 3):** `DecorationOptions::line_height` (Monaco `lineHeight`), a multiple of the editor's line height for the lines a decoration covers. The tallest decoration on a line wins.
  - **Rendering:** text, line numbers and glyphs are centered in a taller line. Selections, the cursor, line highlights, backgrounds and guides fill it.
  - **Hit testing, reveal and widgets** follow the new heights.
  - **`EditorOptions::allow_variable_line_heights`** (default on) turns them off.
- **Wrapping for proportional fonts (#87, part 2):** `EditorOptions::wrapping_strategy` (Monaco `wrappingStrategy`). `Advanced` wraps by the editor font's measured character widths, so proportional fonts wrap at the edge. `Simple`, the default, counts monospace columns.
  - Wrapped lines are indented by space widths, as in Monaco, instead of `m` widths.
- **Font options and zoom (#87, part 1):**
  - **Font options:** `EditorOptions::font_weight` and `font_ligatures` (`FontLigatures::Off`, `On`, or CSS `font-feature-settings` such as `"calt", "ss01" on`). Ligatures are off by default, as in Monaco.
  - **Zoom:** a global zoom level (Monaco `EditorZoom`) scales every editor's font size and line height by 10% per level, clamped to −5…20. It's set with `editor.action.fontZoomIn`, `fontZoomOut` and `fontZoomReset` (`FontZoomIn`, `FontZoomOut`, `FontZoomReset`) and with `set_editor_zoom_level` / `editor_zoom_level`. `options()` keeps the unzoomed values.
  - **`mouse_wheel_zoom`:** Cmd+wheel (Ctrl elsewhere) zooms. A physical wheel steps one level; trackpad gestures zoom smoothly.
- Comment toggling: `editor.action.commentLine` (Cmd+/), `addCommentLine`, `removeCommentLine` and `blockComment` (Shift+Alt+A), using the language at the cursor (injections included). This is a port of Monaco's comment commands and their tests (#79).
- Word commands: `cursorWordStart/End{Left,Right}{,Select}`, `deleteWordStart/End{Left,Right}`, `deleteWordPart{Left,Right}` (Ctrl+Alt+Backspace/Delete on macOS) and `deleteInsideWord`. Also `lineBreakInsert` (Ctrl+O on macOS) (#81).
- Multi-cursor commands: `addSelectionToPreviousFindMatch`, `moveSelectionToPreviousFindMatch`, `insertCursorAtEndOfEachLineSelected` (Shift+Alt+I), `addCursorsToBottom`/`Top` and `focusNext`/`PreviousCursor` (#81).
- Keyboard column selection (`cursorColumnSelect*`, Cmd/Ctrl+Shift+Alt+arrows), `cursorLineStart`/`End` (Ctrl+A/E on macOS) and `cancelSelection` (#81).
- Cursor undo and redo (`cursorUndo`, Cmd/Ctrl+U) and the selection anchor (`setSelectionAnchor` Cmd+K Cmd+B, `selectFromAnchorToCursor` Cmd+K Cmd+K, `goToSelectionAnchor`, `cancelSelectionAnchor`) (#81).
- Scroll commands: `scrollLineUp`/`Down`, `scrollPageUp`/`Down` and `scrollEditorTop`/`Bottom`, plus `CodeEditor::scroll_by`, `scroll_lines` and `scroll_pages` (#81).
- Overtype mode (`toggleOvertypeInsertMode`, Insert), with `overtype_cursor_style` and `overtype_on_paste`, including IME compositions (#81).
- Options: `multi_cursor_modifier` (Alt or Cmd/Ctrl), `multi_cursor_paste`, `multi_cursor_limit`, `multi_cursor_merge_overlapping` and `column_selection` (#81).
- Line and text commands (#80):
  - Sort lines ascending/descending, delete duplicate lines and reverse lines.
  - Trim trailing whitespace (Cmd+K Cmd+X) and `deleteAllRight` (Ctrl+K / Cmd+Delete on macOS).
  - Join lines (Ctrl+J), `transpose`, `transposeLetters` (Ctrl+T) and move caret left/right.
  - Case transforms: upper, lower, title, snake, camel, Pascal and kebab.
  - Duplicate selection.
  - Reindent lines and reindent selected lines.
  - In-place replace (Cmd+Shift+. / ,).
  - Remove brackets (Cmd+Alt+Backspace).
- `EditOperation::from_edits`.
- Occurrence highlighting (#83):
  - The symbol at the cursor is highlighted after a short delay, from a new `DocumentHighlightProvider` (with read and write kinds) or the word's textual occurrences.
  - Other occurrences of the selected text are highlighted too.
  - F7 / Shift+F7 move between the highlights.
  - Options: `occurrences_highlight`, `occurrences_highlight_delay`, `selection_highlight`, `selection_highlight_max_length` and `selection_highlight_multiline`.
  - Theme colors: `editor.wordHighlight*` and `editor.selectionHighlightBackground`.
- Rendering options (#84):
  - `render_whitespace`: none, boundary, selection (default), trailing or all.
  - Indent guides with the active guide highlighted (`guides`), using a port of Monaco's guide computation.
  - `rulers`.
  - `render_control_characters`: shows ␇-style control pictures.
  - `render_final_newline`.
  - Theme colors: `editorWhitespace.foreground`, `editorIndentGuide.*` and `editorRuler.foreground`.
- Bracket pair colorization (`bracket_pair_colorization`, on by default):
  - Brackets are colored by nesting level, skipping strings and comments, and unmatched brackets use the unexpected-bracket color.
  - It honors `colorizedBracketPairs` and the `editorBracketHighlight.*` theme colors (#85).
- Faster whole-document highlighting: injection layers are merged per depth (5.5s → 0.24s on 100k lines of Rust).
- Embedder API (#105):
  - `TextModel`: `line_count`, `line_content`, `validate_position` and `validate_range`, `offset_at` and `position_at`, `value_in_range`, `word_at_position`, `word_until_position` and `find_matches`.
  - `CodeEditor`: `reveal_*` (a port of Monaco's reveal math, with `RevealType`), `visible_ranges`, `execute_edits`, `push_undo_stop`, `trigger` by action name and `has_text_focus`.
- Mouse events with hit-test targets (`MouseDown`, `MouseUp`, `MouseMove`, `MouseLeave`, `ContextMenu`, `CodeEditor::mouse_target`), a port of Monaco's `IMouseTarget` kinds (#105).
- `TextModelEvent::Edited` now carries `changes`: Monaco-style `ContentChange`s with the range, offset, length and new text (#105).
- `CodeEditorEvent::SelectionsChanged` now carries a `SelectionChange` with Monaco's `CursorChangeReason` (explicit, paste, undo, redo, content flush, recover from markers) and source (keyboard, mouse, API, model) (#105). This is a breaking change: match `SelectionsChanged(_)`.
- `CodeEditor::create_decorations_collection` returns a `DecorationsCollection`, a port of Monaco's `IEditorDecorationsCollection` (#105).
- `TextModel::language_at` (Monaco `getLanguageIdAtPosition`), and the `comments` editor option.
- Language configurations accept `lineComment` as `{ "comment", "noIndent" }`.
- **`gpui-monaco-lsp`**, a new crate that bridges language servers to the provider traits (#54):
  - document sync: `didOpen`, incremental `didChange` (also flushed before each request), `didClose`, `didSave`
  - diagnostics shown as markers
  - completion, hover, definition, document highlights and signature help
  - the requests a server sends: `workspace/configuration`, `applyEdit` and `workspaceFolders`
  - `$/cancelRequest` when a request is dropped
  - The playground runs it with `PLAYGROUND_LSP`.
- `gpui_monaco::events::content_changes`, which turns a patch into `ContentChange`s.
- **Formatting (#94):**
  - New `DocumentFormattingProvider`, `DocumentRangeFormattingProvider` and `OnTypeFormattingProvider` traits.
  - Format Document (`Shift+Alt+F`) and Format Selection (`Cmd/Ctrl+K Cmd/Ctrl+F`), plus the `format_on_type` and `format_on_paste` options.
  - Edits are reduced to minimal character changes (Monaco `computeMoreMinimalEdits`) and applied as one undo step. The cursor keeps its place on screen, and stale results are dropped.
  - The LSP bridge forwards `textDocument/formatting`, `rangeFormatting` and `onTypeFormatting`.
- **Navigation and the peek view (#95):**
  - `ReferenceProvider`, `DeclarationProvider`, `TypeDefinitionProvider` and `ImplementationProvider`.
  - Go to and peek commands: Shift+F12 references, Alt+F12 peek definition, Cmd/Ctrl+F12 implementations and Cmd/Ctrl+Shift+F12 peek implementations.
  - Monaco's `gotoLocation` options (peek, goto or goto-and-peek for several results, plus alternative commands), and F4 through results.
  - The peek view: a zone with an editable preview editor and a results list grouped by file, using the `peekView.*` theme colors.
  - Inline messages ("No definition found"), via `show_message`.
  - `register_model`, `model_for_uri` and `set_model_resolver`, so other documents can be previewed.
  - The LSP bridge forwards references, declaration, type definition and implementation requests.
- Clicks in view zones and widgets no longer focus the editor.
- **Smart select (#82):**
  - Expand and shrink selection through `SelectionRangeProvider`s, the syntax tree (`SyntaxSnapshot::selection_ranges`), or brackets, plus Monaco's word ranges and whole-line steps.
  - The `smart_select` options.
  - Ports `smartSelect.ts`, `wordSelections.ts` and `bracketSelections.ts` with their tests.
  - The LSP bridge forwards `textDocument/selectionRange`.
  - On Linux and Windows, Shift+Alt+Left/Right is now smart select, as in Monaco, instead of word-part selection (Monaco leaves word-part selection unbound there).
- **Folding commands (#102):**
  - Commands: fold and unfold recursively, toggle recursively, Fold Level 1–7, fold all block comments and regions, fold and unfold all except the selection, go to parent, previous and next fold, manual folding ranges from the selection (and removing them), and toggle import fold.
  - Monaco's gutter Shift- and Alt-clicks; clicking `⋯` unfolds.
  - Options: `folding_highlight` (`editor.foldBackground`), `unfold_on_click_after_end_of_line` and `folding_imports_by_default`. `folding_maximum_regions` also caps indentation ranges.
  - Ports `foldingModel.ts`'s commands and its tests.
- **Folding sources (#102):**
  - `FoldingRangeProvider` with `FoldingRangeKind`.
  - Syntax folding from tree-sitter `folds.scm` queries, now bundled for every language (`SyntaxSnapshot::fold_ranges`, `Highlighter::fold_ranges`).
  - The `folding_strategy` and `folding_maximum_regions` options, with Monaco's `sanitizeRanges` and nesting-level limit.
  - Debounced recomputation, and `folding_ranges` / `collapsed_folds`.
  - The LSP bridge forwards `textDocument/foldingRange`.
  - Breaking: `FoldRange::is_marker` becomes `kind`.
- **Inlay hints (#56):**
  - `InlayHintsProvider`, shown as injected text, with Monaco's request triggers (viewport, edits, `refresh_inlay_hints`).
  - The `inlay_hints` options, including Ctrl+Alt modes and maximum length.
  - The `editorInlayHint.*` colors.
  - The LSP bridge forwards `textDocument/inlayHint` and refresh.
- **Code actions (#56):**
  - `CodeActionProvider` with hierarchical `CodeActionKind`s and `resolve_code_action`.
  - The lightbulb, and Monaco's grouped menu.
  - Commands: Quick Fix (Cmd/Ctrl+.), Refactor, Source Action, Organize Imports, Fix All and Auto Fix, plus `trigger_code_actions`.
  - A command registry: `register_command` and `execute_command`.
  - `TextModelEvent::MarkersChanged`.
  - The LSP bridge forwards code actions, resolve and `workspace/executeCommand`.
- **Completion behavior (#103), ported from Monaco's `SuggestController` and `SuggestModel`:**
  - `CompletionProvider::resolve_completion_item`: the focused item is resolved, and additional edits that arrive after accepting are applied unless the text changed before them.
  - `CompletionItem` gains `replace_range`, `tags` (deprecated items are struck through), `command` (run after inserting) and `data`.
  - Insert and replace modes (`suggest.insert_mode`); Shift+Enter or Shift+Tab accepts in the other mode.
  - `accept_suggestion_on_enter` (on, smart or off) and `accept_suggestion_on_commit_character`.
  - `suggest_selection` (first, recently used, or recently used by prefix), with Monaco's selection memories.
  - Quick suggestions wait `quick_suggestions_delay`, need a word at the cursor, skip numbers, and can be enabled separately in comments, strings and other code.
  - `suggest_on_trigger_characters`.
  - Word-based suggestions (`word_based_suggestions`) from the current, matching or all registered documents when providers have nothing.
  - `suggest.hidden_kinds`, `suggest.show_deprecated`, `suggest.filter_graceful`, `suggest.match_on_word_start_only` and `snippet_suggestions`.
  - Select the first or last suggestion (Cmd+Up/Down, or Ctrl+Home/End elsewhere).
  - The LSP bridge maps tags, insert-and-replace edits and commands, and forwards `completionItem/resolve`.
  - Tab completion (`tab_completion`): at the end of a word, Tab inserts the best suggestion without showing the widget. Tab and Shift+Tab then cycle through the others, as do they after accepting with Tab or Enter.
  - `suggest.locality_bonus`: among equal matches, words used in the innermost bracket block sort first.
  - The suggest widget (Monaco `suggestWidget`):
    - "Loading..." for slow explicit requests, and "No suggestions." when one finds nothing.
    - The details panel is collapsed by default, as in Monaco. Ctrl+Space (or Cmd/Ctrl+I) toggles it, and the choice is remembered. It goes beside the list when there's room, else below.
    - Resizing by the right and bottom edges, remembered for later sessions.
    - The status bar (`suggest.show_status_bar`).
    - `CompletionItem::label_detail` and `label_description` (LSP `labelDetails`).
    - Color swatches for `Color` items.
    - Options: `suggest.font_size`, `line_height`, `show_icons` and `show_inline_details`.
    - Frozen sessions drop bold highlights.
    - Theme colors: `editorSuggestWidget.*`.
  - The session follows Monaco's rules as you type. It closes on another line or when the indentation changes. It asks again after deleting back past where it opened, or on starting a new word. An explicitly requested session keeps its last matches while nothing matches.
- **Snippet syntax (#55, part 1):**
  - `snippet::parser`: a port of Monaco's `SnippetParser` and its tests, with tab stops, placeholders, choices, variables, and transforms with format strings.
  - `snippet::variables`: Monaco's variable resolvers (selection, file, clipboard, comments, time, random and UUID, plus workspace when the host provides one).
  - `TextModel::change_decoration_options`.
  - Theme colors: `editor.snippet*TabstopHighlight*`.
- **Snippet sessions (#55, part 2):** ported from Monaco's `SnippetSession` and `SnippetController2`.
  - `CodeEditor::insert_snippet` with `SnippetInsertOptions`.
  - Tab, Shift+Tab and Escape move between tab stops or leave, plus `finish_snippet`.
  - Mirrors, transforms, nested snippets, and choices shown in the suggest widget.
  - Per-cursor variables, and indentation following the cursor's line.
  - Completion items with `insert_text_is_snippet` expand as snippets, and every item is inserted the way Monaco does it, re-indenting multi-line text unless `keep_whitespace` is set.
  - `set_snippet_workspace`.
  - The LSP bridge advertises `snippetSupport` and maps `insertTextMode`.
  - `TextModel::start_transaction`, `end_transaction` and `last_transaction_id`.
- **Diagnostic tags and related information (#90):**
  - `Marker::tags` (`MarkerTag::Unnecessary` fades, `MarkerTag::Deprecated` strikes through) and `Marker::related_information`.
  - The options `show_unused`, `show_deprecated` and `render_validation_decorations`.
  - Marker hovers list related information as links, with "View Problem" (`CodeEditor::show_marker`) and "Quick Fix..." actions. The marker zone also lists the related information.
  - Untagged hints cover two columns, as in Monaco.
  - `DecorationOptions` gains `validation`, `unnecessary` and `deprecated`, and `TokenStyle` gains `opacity`.
  - Theme colors: `editorUnnecessaryCode.opacity` and `.border`.
  - The LSP bridge maps diagnostic tags and related information.
  - `Marker` and `MarkerSeverity` now implement `Default`.
- **Minimap section headers (#88, part 4):** labels in the minimap for `#region` markers (from the language's folding markers) and `MARK:` comments, as in Monaco.
  - **Options:** `MinimapOptions::show_region_section_headers`, `show_mark_section_headers`, `mark_section_header_regex` (`label` and `separator` groups), `section_header_font_size` and `section_header_letter_spacing`.
  - **Behavior:** a leading `-` draws a separator line, long labels are shortened in the middle, and mark headers only count inside comments. Headers are found in the background, 250ms after edits.
- **Minimap options (#88, part 3):** `MinimapOptions::side` (left or right), `render_characters` (characters or blocks) and `size`.
  - **Sizes:** `Proportional`, `Fill` or `Fit`, following Monaco's layout computation. Documents longer than the editor's height are sampled.
  - **Rendering:** the minimap is now drawn into an image in the background, as Monaco draws into a canvas. Painting it is one image instead of thousands of quads, and typing got faster.
  - **Fix:** the minimap's colors arrive once the syntax tree has been parsed; before, it could stay grey.
- **Scrollbar and overview ruler options (#88, part 2):**
  - **Scrollbar:** `EditorOptions::scrollbar` (Monaco `scrollbar.*`).
    - Visibility: `auto` (the default, now as in Monaco) shows the sliders while the mouse is over the editor or for 500ms after scrolling, then fades them over 800ms. `visible` and `hidden` do what they say.
    - Sliders take the hover and active colors. `use_shadows` adds a left shadow when scrolled sideways.
    - Arrows (`*_has_arrows`, `arrow_size`) scroll a wheel step and repeat while held. Also `*_slider_size`, `handle_mouse_wheel`, `always_consume_mouse_wheel` and `scroll_by_page`.
  - **Overview ruler:** `overview_ruler_lanes`, `overview_ruler_border` (`editorOverviewRuler.border`) and `hide_cursor_in_overview_ruler` (the cursors are now marked).
- **Cursor and scrolling options (#88, part 1):**
  - **Cursor:** `cursor_blinking` is now Monaco's `CursorBlinking` (blink, smooth, phase, expand, solid, using Monaco's keyframes; read-only editors don't blink), plus `cursor_smooth_caret_animation` (the 80ms caret slide; off/explicit/on) and `cursor_height`.
  - **Scroll position:** `cursor_surrounding_lines` and its style. Cursor moves now reveal like Monaco's `_computeScrollTopToRevealRange`, so the sticky lines' context applies too, and mouse moves get one line of room. Also `smooth_scrolling` (125ms ease-out for reveals, cursor moves and wheel steps) and `padding` (top/bottom).
  - **Scroll range:** `scroll_beyond_last_column` (4 columns instead of 2) and `reveal_horizontal_right_padding`, with Monaco's 30px on the left.
  - **Mouse:** `fast_scroll_sensitivity` (Alt+wheel), `scroll_predominant_axis` and `scroll_on_middle_click` (Monaco's middle-click scrolling, with its anchor dot).
  - **Look:** `rounded_selection` (3px outer corners) and `render_line_highlight_only_when_focus`.
- **Sticky scroll polish (#106):**
  - **Commands:** "Toggle Editor Sticky Scroll" (`ToggleStickyScroll`) and "Focus Editor Sticky Scroll" (`FocusStickyScroll`).
  - **Keyboard focus:** Up/Down move between the sticky lines, Enter goes to the focused one, and Escape returns to the text (Monaco `selectNext/PreviousStickyScrollLine`, `goToFocusedStickyScrollLine`, `selectEditor`).
  - **Folding controls:** shown in the sticky gutter. Clicking one folds the scope.
  - **Mouse:** hovered lines use `editorStickyScrollHover.background`. Shift over a sticky line shows the end of its scope, and Shift+click reveals it.
  - New option `StickyScrollOptions::scroll_with_editor` (default on), and theme colors `editorStickyScrollHover.background` and `focusBorder`.
- **Find controller and widget (#104, part 2):**
  - **Commands:** `actions.find`, Replace, Find Next/Previous (`matchFindAction`), Find Next/Previous Selection (Cmd/Ctrl+F3, with Shift) and Find with Selection (Cmd+E on macOS) follow Monaco's `FindController`:
    - Find Next on a closed widget opens it without focusing it, seeded from the cursor when the query is empty.
    - Replace seeds only from a one-line selection while the find input isn't focused, then focuses replace.
    - Find with Selection takes multi-line selections.
    - Monaco's `find.closeOnResult` option (`close_on_result`).
  - **Remembered toggles:** match case, whole word, regex and preserve case carry over to new editors and models (Monaco's stored query state).
  - **History:** Up/Down in the find and replace inputs walk a shared history (`find.history`, `find.replaceHistory`). Typed strings join it after 500ms.
  - **Extra space on top:** the widget no longer covers the first line (`find.addExtraSpaceOnTop`, a view zone above it).
  - **Resizing:** drag the widget's left edge to resize it; double-click toggles widest.
  - **States:** "No results" shows in `errorForeground`, and an invalid regex marks the input.
  - New: `find_search_scope`, `is_find_query_invalid`, `set_find_replace_visible` and `start_find_with_selection`.
- **Find model (#104, part 1):** find is now a port of Monaco's `FindModelBoundToEditorModel` and `FindDecorations`, checked against `findModel.test.ts`.
  - **Incremental search:** starts where the cursor was, and restarts after explicit moves, undo and redo.
  - **Matches:** the "N of M" position is kept on decorations. Next and previous step past empty matches and respect the scope. Scopes can be several selections, cover whole lines, and are shown with `editor.findRangeHighlightBackground`.
  - **Over 1000 matches:** merged overview ruler marks.
  - **After edits:** the matches update 100ms later.
  - **Replace all:** keeps the selection.
  - **Select all matches:** keeps the primary selection.
  - **Replace patterns:** a port of Monaco's `replacePattern.ts` (`gpui_monaco::replace_pattern`): `$n`/`$nn`, `$&`/`$0`, `$$`, `\n`, `\t`, `\\`, and the case escapes `\u` `\U` `\l` `\L`.
  - **Preserve case:** `FindOptions::preserve_case` and `TogglePreserveCase` (Cmd+Alt+P / Alt+P, and an `AB` toggle).
  - New `EditorOptions::find` (`FindEditorOptions`), plus `change_find_options`, `set_find_search_scope` and `find_go_to_match`. Options: `cursor_move_on_type`, `loop_`, `seed_search_string_from_selection` (never/always/selection, escaped in regex mode) and `auto_find_in_selection` (never/always/multiline); `add_extra_space_on_top` arrives with part 2.
  - `find_matches` now takes `cx`.
- **Context menu (#91):** right-click (over text) and Shift+F10 show Monaco's editor context menu.
  - **Groups:** navigation (Go to Definition, Declaration, Type Definition, Implementations, References; Go to Symbol; a Peek submenu), modification (Rename Symbol, Change All Occurrences, Format Document/Selection, Refactor, Source Action), cut/copy/paste, and Command Palette.
  - **Visibility:** each item shows only when it can run (providers, read-only, a selection), with its keybinding.
  - **Host actions:** `EditorActionDescriptor::context_menu(group, order)` (Monaco `contextMenuGroupId`/`contextMenuOrder`) adds an action to the menu.
  - **Keys:** Up/Down/Home/End, Enter, Right/Left for submenus, and Escape.
  - **Cursor:** right-clicking moves the cursor unless a selection contains the click.
  - New: the `context_menu` option (Monaco `contextmenu`), `ShowContextMenu`, and `menu.selectionBackground`/`menu.selectionForeground`.
- **Bracket pair guides (#118):** `GuideOptions::bracket_pairs`, `bracket_pairs_horizontal` and `highlight_active_bracket_pair` (Monaco `guides.bracketPairs`, `guides.bracketPairsHorizontal` and `guides.highlightActiveBracketPair`).
  - Defaults: off, active and on.
  - **Vertical guides** sit at a pair's least indentation. **Horizontal guides** run to the brackets and turn along the bottom when text precedes the closing bracket. On wrapped lines they show after the opening bracket and up to the closing one.
  - **Colors:** the bracket pair colors, at 30% for pairs other than the one around the cursor.
  - **Indent guides** give way to bracket guides in the same column, as in Monaco's `getGuidesByLine`.
  - Ports `getLinesBracketGuides` and Monaco's `computeMinIndentation`.
- **Color picker (#97, part 2):** Monaco's color picker.
  - **Header:** the picked color in the chosen presentation (click it to cycle rgb/hsl/hex) next to the original color (click it to restore).
  - **Body:** a saturation box plus opacity and hue strips, which you drag.
  - **In the hover:** it opens over a color, or when you click its swatch (`color_decorators_activated_on`). Letting go writes the color in the chosen presentation as one undo step, and the hover stays open.
  - **Standalone:** "Show or Focus Standalone Color Picker" (`ShowOrFocusStandaloneColorPicker`) opens it at the cursor. "Insert" (Enter) writes at the selections; on a color it selects it and "Replace"s it. Escape hides it.
  - **Presentations:** the one written like the text is chosen (Monaco's `guessColorPresentation`).
  - New theme colors: `button.background` and `button.foreground`.
- **Color decorators (#97, part 1):** a swatch before each color, as Monaco's `ColorDetector` draws them.
