  - Colors come from `DocumentColorProvider`s (`register_color_provider`, with color presentations). Without providers, Monaco's built-in finder handles hex, `rgb()`, `rgba()`, `hsl()` and `hsla()`.
  - New options: `color_decorators`, `color_decorators_limit` (500), `color_decorators_activated_on` and `default_color_decorators` (auto/always/never).
  - Colors update a second after edits.
  - New `gpui_monaco::color` module: a port of Monaco's `Color` with RGBA, HSLA and HSVA conversions, CSS parsing and formatting.
  - `InjectedText` gains `swatch`.
  - Typing no longer restarts the sticky scroll model computation on every keystroke while the first one runs.
- **Sticky scroll models (#100, part 4):** `StickyScrollOptions::default_model` (Monaco `stickyScroll.defaultModel`).
  - **Outline** is the default and follows Monaco's outline sticky model: headers are document symbols (from providers, or the tags query), from the name's line to the line before the symbol's end.
  - **FoldingProvider** uses the folding ranges, and **Indentation** uses indentation.
  - An empty outline falls back to the folding ranges.
  - The model updates 300ms after edits.
  - Tags-based document symbols are now computed off the main thread.
- **Command palette (#100, part 3):** F1 opens quick access with `>` and lists the editor's actions with Monaco's labels and their keybindings.
  - It matches word starts, prefixes and substrings (Monaco's `matchesWords` filter), or the exact id.
  - Recently used actions come first ("recently used" / "other commands"); otherwise the list is alphabetical.
  - Actions whose precondition fails are hidden: editing actions in read-only editors, and language features without a provider.
  - New: `CodeEditor::add_action(EditorActionDescriptor)` (Monaco `addAction`, with alias, keybindings and precondition), `remove_action`, `supported_actions`, `run_action`, and the `RunAction { id }` and `QuickCommand` actions.
  - `trigger` also accepts Monaco ids.
- **Quick access (#100, part 2):** Monaco's standalone quick access in a quick input widget at the top of the editor.
  - Go to line and column with Ctrl+G (`:line[:,#]col`, negative lines from the end, visible columns) and go to offset (`::`). Both preview by revealing and highlighting the line.
  - Go to symbol with Cmd/Ctrl+Shift+O (`@`). It fuzzy-matches the name, with extra words matching the container. `@:` groups by kind. The symbol around the cursor starts active, and the active symbol is previewed.
  - Without a known prefix, it lists the prefixes (help).
  - Escape, or losing focus, restores the view; Enter goes there.
  - New: `CodeEditor::open_quick_access`, the actions `GotoLine` and `QuickOutline`, and `editor.rangeHighlightBackground`.
- **Document symbols (#100, part 1):**
  - `DocumentSymbolProvider` (`SymbolKind`, nested `DocumentSymbol`s) and `CodeEditor::document_symbols`.
  - Without a provider, symbols come from the language's tags query, using `Grammar::with_tags_query` and `SyntaxSnapshot::tags`.
  - Bundled queries cover Rust (plus `impl` blocks), JavaScript, TypeScript, Markdown headings, JSON keys and CSS selectors.
  - The LSP bridge forwards `textDocument/documentSymbol`.
- **Code lens (#96):** ported from Monaco's `CodeLensContribution`.
  - A `CodeLensProvider` (with `resolve_code_lens`) adds commands above lines, shown in view zones and grouped per line with ` | `.
  - Lenses are requested after edits with Monaco's debounce (at least 250ms) and resolved when they scroll into view.
  - Clicking runs the command through the command registry, or emits the new `CodeEditorEvent::ExecuteCommand` when nothing handles it.
  - Options: `code_lens`, `code_lens_font_family` and `code_lens_font_size`. Theme color: `editorCodeLens.foreground`.
- **Linked editing (#101):** ported from Monaco's `LinkedEditingContribution`.
  - With `linked_editing` on, typing in a linked range, such as an HTML start tag's name, is mirrored to the others in the same undo step.
  - A `LinkedEditingRangeProvider` supplies the ranges; without one, HTML tag pairs come from the syntax tree.
  - The ranges follow the cursor (with Monaco's debounce). Text outside the word pattern, Escape or several cursors end it.
  - `StartLinkedEditing` (Cmd/Ctrl+Shift+F2) works with the option off.
  - Theme color: `editor.linkedEditingBackground`.
- **Inline completions, part 2 (#99):** ported from Monaco's `InlineCompletionsModel`, `InlineCompletionsSource` and controller.
  - Typing, pasting, Tab and deleting ask providers for completions after a 50ms debounce. Completions stay valid while what's typed matches them (`inlineCompletionIsVisible`).
  - The selected one shows as italic ghost text after the cursor, with extra lines in a view zone.
  - Tab accepts it, except when the ghost text starts with a full indent inside the indentation. Cmd/Ctrl+Right accepts the next word, and `AcceptNextInlineLine` the next line.
  - Alt+] and Alt+[ cycle (asking explicitly first; `inline_suggestion_index`). Alt+\\ requests explicitly. Escape, explicit cursor moves and blur (unless `keep_on_blur`) hide it.
  - It hides in snippet mode and during IME composition. Accepting runs the item's command.
  - `suppress_suggestions` keeps the suggest widget closed.
  - `suggest.preview` shows the selected suggestion as ghost text.
  - `handle_item_did_show` and `handle_partial_accept` are called.
- **Inline completions, part 1 (#99):**
  - A port of Monaco's `computeGhostText` (with its char diff and tests).
  - The `InlineCompletionsProvider` API and the `inline_suggest` options.
  - `suggest.preview` and `preview_mode`.
  - Theme colors: `editorGhostText.*`.
- **Semantic tokens (#98):**
  - `DocumentSemanticTokensProvider` (full results or edits against the last result) and `DocumentRangeSemanticTokensProvider` (for the viewport until a full result arrives).
  - Ported from Monaco's `documentSemanticTokens` and `viewportSemanticTokens`, with its adaptive debounce. Edits made during a request are replayed onto the result.
  - The tokens are stored in a port of Monaco's sparse multiline tokens, with its tests, and move with edits.
  - Styles come from the theme's `semanticTokenColors`, scored as in VS Code, falling back to VS Code's default TextMate scopes and then to Monaco's standalone `type.modifier` rules. Only the properties a style sets override the syntax highlighting.
  - The `semantic_highlighting` option (`On`, `Off` or `ConfiguredByTheme`). `Theme::semantic_highlighting` is read from VS Code themes; Monaco's built-in themes leave it off.
  - `refresh_semantic_tokens`.
  - Editors repaint when providers are registered or removed, so a language server that starts later is noticed.
  - The LSP bridge forwards `textDocument/semanticTokens/full`, `full/delta` and `range`, and handles `workspace/semanticTokens/refresh`.
- **Links (#93):**
  - Monaco's link computer (ported with its tests) underlines URLs.
  - `LinkProvider` (with `resolve_link`) adds or overrides links; later providers win where links overlap.
  - Holding Cmd/Ctrl over a link highlights it (`editorLink.activeForeground`), and the hover says "Follow link (cmd + click)".
  - Cmd/Ctrl+click and `editor.action.openLink` emit `CodeEditorEvent::OpenLink` for the host to open, with `file://./` links made relative to the model.
  - The `links` option.
- **Rename (#56):** `RenameProvider` (with optional `resolve_rename_location`), F2 with Monaco's rename box, `WorkspaceEdit` and `CodeEditor::apply_workspace_edit`. The LSP bridge forwards `prepareRename` and `rename`.

### Changed
- `EditorOptions::quick_suggestions` is now `QuickSuggestions { other, comments, strings }` instead of a `bool` (breaking).

### Fixed

These are the small correctness follow-ups from #76:

- **Content widget placement:** when no preference fits, a content widget falls back to its first preference, as in Monaco, instead of always going below.
- **Line endings:** `TextModel::set_line_ending` takes `cx` and emits the new `TextModelEvent::LineEndingChanged` (breaking).
- **Scrolling:** `set_scroll_position` and wheel scrolling clamp at the maximum scroll right away.
- **Folding:** collapsed folds that edits shrink to one line are dropped.
- **Markdown:** inline code uses the code font, and web and mail links open on click.
- **Undo history:** `text::Buffer::clear_history` also drops the edits of an open transaction.
- **Parameter hints:** they re-query on cursor moves and close on clicks, as in Monaco's `ParameterHintsModel`.
- **Language configuration regexes:**
  - They translate more JavaScript syntax: `[^]`, `[]`, a literal `[` in a class, and Annex B hyphens such as `[\w-.]`.
  - They ignore `g`, `y`, `d`, `u` and `v` flags.
  - `Pattern::error` and `LanguageConfiguration::patterns` report regexes that don't compile.
  - The bundled HTML `onEnterRules` and CSS `wordPattern` never compiled before and now work.

Also fixed:

- **Undo:** `text::Buffer::set_last_transaction_metadata` sets the cursor state of the open transaction, if there is one, instead of the previous undo step.
- **View zones:** many zones (for example thousands of code lenses) no longer slow down every frame. Zone positions are found by binary search, only zones on screen are rendered, and zone anchors are re-based as they're resolved.

## [0.1.0] - 2026-10-09

This is the first release: a Monaco-like code editor component for GPUI. It is distributed through git and pinned to gpui at zed rev `f16f9652ec57bf806e65b2a0d51bb92a63644914`. See the [guide](book/src/getting-started.md) for setup.

### Text (`gpui-monaco-text`)
- **Rope:** built on `sum_tree`, with byte, char, UTF-16 and line summaries, and `Point` / `PointUtf16` / `OffsetUtf16` coordinates.
- **Edits:** batch edits, with `Patch` composition, inversion and offset transforms.
- **Anchors:** resolved lazily with left or right bias.
- **Undo:** operation-based undo and redo with edit grouping and transactions.
- **Line endings:** LF and CRLF are detected and normalized, and a BOM is preserved.
- **Conformance:** Monaco's piece-tree and text-model buffer tests are ported.

### Syntax (`gpui-monaco-syntax`, `gpui-monaco-languages`)
- **`LanguageRegistry`:** finds languages by id, alias, extension and file name.
- **Editing rules:** VS Code `language-configuration.json` parsing, with `fancy-regex` patterns.
- **Highlighting:** tree-sitter, with incremental reparsing on a background thread and injections, including combined injections and `include-children`. Unchanged injection layers and their query results are reused across reparses.
- **Custom highlighters:** a pluggable `Highlighter` trait, set per language with `Language::with_highlighter`.
- **Bundled languages:** each is behind a cargo feature: Rust, JavaScript/JSX, TypeScript/TSX, JSON, YAML, TOML, Markdown (with fenced-code injections), HTML (with `<script>` and `<style>`) and CSS.

### Editor (`gpui-monaco`)
- **Model and view:**
  - A `TextModel` entity holds the text, language, undo history, decorations and markers.
  - Any number of `CodeEditor` views can share a model. Edits move the other views' cursors, and undo restores the cursors of the view that made the edit.
  - View state can be saved and restored.
- **Cursor core:** ported from Monaco's `cursor` with its unit tests:
  - character, word, word-part, line and page movement
  - column selection and multi-cursor
  - typing, deleting, tab and outdent
  - line operations
  - clipboard with multi-cursor distribution
  - IME composition
- **Typing helpers:** Monaco's rules for auto-closing pairs, overtype, auto-surround, auto-indent (`onEnterRules`, `indentationRules`, electric characters) and bracket matching.
- **Find and replace:** a find widget with case, whole-word, regex (Rust `regex`) and in-selection search, plus replace and replace all with `$n` substitution.
- **Multi-cursor commands:** add the next match, move to the next match, select all occurrences, and add cursors above or below.
- **View:**
  - gutter with line numbers, glyph margin and fold controls
  - current-line highlight
  - scrollbars with an overview ruler
  - word wrap following Monaco's line-break rules
  - indentation and marker folding
  - minimap
  - sticky scroll
- **Decorations:** `delta_decorations` supports backgrounds, borders, underlines, text color, whole-line decorations, glyphs, line-number color, gutter bars, overview ruler marks, injected before/after text, hover messages, stickiness and per-editor ownership.
- **Widgets:** view zones, content widgets and overlay widgets, any of which can be a GPUI view.
- **Providers:**
  - **Completion:** Monaco's `fuzzyScore` and completion model, and a suggest widget with docs.
  - **Hover:** markdown, with highlighted code blocks.
  - **Go to definition:** Cmd/Ctrl+hover link, Cmd/Ctrl+click and F12.
  - **Signature help.**
- **Diagnostics:** `set_markers` with squiggles, overview ruler marks, hover messages, and F8 navigation in an inline zone.
- **Themes:**
  - Monaco `vs` and `vs-dark`.
  - VS Code color theme JSON, with `include`; the type is inferred when the file omits it.
  - `Theme::define`.
- **Keymap:** Monaco's default keybindings per platform, as GPUI actions in the `CodeEditor` key context.

### Tooling
- **Playground:** an example app with:
  - file open and language and theme switching
  - option toggles and a status bar
  - demo providers and widgets
  - screenshot and perf modes
- **Performance:**
  - Criterion benchmarks for the text and editor components.
  - A large-file budget test: open in under 200ms, keystroke-to-paint p95 under 8ms and reparse in under 50ms on 100k lines.
  - [`docs/PERFORMANCE.md`](docs/PERFORMANCE.md) has the numbers.
- **Docs:** an mdBook guide in `book/`, and rustdoc on every public item.

### Known limitations
The [post-MVP issues](https://github.com/ty-ler/gpui-monaco/issues?q=label%3Apost-mvp) track:

- the diff editor
- screen reader support
- an LSP adapter
- snippets
- code actions, rename and inlay hints
- crates.io publishing
- wasm
- very long lines

[Differences from Monaco](book/src/differences.md) lists the smaller gaps, such as comment toggling, indent guides, bracket pair colorization and reindent on paste.

[0.1.0]: https://github.com/ty-ler/gpui-monaco/releases/tag/v0.1.0
